Our differentiable mesh rendering method scales to unbounded, real-world 3D scenes, where it yields high-quality novel view synthesis results approaching those of state-of-the-art, non-mesh methods. Our method suggests that it may be possible to solve the differentiable rendering problem without relying on specialized renderers, only using conventional tools from the computer graphics toolbox.

</details>


### [36] [SIGNET: Motion-Level Knowledge Transfer for Cross-Language Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28626)
*Sobhan Asasi,Ozge Mercanoglu Sincan,Richard Bowden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIGNET是一个跨语言手语翻译框架，通过注意力机制和手部先验聚合，从多个预训练手语骨干网络中动态选择最相关的专家，实现运动级知识迁移，在四个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语翻译方法通常针对单一数据集或语言设计，难以扩展，且忽视了不同手语之间的联系，无法有效进行跨语言迁移。

Method: 提出SIGNET框架，集成多个预训练手语骨干网络，通过基于注意力的手部先验聚合机制引导门控融合网络动态选择最相关的专家，实现运动级知识跨语言迁移。

Result: 在How2Sign、Phoenix14T、CSL-Daily和MeineDGS四个基准上取得SOTA翻译性能，并在WLASL手语识别任务上超越先前方法。

Conclusion: 尽管不同手语在语法和词汇上存在差异，预训练模型捕获的运动级视觉模式可以跨数据集和语言复用，验证了运动级知识迁移的有效性。

Abstract: Sign language translation (SLT) remains challenging due to its high spatio-temporal complexity, long sequences, and the need to model multiple articulators without relying on gloss annotations. Existing approaches are typically tailored to individual datasets or languages and struggle to scale, while overlooking the relationships between sign languages that could inform more effective cross-lingual transfer. We present \textbf{SIGNET}, a framework that enables motion-level knowledge transfer for cross-language sign language translation. Our key insight is that, although sign languages differ in grammar and lexicon, pretrained models capture motion-level visual patterns that can be reused across datasets and languages. \textbf{SIGNET} integrates multiple pretrained sign language backbones through an attention-based, hand-prior aggregation mechanism that guides a gated fusion network in dynamically selecting the most relevant experts. Comprehensive experiments on four benchmarks (How2Sign, Phoenix14T, CSL-Daily, and MeineDGS) demonstrate state-of-the-art translation performance, and \textbf{SIGNET} also surpasses prior methods on WLASL for sign language recognition.

</details>


### [37] [Physics-Grounded Disentangled Flow Modeling for Brain Disease Progression Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28630)
*Jun Wang,Peirong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PDF框架，通过物理约束的解耦流匹配网络，将脑部病变纵向预测分解为形态演变和强度演变两个过程，在三个公开数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接学习基线图像到未来观测的映射，未显式建模病变进展的物理机制，导致物理合理性、泛化性和可解释性不足。

Method: 提出PDF框架，包含两个流匹配网络分别学习形态演变（病变生长和结构变形）和强度演变（信号变化）；引入基于病变生长动力学的PDE正则化损失（扩散-反应-平流公式）施加物理约束。

Result: 在三个涵盖不同脑部疾病的公开纵向数据集上取得SOTA性能，验证了解耦建模和物理约束学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 物理引导的解耦流匹配框架能有效提升脑病变纵向预测的物理合理性与预测精度。

Abstract: Forecasting longitudinal brain lesion evolution is critical for disease monitoring and treatment planning. Existing approaches typically learn a direct mapping from a baseline image to a future observation, without explicitly modeling the physical mechanisms underlying the lesion progression. Such an entangled modeling of structural deformation and image intensity variation limits physical plausibility, model generalization, and interpretability. To address this, we propose PDF, a Physics-grounded Disentangled Flow matching framework for longitudinal brain disease forecasting. We explicitly decompose the longitudinal modeling of lesion growth into two processes, each learned by a dedicated flow matching network: morphology evolution, which captures lesion growth and structural deformation; and intensity evolution, which models signal changes driven by variations in lesion concentration. To enforce physics-grounded constraints, we introduce a PDE-regularized loss based on lesion growth dynamics, that enforces a diffusion-reaction-advection formulation for morphological evolution. Experiments on three public longitudinal datasets spanning diverse brain diseases demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, validating the effectiveness of the disentangled modeling framework and physics-grounded learning design. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/jhuldr/PDF.

</details>


### [38] [AEGIR: Modeling Area Emitters for Indoor Inverse Rendering using Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28635)
*Mohamed Shawky Sabae,Philipp Langsteiner,Jan-Niklas Dihlmann,Hendrik Lensch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AEGIR提出了一种在可重光照的高斯溅射表示中显式建模局部面光源的框架，通过可微延迟渲染管线结合多重重要性采样和针对性正则化，解决了光照与材质分解的歧义问题，在复杂局部光照场景下实现了更准确的光照重建和下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可重光照方法将场景光照近似为离散点光源、全局环境贴图或隐式表示，忽略了真实世界发光体的物理空间范围，导致错误的光衰减和不真实的阴影。作者旨在通过显式建模局部面光源来解决这一局限。

Method: 提出AEGIR框架，在可重光照高斯溅射中显式引入局部面光源表示；设计可微延迟渲染管线，集成多重重要性采样（MIS）和针对性正则化，联合优化发光体、材质和几何参数。

Result: 实验表明，显式面光源表示显著改善光照重建质量，并在新视角合成、可控重光照和虚拟物体插入等下游任务中取得更优性能，尤其在复杂局部光照场景中效果突出。

Conclusion: 显式建模面光源能够更准确地模拟局部光传输，实现更一致的光照-材质分解，是提升逆渲染和可重光照质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Inverse rendering requires separating illumination from surface materials, which is highly ambiguous due to their tight coupling in observed images. While Gaussian Splatting is efficient for novel view synthesis, existing relightable methods approximate scene lighting using discrete point lights, global environment maps, or implicit representations. By ignoring the physical spatial extent of real-world emitters, these approaches produce incorrect light attenuation and unrealistic shadows. We present AEGIR (Area Emitters for Gaussian Inverse Rendering), a framework that explicitly models local area emitters within a relightable Gaussian Splatting representation. Joint optimization of emitters, materials, and geometry is challenging due to flexible emitter parameterization, which increases both the number of parameters and the ambiguity between illumination and materials. We address this by introducing a differentiable deferred rendering pipeline that integrates multiple importance sampling with targeted regularization. As a result, AEGIR accurately simulates local light transport and achieves more consistent decomposition. Experiments show that explicit area emitters improve illumination reconstruction and enhance downstream tasks, including novel view synthesis, controlled relighting, and virtual object insertion, particularly in scenes with complex local lighting.

</details>


### [39] [Obliviate: Erasing Concepts from Autoregressive Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28643)
*Hossein Shakibania,Jonas Henry Grebe,Tobias Braun,Ege Aktemur,Saleh Aslani,Mehmet Görkem Yiğit,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Obliviate，一种基于引导的概念擦除方法，用于自回归图像生成模型，在移除显式内容、暴力和品牌图像方面显著优于现有方法，同时保持了模型整体效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管概念擦除在多模态生成模型中已有研究，但针对自回归图像生成的概念擦除方法尚未被充分探索，而这类模型在统一多模态架构趋势中日益重要。

Method: Obliviate基于三个关键设计：视觉token分布上的KL散度监督、完整自回归rollout的轨迹级更新、以及用于稳定目标构建的对齐视觉前缀。

Result: 在Liquid、Emu3-Gen和Janus-Pro三种先进自回归文生图模型上评估，Obliviate在擦除显式内容、暴力和品牌图像方面持续优于现有替代方法，在RAB防御基准上将色情内容从91.58降至3.15。

Conclusion: Obliviate有效填补了自回归图像生成领域概念擦除的研究空白，在显著降低有害内容生成的同时很好地保留了模型原有能力。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of generative AI models has intensified concerns about misuse, including the creation of unsafe or disturbing imagery. To mitigate such issues, several concept erasure approaches have been proposed to remove harmful content from multimodal generative models. Yet concept erasure for autoregressive image generation remains largely unexplored, despite the growing relevance of these models in recent trends toward unified multimodal architectures. In this work, we fill this gap by introducing Obliviate, a guidance-based concept erasure method for autoregressive image generation. Our method builds on three key design choices: KL-based supervision over visual token distributions, trajectory-level updates over full autoregressive rollouts, and aligned visual prefixes for stable target construction. We evaluate Obliviate on three state-of-the-art autoregressive text-to-image models, Liquid, Emu3-Gen, and Janus-Pro, covering the erasure of explicit content, graphic violence, and branded imagery. Obliviate consistently outperforms current alternatives, reducing nudity on the defensive RAB benchmark from 91.58 to 3.15 while preserving overall model utility.

</details>


### [40] [FedLAS: Feature-Modulated Bidirectional Label Smoothing for Neural Network Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28654)
*Thiru Thillai Nadarasar Bahavan,Sachith Seneviratne,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FedLAS是一种基于特征范数的双向标签平滑算法，通过置信度指示器(NCI)和双向校准门控(BCG)模块，根据每个样本的置信状态动态调整平滑程度，同时处理过自信和欠自信问题，在保持准确率的同时显著降低校准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标签平滑方法(包括Margin-based LS)使用预定义的均匀平滑规则，仅能处理过自信问题，无法适应样本的多样性特征（如难度/模糊性）以及模型训练过程中的动态变化。需要一种能识别每个样本具体置信状态并调整平滑程度的机制。

Method: 提出FedLAS算法，包含两个核心模块：1) 基于特征范数的置信度指示器(NCI)，用于控制平滑程度；2) 双向校准门控(BCG)模块，用于检测过自信和欠自信。该算法可作为即插即用模块集成到LS和MbLS损失函数中。

Result: 在标准数据集和细粒度高分辨率视觉基准上，FedLAS在保持Top-1准确率的同时，持续降低期望校准误差(ECE)和自适应ECE，优于现代基线方法。

Conclusion: FedLAS通过样本级别的动态双向校准机制，有效解决了传统标签平滑方法的局限性，实现了更好的模型校准性能。

Abstract: Deep Neural Network (DNN) classifiers suffer from poor calibration when their softmax outputs (predictive confidence) deviate from the empirical likelihoods. This manifests itself as either overconfident incorrect predictions or under-confident correct predictions. Label smoothing (LS) enhances model calibration by introducing entropy regularization during training through redistributing probability mass from the ground-truth label to the remaining classes. LS, including Margin-based LS (MbLS), have restrictive assumptions: they rely on predefined, uniform smoothing rules and only tackle overconfidence. In reality, samples exhibit diverse characteristics, such as difficulty/ambiguity, that interact with the evolving nature of the model being trained. In training, samples may have various degrees of under- or overconfidence. To overcome this, a mechanism that identifies the specific confidence state of each sample and determines the appropriate degree of smoothing in each training step is needed, tailoring the adjustment to the individual sample. We propose FedLAS: Feature-Modulated Bidirectional Label Smoothing, a plug-and-play algorithm for label smoothing-based losses. In FedLAS, we introduce a Feature Norm-based Confidence Indicator (NCI) to control smoothing and a Bidirectional Calibration Gating (BCG) module to detect both over and under-confidence. Our algorithm can be integrated with LS and MbLS based losses when applied to standard DNNs, enhancing performance. Extensive experiments on standard and fine-grained high-resolution vision benchmarks show that FedLAS consistently improves calibration compared to modern baselines, reducing Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and Adaptive ECE while maintaining Top-1 accuracy. Code: github.com/nadarasarbahavan/FEDLAS

</details>


### [41] [SemDynReg: Semantics-Guided Deformation Regularization for Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28656)
*Ruitao Chen,Mozhang Guo,Jinge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种语义引导的框架，通过SAM提取分割掩码和语义特征，构建对象ID图，对每个对象的top-k贡献高斯体施加变形参数（位置、尺度、旋转）一致性正则化，从而实现动态3DGS中对象级的一致变形，提升渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于变形场的动态3DGS方法缺乏显式的对象级建模，导致单个对象内高斯变形不一致以及不同对象间不期望的耦合。

Method: 1) 使用SAM提取分割掩码及语义特征；2) 通过特征相关性匹配与预定义对象字典构建对象ID图；3) 根据对象ID图识别每个对象的像素级top-k贡献高斯体；4) 对这些高斯体的位置、尺度和旋转变形参数施加一致性正则化。

Result: 在动态3DGS渲染中取得了更高的PSNR和SSIM，更低的LPIPS，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 语义感知的正则化改善了对象级变形一致性，有效提升了动态场景渲染质量。

Abstract: Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an efficient approach for rendering dynamic scenes in a wide range of 3D applications. However, existing deformation field-based approaches largely lack explicit object-level modeling, often resulting in inconsistent Gaussian deformations within individual objects and unwanted coupling between different objects. To address this limitation, we introduce a semantics-guided framework that enforces dynamic regularization at the object level, aiming to achieve spatially consistent object-wise deformation. Specifically, we first extract segmentation masks using the Segment Anything Model (SAM) and derive semantic features from input images. An object-ID map is then constructed via feature relevance matching with a predefined object dictionary. Guided by this object-ID map, we identify the pixel-wise top-k contributing Gaussians for each object and impose consistency regularization on their deformation parameters, including position, scale, and rotation. Unlike prior methods that learn deformation fields without explicit object-level constraints, our approach incorporates semantic cues to guide deformation behavior at the object level. Experimental results demonstrate that our semantics-aware regularization improves object-level deformation consistency and outperforms baseline methods in rendering quality, achieving higher PSNR and SSIM and lower LPIPS in dynamic 3DGS rendering. Our project page is available at https://dyn-reg-3dgs.github.io/.

</details>


### [42] [BackTranslation2.0 -- A Linguistically Motivated Metric to Assess Sign Language Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28673)
*Oliver Cory,Maksym Ivashechkin,Karahan Sahin,Oline Ranum,Jianhe Low,Edward Fish,Anton Pelykh,Ozge Mercanoglu Sincan,Richard Bowden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BackTranslation2.0，一种基于语言学基础的手语翻译评估指标，通过代理框架整合多个专业工具评估语法正确性、音韵准确性、运动流畅性和生成保真度四个维度，并利用LLM交叉引用模块进行结构化推理，在BSL数据集上展现出与人类判断的高度相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语生成评估指标过于简单且与人类判断对齐不佳，缺乏语言学基础的评估框架。

Method: 采用代理框架，通过确定性管线编排多个专业工具评估四个维度；利用LLM交叉引用模块进行跨工具一致性检查和语言学期望验证；最终通过确定性加权公式计算维度分数。

Result: 在BSL数据集上与人类判断在所有维度上展现强相关性，优于六种基线指标。

Conclusion: BackTranslation2.0提供了一个更全面、可解释且语言学原则化的手语生产系统评估框架。

Abstract: Sign Languages (SLs) are the primary means of communication for millions of deaf individuals, yet existing evaluation metrics for generated SL remain simplistic and poorly aligned with human judgements. We introduce BackTranslation2.0, a linguistically grounded evaluation metric for text-to-sign translation that moves beyond naïve backtranslation. Our approach adopts an agentic framework in which a deterministic pipeline orchestrates a suite of specialised tools to assess four scoring dimensions - grammatical correctness, phonological accuracy, motion fluency, and generation fidelity - aligned with human rater assessments. Tool outputs are not treated independently: a set of large language model (LLM)-based cross-referential comparison modules evaluates consistency across tools and checks outputs against linguistic expectations, enabling structured reasoning over grammatical, phonological, and motion-level evidence. Final dimension scores are computed through deterministic weighted formulas over validated tool outputs. To validate BackTranslation2.0, we introduce and evaluate on a British Sign Language (BSL) dataset rated in a human rater study across the same quality dimensions, following a protocol developed in collaboration between linguists and deaf experts, benchmarking against six baseline metrics. Our method demonstrates strong correlation with human judgements across all dimensions, providing a more comprehensive, interpretable, and linguistically principled evaluation framework for sign language production systems.

</details>


### [43] [Predicting Metastatic Risk from Primary Tissue Architecture via Distance-Aware Spatial Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28676)
*Sandesh Pokhrel,Hamid Manoochehri,Bodong Zhang,Beatrice S Knudsen,Tolga Tasdizen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DTMf-MIL方法，通过显式建模肿瘤微环境中细胞间空间距离关系（使用符号距离函数SDF），在组织病理学图像分析中提升远处转移风险预测性能，优于忽略空间布局的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MIL方法将组织切片视为无序包，丢弃了决定转移潜力的空间布局信息。现有方法无法捕捉肿瘤细胞、成纤维细胞和浸润淋巴细胞之间的几何排列关系，而这些空间结构是预测转移风险的关键。

Method: 提出Distance aware Tissue Modeling for Multiple Instance Learning (DTMf-MIL)，通过计算组织表型间的符号距离函数（SDF）来显式编码空间先验，强化视觉特征，使模型学习转移风险的结构签名。

Result: 在原发性肿瘤组织转移预测任务上显著优于忽略空间布局的SOTA方法；在公共基准数据集上验证了空间感知能力能持续提升多种临床任务的诊断准确性。

Conclusion: 肿瘤微环境中细胞的空间几何排列是决定转移风险的关键因素，显式引入空间先验能显著提升病理学计算模型的临床性能。

Abstract: Predicting the risk of distant metastasis from primary tumor tissue histology is a critical yet challenging task in computational pathology. Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) approaches can attend to subdomains in tumor regions that harbor features of metastatic cancer progression. However MIL models treat tissue patches as unordered bags, discarding the spatial layout that defines the metastatic potential. We propose that metastatic risk is inherently dictated by the geometric arrangement of the tumor microenvironment at the interface with tumor cells. Our model is designed to explicitly capture the spatial relationships between tumor cells, tumor associated fibroblasts and infiltrating lymphocytes. For this purpose, we propose Distance aware Tissue Modeling for Multiple Instance Learning(DTMf-MIL), a novel method that reinforces visual features with explicit spatial priors. By computing signed distance functions (SDF) relative to tissue phenotypes, our model learns to recognize structural signatures of metastatic risk. This geometric awareness translates directly to superior clinical performance as DTMf-MIL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods that ignore spatial layout on metastasis prediction from tissue in the primary tumor. We further validate our approach on public benchmarks, demonstrating that spatial awareness consistently improves diagnostic accuracy across diverse clinical tasks.

</details>


### [44] [SATB-VR: Training Few-Step Video Restoration Diffusion Model using SNR-Aware Trajectory Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28677)
*Haoran Bai,Xiaoxu Chen,Xiaoyu Liu,Zongsheng Yue,Sibin Deng,Wangmeng Zuo,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SATB-VR提出了一种基于SNR感知轨迹混合（SATB）策略的少步视频复原范式，通过辅助预测器跳过低SNR早期去噪步骤，并利用Denoiser-Driven Consistency损失缓解训练-推理不一致，在≤5步推理下达到优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在视频复原中性能优异但迭代步数过多导致效率低下，而激进单步蒸馏又会损失精细纹理细节。现有方法难以在效率和复原质量间取得良好平衡。

Method: 1) 引入辅助预测器跳过低信噪比早期步骤实现少步去噪；2) 提出SNR-Aware Trajectory Blending (SATB)策略，在正向过程中根据信噪比动态混合预测器输出与真实轨迹构建噪声输入，解决训练-推理不一致；3) 提出Denoiser-Driven Consistency (DDC)损失，利用去噪器作为动态评估器对齐内部特征并提升预测器精度。

Result: 在合成、真实世界和AIGC基准上，SATB-VR在灵活少步（≤5步）推理设置下优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过SATB策略和DDC损失，SATB-VR成功实现了高效与高质量视频复原的统一，为少步扩散模型在底层视觉任务中的应用提供了有效范式。

Abstract: While diffusion models excel in video restoration, their reliance on extensive iterative steps limits efficiency. Conversely, aggressive single-step distillation often compromises fine texture recovery. To achieve an optimal balance, we present SATB-VR, a few-step paradigm that jump-starts the denoising process via an auxiliary predictor, explicitly bypassing early low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) steps. However, naive joint training of the predictor and the denoiser inherently introduces a severe train-inference discrepancy. To resolve this, we propose the SNR-Aware Trajectory Blending (SATB) strategy. During the forward process, SATB constructs the noisy input by dynamically blending the predictor's output with the ground-truth trajectory based on the SNRs. This forces the denoiser to robustly compensate for initial prediction errors while smoothly converging to the clean data manifold. Furthermore, we introduce a Denoiser-Driven Consistency (DDC) loss, leveraging the concurrently updated denoiser as a dynamic evaluator to explicitly align internal features and boost predictor accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, under flexible few-step inference regimes (\eg, $\le 5$ steps), SATB-VR performs favorably against existing approaches on synthetic, real-world, and AIGC benchmarks.

</details>


### [45] [LogiCo: A Unified Framework for Logical and Structural Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28688)
*Ximiao Zhang,Min Xu,Xiuzhuang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LogiCo是一个通过组件级特征重构统一检测逻辑异常和结构异常的框架，在四个基准数据集上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法主要关注结构异常，对违反逻辑约束的异常关注不足；而逻辑异常检测方法虽能建模全局语义一致性，但因检测粒度不足在细微结构异常上表现较差。亟需一个能同时高效检测两类异常的 unified 框架。

Method: 提出LogiCo框架：1) 将预训练图像特征映射到离散的组件级特征空间；2) 在组件级和块级进行协同特征重构以捕获组件间逻辑约束和结构异常；3) 集成分割图判别器应对计数相关的逻辑异常。

Result: 在MVTec-LOCO、MVTec-AD、VisA和Real-IAD四个基准上，同时达到了逻辑异常和结构异常检测的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: LogiCo通过组件级特征重构统一了逻辑异常与结构异常检测，验证了其有效性和实用性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Current anomaly detection methods primarily focus on structural anomalies, while paying insufficient attention to anomalies that violate logical constraints. Conversely, top-performing logical anomaly detection approaches address this by modeling global semantic consistency, but perform poorly on subtle structural anomalies due to inadequate detection granularity. In this paper, we propose LogiCo, a unified framework for Logical and structural anomaly detection via Component-level feature reconstruction. Unlike existing methods that rely on explicit global semantic modeling, LogiCo employs a novel component-level feature reconstruction technique to capture inter-component logical constraints. Specifically, LogiCo maps pre-trained image features into a discrete component-level feature space and performs collaborative feature reconstruction at both component and patch levels, enabling it to effectively detect both logical and structural anomalies. Furthermore, to address the specific challenge of count-related logical anomalies, we integrate a segmentation-map discriminator that extends the model's capability to identify quantitative inconsistencies. LogiCo achieves state-of-the-art performance on both logical and structural anomaly detection across four benchmarks, including MVTec-LOCO, MVTec-AD, VisA, and Real-IAD, demonstrating its superiority and practical feasibility. The code is available at https://github.com/cnulab/LogiCo.

</details>


### [46] [Mitigating Batch Effects in Histopathology via Language-Mediated Robust Embedding Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28697)
*Yishu Zhang,Shushan Wu,Zhenzhong Zhang,Didong Li,Huaxiu Yao,Yun Li,Iain Carmichael,Katherine A. Hoadley,Hongtu Zhu,Di Wu,Daiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GLMP框架利用通用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）将组织病理学图像先转换为文本描述，再生成数值嵌入，从而有效消除批次效应（跨机构非生物变异），提升模型跨机构泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型（PFMs）受批次效应影响严重，传统方法（如染色归一化）难以解决高维复杂伪影，亟需一种能够优先保留生物学信号、抑制机构特异性伪影的通用解决方案。

Method: GLMP利用预训练的通用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）将组织病理学图像补丁转换为中间文本表示（描述组织学特征），再通过文本编码器生成鲁棒的数值嵌入，从而绕过图像域中的批次效应。

Result: GLMP在跨机构泛化任务中优于传统PFMs，证明了通用非专业MLLMs在计算病理学中的潜力，成为首个使用组织学特征文本描述作为中间表示来生成数值嵌入的病理模型。

Conclusion: GLMP开创了利用通用MLLMs构建可泛化、鲁棒的病理模型的新范式，揭示了非专业领域大模型在计算病理学中未被充分利用的潜力。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) have demonstrated strong potential across clinical and scientific applications, yet their performance is often hindered by batch effects, which are non-biological variations across tissue source institutions (TSIs) that distort learned feature representations and impair generalization. Conventional mitigation strategies, such as stain normalization, offer limited success in addressing these high-dimensional, complex artifacts. We present GLMP (General-purpose LLM-Mediated Pathology model), a novel framework that generates robust numerical embeddings from histology image patches through an intermediate textual representation. By leveraging pretrained general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and text encoders, GLMP effectively prioritizes biologically meaningful signals over TSI-specific artifacts, thereby improving cross-institutional generalization. To our knowledge, GLMP is the first pathology model to use text descriptions of histological features as an intermediate representation for generating numerical embeddings from histology images. Our results highlight the untapped potential of broad-domain, non-specialized MLLMs in computational pathology and introduce a new paradigm for building versatile, generalizable, and robust pathology models.

</details>


### [47] [CCRC: A Change-Aware Captioning and Reasoning Chain for Image Change Captioning and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28724)
*Jinhong Hu,Xiaoping Wang,Shuyin Huang,Guojin Zhong,Kaitai Liu,Kai Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图像变化描述与分割（ICCS）新任务及变化感知描述与推理链（CCRC）双链框架，将变化感知注意力模块插入多模态大语言模型，通过语义与空间解耦实现变化描述和像素级定位，在合成和真实数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像变化描述（ICC）方法缺乏空间定位能力，无法精确定位变化区域，限制了在监控、图像编辑等任务中的精度。

Method: 提出CCRC双链框架：1）CCC链：在多模态大语言模型的视觉与语言组件间插入基于多头变化感知注意力的视觉融合模块，增强细粒度变化感知并判断变化是否可分割；2）CCS链：利用CCC的空间先验，通过变化感知Token精炼器细化掩码实现精确边界定位。不可分割时仅由CCC生成描述。

Result: 在合成和真实变化检测基准上，CCRC达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: CCRC通过语义推理与空间分割解耦的设计，有效解决了传统ICC缺乏空间定位的问题，验证了联合变化描述与分割的可行性。

Abstract: Understanding and localizing subtle changes between paired images is critical for tasks such as surveillance and image editing. However, traditional Image Change Captioning (ICC) methods lack spatial grounding, limiting their precision. We introduce Image Change Captioning and Segmentation (ICCS), a new multimodal task that jointly requires structured change description and pixel-level localization. To address ICCS, we propose the Change-aware Captioning and Reasoning Chain (CCRC), a dual-chain framework that decouples semantic reasoning from spatial segmentation. The first chain, Chain-of-Change-Captioning (CCC), enhances fine-grained change perception via a visual fusion module based on Multi-Head Change-aware Attention inserted between the visual and language components of a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM). CCC also determines whether a change is segmentable. If not, it alone generates the caption. Otherwise, the second chain, Chain-of-Change-Segmenting (CCS), is activated, leveraging spatial priors from CCC and refining masks with a Change-aware Token Refiner for accurate boundary localization. We evaluate CCRC on both synthetic and real-world change detection benchmarks with pixel-level supervision. Experiments show CCRC achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [48] [FreqOrtho-SR: Frequency-Guided Orthogonal Expert Learning for Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28745)
*Minh Son Hoang,Dinh Phu Tran,Quyen Nguyen Duc,Dam Hoang Phuong,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreqOrtho-SR 提出频率引导的混合 LoRA 专家（FreqMoE）和正交梯度投影（OGP）方法，分别解决图像超分中多样性退化适配问题和像素保真度与语义质量的双目标优化冲突，在多个基准上取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散先验超分方法面临两个关键挑战：1）像素保真度与语义质量之间的平衡难以把握，现有双分支方法在训练语义分支时冻结像素模块，但语义分支仍会在像素子空间内扩展容量，无法实现真正的感知提升；2）使用单一静态适配器无法泛化到异构的真实世界退化类型。

Method: 提出 FreqOrtho-SR，包含两个核心组件：1）FreqMoE（频率引导的混合 LoRA 专家）：通过非参数化的 FFT 退化特征提取器编码频域特征，将输入路由到专门的 LoRA 专家，实现稳定且可解释的退化类型特化；2）OGP（正交梯度投影）：将双目标优化重述为子空间约束问题，通过对组合专家权重增量进行 SVD 分解提取像素保真度子空间，将语义梯度投影到其零空间，保证两个目标正交，实现无干扰的互补学习。

Result: 在多个基准上取得竞争性整体性能，实现了强保真度-感知质量权衡，且支持高效的单步推理。

Conclusion: FreqOrtho-SR 通过频域引导的 MoE 实现退化感知的专家特化，并通过正交梯度投影从根本上解决双目标优化中的相互干扰问题，为真实世界图像超分提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion prior-based methods have shown impressive results in real-world image super-resolution (ISR), yet two key challenges persist: balancing pixel-level fidelity with semantic quality, and adapting to diverse degradations. Existing dual-branch approaches freeze the pixel module during semantic training, but the semantic branch can still expand capacity within the pixel subspace, precluding genuine perceptual improvement. Moreover, using a single static adapter cannot generalize across heterogeneous real-world corruptions. To address both issues, we propose FreqOrtho-SR, which comprises: $\textbf{Freq}$uency-guided Mixture of LoRA Experts (FreqMoE), it routes inputs to specialized experts via a non-parametric FFT-based degradation-feature extractor that encodes frequency-domain signatures, enabling stable and interpretable specialization across corruption types; and $\textbf{Ortho}$gonal Gradient Projection (OGP), which reframes the dual-objective optimization as a subspace-constrained problem: by extracting the pixel-fidelity subspace via SVD on combined expert weight deltas and projecting semantic gradients onto its null space, OGP guarantees orthogonality between the two objectives, enabling genuinely complementary learning without mutual interference. Experiments show that FreqOrtho-SR achieves competitive overall performance and a strong fidelity-perception trade-off across multiple benchmarks with efficient single-step inference. The source code of our method can be found at $\href{https://github.com/sonhm3029/FreqOrtho-SR}{\texttt{sonhm3029/FreqOrtho-SR}}$.

</details>


### [49] [A Physics-Grounded Benchmark for Multi-Agent Dynamics in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28757)
*Nuo Chen,Lulin Liu,Zihao Li,Ziyao Zeng,Zihao Zhu,Wenyan Cong,Junyuan Hong,Yunhao Yang,Zhengzhong Tu,Yan Wang,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Zhangyang Wang,Yang Zhou,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrashTwin 是一个物理可信性评估框架，通过校准无关的重建管道和多智能体碰撞场景数据集，系统性地测试世界模型在时空一致性、动量/动能守恒和世界动力学完整性三个维度的物理可信度，揭示了高感知质量常掩盖严重物理违规的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型评估过度依赖视觉保真度和语义对齐，无法量化生成动态是否符合基本物理定律，缺乏评估物理合理性的指标和从未校准视频中提取度量级运动学的方法。

Method: 构建包含25K可控合成和12K真实世界碰撞序列的数据集，提出校准无关的3D物理属性重建管道，设计涵盖时空一致性、动量/动能守恒和世界动力学完整性的诊断套件。

Result: 对现有最先进模型的基准测试显示，高感知质量经常掩盖复杂交互过程中的严重物理违规。

Conclusion: CrashTwin 为开发物理可信的世界模型提供了关键诊断工具，确保其能够可靠地进行真实世界模拟。

Abstract: Generative world models hold immense promise as scalable simulators for autonomous systems, particularly for synthesizing rare but safety-critical multi-agent interactions, such as vehicle collisions. However, current evaluation paradigms index heavily on visual fidelity and semantic alignment, leaving a critical blind spot: they cannot reliably quantify whether generated dynamics actually obey the fundamental physical laws required for reliable simulation. Assessing this physical plausibility is inherently difficult due to a lack of physical metrics and the challenge of extracting metric-scale kinematics from uncalibrated video rollouts. To bridge this gap, we introduce CrashTwin, a physics-grounded evaluation framework designed to stress-test the physical trustworthiness of world models. CrashTwin couples a diverse dataset of multi-agent collision scenarios, comprising 25K controllable synthetic and 12K in-the-wild real-world collision sequences with a novel calibration-free reconstruction pipeline, enabling the recovery of 3D physical attributes directly from world model rollouts. We propose a diagnostic suite that systematically evaluates three dimensions: spatio-temporal consistency, momentum and kinetic energy conservation, and world-dynamics integrity. Extensive benchmarking of state-of-the-art models reveals a crucial insight: high perceptual quality frequently masks severe physical violations during complex interactions. By quantitatively exposing these failure modes, CrashTwin provides a vital diagnostic tool for developing physically grounded world models capable of reliable real-world simulation.

</details>


### [50] [X-Mind: Efficient Visual Chain-of-Thought via Predictive World Model for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28758)
*Bohao Zhao,Chengrui Wei,Guangfeng Jiang,Ruixin Liu,Xuejie Lv,Liu Liang,Sutao Deng,Xiuyang Fan,Pengkun Zheng,Jinyun Zhou,Rui Guo,Hanpeng Liu,Yutong Zheng,Yi Guo,Xinlong Zheng,Qingyu Luo,Zhuangzhuang Ding,Yu Zhang,Hang Zhang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-Mind通过将预测世界模型内化为视觉思维链，使VLA模型具备前瞻性推理能力。它利用紧凑的BEV草图表示（12帧仅96 tokens）和循环块扩散机制（将去噪步骤折叠进骨干网络单次前向传播），在资源受限的车载平台上实现了低延迟的端到端驾驶策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏预测未来状态的能力，仅依赖反应式感知-动作映射。虽然集成预测世界模型可弥补这一缺陷，但现有方案要么产生级联延迟，要么仅作为浅层终端任务，未能深度融合前瞻推理。

Method: (1) 将PWM内化为视觉思维链(Visual CoT)，在动作前强制进行世界展开推理；(2) 紧凑表示：BEV布局融合抽象驾驶先验（导航意图、交通规则），通过深度压缩自编码器将12帧未来展开压缩至96 tokens；(3) 循环块扩散方案：将去噪步骤跨层展开到驱动模型骨干网络中，将迭代细化折叠进单次前向传播。

Result: 在大规模真实世界数据上训练和验证，X-Mind实现了具有竞争力的端到端驾驶性能，成为一个低延迟的实用解决方案，成功将大规模认知推理部署到资源受限的车载平台。

Conclusion: X-Mind通过紧凑的视觉思维表示和高效的扩散机制，在保证端到端驾驶性能的同时实现了低延迟，使得在资源受限平台上部署大规模认知推理成为可能。

Abstract: Predicting future states is essential for autonomous agents, yet current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models fundamentally lack this capability, relying instead on reactive perception-action mapping. While integrating Predictive World Models (PWMs) addresses this gap, existing approaches either incur prohibitive cascaded latency or act as shallow terminal tasks that fail to deeply embed forward-looking reasoning. To endow VLA models with this reasoning capability, we propose X-Mind. Rather than treating PWMs as an external auxiliary module, this framework internalizes them as the Visual Chain-of-Thought (Visual CoT). By enforcing a world rollout prior to action, the model is constrained to imagine future evolution first, yielding a driving policy that is robustly grounded in environmental dynamics and aware of the future consequences its actions will unfold. The challenge here is efficiency, and we tackle it on two fronts. First, we introduce a compact representation of visual thinking: an abstract sketch that fuses a Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) layout with abstract driving priors (e.g., navigation intents and traffic rules). Rather than rolling out dense future frames, the model reasons over this sketch as a mental canvas; aided by a Deep Compression Autoencoder (DC-AE), a 12-frame future rollout is reduced to merely 96 tokens, alleviating the long-context computational bottleneck. Second, to accelerate generation further, we propose a recurrent block diffusion scheme that unrolls the denoising steps across the layers of the large drive model, folding iterative refinement into the backbone's one forward pass. Trained and validated on large-scale real-world data, X-Mind achieves competitive end-to-end driving performance, which makes it a highly practical, low-latency solution that successfully deploys large-scale cognitive reasoning directly onto resource-constrained vehicle platforms.

</details>


### [51] [Stochastic Optimal Control Sampling for Diffusion Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28785)
*Jie Zhang,Youmei Qiu,Hanling Tian,Jingyuan Zhang,Xiang Yin,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出随机最优控制采样（SOCS），将扩散模型的去噪过程建模为动态系统，通过推导封闭形式的控制更新来注入控制信号，从而实现高效且高质量的图像逆问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于随机最优控制（SOC）的逆问题方法需要对整个轨迹进行优化，计算成本高昂。本文旨在通过推导封闭形式的控制更新，在每个采样步即时注入控制信号，在降低计算开销的同时保持测量一致性和感知质量。

Method: 将去噪过程建模为动态系统，在每个采样步骤推导封闭形式的控制更新，将测量一致的干净预测拉回到去噪流上，并支持调节控制强度以匹配扩散模型的固有生成能力。

Result: 在多种线性随机微分方程骨干和广泛的图像逆任务上，SOCS实现了准确的测量对齐重建，并在视觉保真度和定量指标上表现更优。

Conclusion: SOCS是一种高效、即时的控制方法，能够在不牺牲感知质量的前提下实现测量一致性，且具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Benefiting from the strong ability to capture data distributions, diffusion models have become powerful tools for solving image inverse problems. The key is to controllably steer the sampling trajectory toward the measurements while respecting the diffusion prior. In this work, we introduce Stochastic Optimal Control Sampling (SOCS), which models the denoising process as a dynamical system and injects control signals via SOC. Previous SOC-based approach addresses inverse problems by optimizing over the entire trajectory, which is computationally expensive. In contrast, we derive a closed-form control update and apply it at each sampling step, pulling the measurement-consistent clean prediction back onto the denoising flow. In SOCS, we can readily modulate the control strength to align with the diffusion model's native capabilities and thereby enhance perceptual quality. Our method is compatible with a variety of linear stochastic differential equation backbones. Extensive experiments across a broad spectrum of image inverse tasks demonstrate that SOCS achieves accurate measurement-aligned reconstructions with improved visual fidelity and stronger quantitative performance.

</details>


### [52] [BREIT: A Framework for Brain Stroke Reconstruction using Multi-Frequency 3D EIT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28787)
*Djahid Abdelmoumene,Ishak Ayad,Maï K. Nguyen,Christian Daveau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BREIT框架，用于3D多频电阻抗断层成像中脑卒中重建，包含从CT/MRI到EIT的完整管线、3D正问题求解器和D-bar反演方法。基于BREIT，提出dFNO-bar方法，将傅里叶神经算子集成到D-bar中，在合成数据上取得了优于传统方法的脑部SSIM指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MF-EIT在3D深度学习重建中面临缺乏大规模配对真值数据集以及数据生成、仿真和评估流程非标准化的问题，限制了该领域的发展。

Method: 提出BREIT模块化框架，包含：(i) 神经影像到EIT的管线，将CT/MRI转换为频率依赖的真值导纳率体积；(ii) 自包含的Python 3D完整电极模型正问题求解器；(iii) 支持非均匀电极布局的3D D-bar实现。在此基础上提出dFNO-bar，通过傅里叶神经算子学习从散射数据到电导率的映射。

Result: 在UCLH匹配的合成数据上，dFNO-bar与D-bar、Deep D-bar和高斯-牛顿方法对比，在不同噪声设置下获得了更高的脑部SSIM，且CC相当。

Conclusion: BREIT为3D MF-EIT脑卒中重建提供了标准化的端到端流程，dFNO-bar有效结合了数据驱动与物理驱动的优势，为EIT深度学习重建提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Multi-Frequency Electrical Impedance Tomography (MF-EIT) is a non-invasive, low-cost modality that reconstructs electrical property distributions from boundary voltages. For stroke imaging, progress in 3D deep-learning reconstruction is limited by the lack of large-scale datasets with paired ground-truth (GT) volumes and by non-standardized pipelines for data generation, simulation, and evaluation. We introduce BREIT, a modular framework for 3D MF-EIT stroke reconstruction providing: (i) a neuroimaging-to-EIT pipeline that converts CT/MRI into frequency-dependent GT admittivity volumes; (ii) a self-contained Python 3D Complete Electrode Model (CEM) forward solver for simulating MF-EIT voltages; and (iii) a 3D D-bar implementation supporting non-uniform electrode layouts. Building on BREIT, we propose dFNO-bar, which integrates Fourier Neural Operators into D-bar by learning a mapping from scattering data $t(ξ)$ to conductivity $σ(x){=}\Re\{γ\}$. We evaluate dFNO-bar against D-bar, Deep D-bar, and Gauss--Newton reconstructions on UCLH-matched synthetic data, and observe higher brain SSIM with comparable CC across noise settings. Code and data are publicly available at: https://github.com/djahiddj13/BREIT

</details>


### [53] [Virtual Ring Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28792)
*Vishnu D. Burkhawala,Zankhana J. Barad,Harshadkumar B. Prajapati,Vipul K. Dabhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于移动应用的虚拟戒指试戴方法，利用MediaPipe手部关键点检测和YOLO-V8目标检测技术，结合向量代数计算角度偏差和尺寸缩放，实现戒指在用户手指上的真实感贴合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用户能够通过移动设备便捷地尝试不同款式的戒指，提升线上购物体验，解决传统虚拟试戴中贴合不准确、真实感不足的问题。

Method: 1. 用户通过React Native GUI拍摄手部图像；2. MediaPipe检测手部关键点并生成感兴趣区域（ROI）掩膜；3. YOLO-V8对戒指进行目标检测，去除背景；4. 利用向量代数计算手指参考轴与戒指主轴的角偏差；5. 根据手指厚度缩放戒指尺寸并保持宽高比；6. 将戒指贴合到手部图像上生成最终输出。

Result: 系统能够生成用户手部与戒指合成的图像，实现了基于手部关键点检测和物体检测的虚拟戒指试戴效果。

Conclusion: 结合MediaPipe和YOLO-V8的方法能够有效实现虚拟戒指试戴，为用户提供直观、便捷的线上试戴体验。

Abstract: This paper presents an innovative approach that enables the users to capture their hand and try the jewel ring on their hand. The user captures the image of the hand using the React Native base GUI of the mobile application and selects the ring that the user wants to try, and the output image will have the user's hand with the ring image. This approach is implemented using a combination of MediaPipe hand point detection and YOLO-V8 custom object detection. The hand image uploaded by the user first undergoes mediapipe hand point detection. It will give the hand points and a Region of Interest mask where the ring is going to be placed. Then the ring is passed through YOLO object detection, in which ring points are detected, and background is removed. After that, using vector algebra, the angular discrepancy between the finger's reference axis and the ring's principal axis is computed. Also, ring size is rescaled according to finger thickness, preserving the aspect ratio to maintain perceptual realism. Then the ring is placed on the hand image and the output image is generated and shown on the user screen.

</details>


### [54] [PSP: Harnessing Position and Shape Priors for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28799)
*Bin Xu,Yazhou Zhu,Haofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对跨域小样本医学图像分割中纹理差异导致的性能崩溃问题，提出利用位置与形状先验（PSP）框架，通过位置坐标嵌入、形状原型调制和混合原型预测三个模块实现域不变特征学习，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小样本医学图像分割方法在不同成像模态间泛化能力差，核心原因是纹理差异导致模型被源域强度分布误导。已有方法虽尝试对齐频率或局部纹理特征，但未能将语义结构与领域特定外观解耦。

Method: 提出PSP框架，包含三个模块：(1) 位置坐标嵌入（PCE）模块，注入相对空间坐标以快速定位器官；(2) 形状原型调制（SPM）模块，通过显式形状先验构建域不变结构原型，过滤纹理噪声；(3) 混合原型预测（HPP）模块，自适应校准支持原型到查询特征分布，缓解特征不对齐。

Result: 在两个公开医学影像数据集上的大量实验表明，PSP显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 器官的位置和几何形状在不同模态间保持鲁棒一致性，利用这一不变性设计的位置与形状先验框架能有效解决跨域小样本医学图像分割中的纹理差异问题。

Abstract: Few-Shot Medical Image Segmentation (FSMIS) offers a powerful solution to data scarcity but struggles to generalize across different imaging modalities. This performance collapse stems primarily from the drastic texture discrepancies between domains, which mislead models trained on source-specific intensity distributions. While existing methods attempt to align frequency or local texture features, they often fail to decouple semantic structure from domain-specific appearance. To address this, we identify a critical invariance: despite distinct imaging physics, the position and geometric shape of organs remain robustly consistent across modalities. Therefore, we propose a novel framework that harnesses Position and Shape Priors (PSP) for cross-domain FSMIS. Specifically, PSP first introduces a Position Coordinate Embedding (PCE) module to inject relative spatial coordinates for rapid organ localization. Subsequently, a Shape Prototype Modulation (SPM) module constructs domain-invariant structural prototypes via explicit shape priors, effectively filtering out texture noise. Furthermore, the Hybrid-Prototype Prediction (HPP) module adaptively calibrates the support prototype to the query feature distribution, mitigating feature misalignment. Extensive experiments on two public medical imaging datasets demonstrate that PSP significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [55] [ViPSim: Collaborating Visual and Parameter Spaces for Consistent Long-Horizon Embodied World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28804)
*Longyu Chen,Heng Li,Wei Yang,Manqi Zhao,Dongsheng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViPSim通过视觉空间（显式空间先验）和参数空间（数值驱动）的协同，弥合低维动作与高维视频合成之间的表征差距，实现具身世界模型中一致的长程轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身世界模型（EWMs）作为评估基准和基础仿真器时，因低维动作与高维视频合成之间的表征差距导致几何对应缺失，表现为长程 rollout 中的轨迹漂移和机器人-物体交互不一致。

Method: 提出ViPSim框架，定义视觉空间（集成末端执行器位姿、相机视角、深度感知场景几何和机器人形态掩码的像素对齐投影）和参数空间（注入原始动作序列和相机矩阵），通过两个空间的协同统一确保生成状态同时受几何边界约束和数值指令引导。

Result: ViPSim是骨干网络无关的，显著提升轨迹一致性；在可变形物体（如布料折叠）的复杂交互中展现出涌现能力，并在分布外和跨形态场景中保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: ViPSim为具身智能体的自动化评估和预测控制提供了高保真基础，弥合了动作空间与视觉生成之间的表征鸿沟。

Abstract: Embodied World Models (EWMs) have emerged as a scalable and risk-free paradigm for advancing embodied intelligence, enabling the safety-critical evaluation of Vision-Language-Action systems. However, their reliability as evaluation benchmarks and foundational simulators is often hindered by the representation gap between low-dimensional actions and high-dimensional video synthesis. This gap results in a lack of geometric correspondence, manifesting as accumulated trajectory drift and inconsistent robot-object interactions during long-horizon rollouts. To bridge this gap, we propose ViPSim, a framework that achieves consistent long-horizon generation through the synergistic collaboration of Visual and Parameter Spaces. We define the Visual Space as a domain of explicit spatial priors, integrating pixel-aligned projections of end-effector pose, camera perspectives, depth-informed scene geometry, and robotic morphological masks to provide dense structural grounding. Concurrently, the Parameter Space serves as a domain of numerical drivers, injecting raw action sequences and camera matrices to provide precise motion guidance. By unifying these two spaces, ViPSim ensures that the generated states are simultaneously anchored by geometric boundaries and steered by numerical commands. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ViPSim is backbone-agnostic and significantly enhances trajectory consistency. Notably, our approach exhibits emergent capabilities in generating complex interactions with deformable objects (e.g., cloth folding) and maintains robust performance in out-of-distribution and cross-embodiment scenarios, providing a high-fidelity foundation for the automated evaluation and predictive control of embodied agents.

</details>


### [56] [CoGS: Compositional Dynamic Human-Object Scenes Gaussian Splatting from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28820)
*Jerrin Bright,John Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoGS提出了一种组合式高斯泼溅框架，将单目视频中的人-物交互场景分解为人体、刚性物体和静态场景三个独立分支，通过六阶段优化实现高保真重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态辐射场和高斯泼溅方法常将人体、操作物体和背景纠缠在一起，导致物体运动泄漏到人体或静态场景中，且单目人体重建在稀疏观测区域存在欠约束问题。

Method: 将视频分解为三个协调分支：基于完整规范先验初始化的人体（铰接模型）、由估计轨迹驱动的刚性物体场、以及由弱平面基元正则化的静态场景场。采用六阶段优化流程：先独立稳定人体和物体，再在全图监督、可见性感知人体锚定、物体轮廓与运动约束、延迟场景正则化下融合三者。

Result: 在HOSNeRF和NeuMan数据集上，CoGS在人体-物体交互重建和野外人体-场景渲染方面均取得更优的保真度和感知质量。

Conclusion: CoGS通过显式解耦人体、物体和场景的运动模型，有效避免了运动泄漏问题，提升了单目人-物交互场景的重建质量。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic human--object interaction scenes from monocular video is difficult because the human, manipulated object, and background obey different motion models while sharing the same pixels. Existing dynamic radiance-field and Gaussian-splatting methods often entangle these components, causing object motion to leak into the human or static scene, and monocular human reconstruction remains underconstrained in regions that are rarely observed. We present CoGS, a compositional Gaussian-splatting framework for monocular human--object scene reconstruction. CoGS decomposes the video into three coordinated branches: an articulated human initialized from a complete canonical prior, a rigid object field driven by an estimated object trajectory, and a static scene field regularized by weak scene-only planar primitives when available. A six-stage optimization schedule first stabilizes the human and object independently, then fuses them with the scene under full-image supervision, visibility-aware human anchoring, object silhouette and motion constraints, and delayed scene regularization. This design keeps each component responsible for its own geometry and motion while allowing photometric evidence to correct the final composite. Experiments on HOSNeRF and NeuMan show that CoGS improves both human--object interaction reconstruction and in-the-wild human--scene rendering, achieving stronger fidelity and perceptual quality across full-frame and human-focused evaluations. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [57] [RefGlass-GS: A UAV-Enabled Fusion Framework for Photorealistic, Semantic and Interactive Digitization of Reflective Glass Facades via Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28826)
*Zhenyu Liang,Xiao Zhang,Boyu Wang,Zhaolun Liang,Ang Li,Jeff Chak Fu Chan,Mingzhu Wang,Jack C. P. Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RefGlass-GS融合框架，通过基于最大后验估计的玻璃面板分割、无人机视角规划优化、反射MLP增强的高斯泼溅建模以及标准化数据组织范式，实现了反射玻璃幕墙的端到端逼真、语义及交互式数字化，在面板分割(mIoU提升0.1927)、视角规划(PSNR提升13.15dB)和反射场景建模(PSNR平均提升5.08dB)上均显著超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有带反射玻璃幕墙建筑的数字化存在几何重建失真、视点相关纹理渲染不真实以及基于对象的语义增强困难等问题。

Method: (1)基于最大后验估计与结构规则性的单个玻璃面板分割方法；(2)最大化视点相关外观覆盖率的无人机视角规划优化函数；(3)集成反射MLP、延迟着色函数和两种增强正则项的优化高斯泼溅框架；(4)将GS表示结构化为基于对象模型的标准数据组织范式。

Result: 玻璃面板分割mIoU提升0.1927，实现实例级面板提取；无人机视角规划相比商业方法PSNR提升13.15dB；RefGlass-GS建模相比SOTA高斯泼溅方法PSNR平均提升5.08dB。

Conclusion: 在真实反射玻璃幕墙场景上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性和优越性，能够实现数字孪生平台上的交互式设施管理。

Abstract: Existing digitization of buildings with reflective glass facades suffers from geometric reconstruction distortion, unrealistic view-dependent texture rendering, and difficulties in object-based semantic enhancement. Therefore, we propose RefGlass-GS, a fusion framework that enables end-to-end UAV-based photorealistic, semantic, and interactive digitization of reflective glass facades. The contributions include: (1) proposing an individual glass panel segmentation method based on maximum a posteriori estimation with structural regularities, robust to severe reflection and background interference; (2) formulating a UAV viewpoint planning optimization function that maximizes the coverage of view-dependent appearance for sufficient data capture; (3) developing an optimized Gaussian Splatting framework with a Reflection MLP, a novel deferred shading function, and two enhanced regularization terms for effective modeling of high-frequency near-field reflections; (4) introducing a standardized data organization paradigm for structuring GS-based representations into object-based models, facilitating interactive facility management on digital twin platforms. Experiments on real-world reflective glass facade scenes validate the effectiveness and superiority of the proposed method. Specifically, the glass panel segmentation achieves an improvement of 0.1927 in mIoU over SOTA methods, and only our method enables instance-level panel extraction. The UAV view planning improves novel view synthesis for reflective facades by 13.15 dB in PSNR compared to commercially used nap-of-the-object planning methods. The RefGlass-GS modeling outperforms SOTA Gaussian Splatting approaches for reflective scenes with an average improvement of 5.08 dB in PSNR.

</details>

### [58] [Generating in the Limit with Infinitely Many Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28354)
*Irene Strauss,Alexandra Butoi,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在语言生成极限框架下引入精确率概念，将问题重新表述为经典的召回率-精确率权衡，分析了允许无限错误但频率趋零的松弛条件，以及连续新颖性约束，揭示了在目标语言被大量隐藏时，松弛条件可显著提升召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的语言识别极限范式与语言生成极限框架之间存在张力：广泛覆盖目标语言往往以牺牲有效性为代价。本文旨在引入精确率概念，将这一问题重新表述为召回率-精确率权衡，并分析更贴近大语言模型实际场景的松弛约束条件。

Method: 定义了语言生成极限下的精确率，分析不要求最终有效的学习者（允许无限错误但频率趋零）；研究连续松弛新颖性约束（仅要求固定比例输出新颖）；在不同枚举、新颖性和有效性约束下分析生成行为。

Result: 允许无限错误但频率趋零的松弛条件（精确率保持为1）在对手永久隐藏大部分目标语言时，可严格提升召回率；连续新颖性约束下也得出相应结果。

Conclusion: 研究结果向更现实的的语言生成模型靠近，在该模型中偶尔的错误和重复不可避免，但其比率可控。

Abstract: The classic paradigm of language identification in the limit models learning as a game between an adversary, who reveals strings from an unknown target language, and a learner tasked with identifying that language. The recently introduced framework of language generation in the limit shifted the objective to better reflect modern language modeling, requiring the learner to produce valid, unseen strings from the target language. Related work highlighted a fundamental tension: a broad coverage of the target often comes at the cost of validity. We introduce a new notion of precision and recast this problem as the classic recall-precision trade-off. We analyze generation in the limit under varying constraints on enumeration, novelty, and validity, aimed at reflecting settings closer to those encountered by large language models. A key contribution is our analysis of learners that are not eventually valid: we allow infinitely many mistakes, provided their frequency tends to zero so that precision remains one. We show that this relaxation can strictly increase recall when the adversary permanently withholds a large portion of the target language. We also study a continuous relaxation of the novelty constraint that requires only a fixed fraction of outputs to be novel. Taken together, our results move toward a more realistic model of language generation where occasional errors and repetitions are unavoidable, but their rates are controlled.

</details>

*Diaa M. Fayed,Aly A. Fahmy,Mohsen A. Rashwan,Wafaa K. Fayed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从阿拉伯语-英语Al-Mawrid机读词典中自动提取词汇信息的方法，采用n-gram分析、KWIC分析和基于规则的信息抽取，在语义信息提取上取得了高精度，但除同义词外其他信息的召回率较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP应用需要大量丰富的语言知识，而电子语言资源（词典、百科全书、语料库）的可用性日益增加，需要自动方法从中提取词汇信息，以克服知识获取瓶颈。

Method: 采用n-gram分析和关键词索引（KWIC）分析发现词汇模式，利用手工制定的规则进行信息抽取，并利用标点符号和启发式方法提取子条目中的同义词集。

Result: 所有类型信息的精确率均较高；同义词召回率高，但其他信息的召回率较低。Al-Mawrid词典包含大量派生词（形态信息）、同义词、领域标签及上下位关系（语义信息）。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效从Al-Mawrid双语词典中自动提取词汇知识，尤其在同义词提取方面表现优异，但其他类型信息的召回率有待提升。

Abstract: Natural language processing (NLP) applications need large and rich amount of linguistic knowledge. Furthermore, electronic language sources such as dictionaries, encyclopedia, and corpora became available. So, automatic methods are emerged to extract lexical information from those sources to overcome the knowledge acquisition bottleneck. We presented a method to automatically extract lexical information from a machine-readable version of the Arabic-English Al-Mawrid dictionary. We used n-gram analysis and key-word-in-context (KWIC) analysis to discover lexical patterns that manifest morphologic, syntactic, or semantic information. Then, we used hand-crafted rule-based information extraction to extract that information. Furthermore, we used punctuation marks and some heuristics to extract a set of synonyms in a subentry. This study registered high precision for all types of information, high recall for synonyms, and low recall for the other information. The study also showed that the Al-Mawrid has significant amount of derivations (morphologic information) and synonyms, domain labels, and hyponym/hypernym relations (semantic information).

</details>


### [60] [Developmental Trajectories of Situation Modeling and Mentalizing in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28524)
*Pamela D. Rivière,Cameron Jones,Sean Trott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过发展心理学视角，追踪Olmo2和Pythia语言模型在不同训练阶段的心理状态推理（错误信念任务）和情境建模能力的涌现模式，发现错误信念任务表现依赖于模型规模与训练量，后期训练（SFT/DPO）能提升但表现脆弱，情境建模能力通常先于且优于心理推理能力，但情境表征在某些方面不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究表明LLMs对文本描述的信念状态敏感，但构念效度问题持续存在。作者采用发展视角，探究心理状态推理能力的涌现条件与前提，以更严格评估LLMs的心智化能力。

Method: 在Olmo2和Pythia模型套件中，跨多个训练阶段追踪错误信念任务表现；对比情境建模能力（场景基本事实报告）与心理推理能力的涌现时序；引入非事实性动词（如thinks）和不同信念条件（真信念/假信念/显式/隐式）进行压力测试；分析对抗智能体的知识状态表示是否受目标智能体知识状态干扰。

Result: 1）错误信念任务表现依赖模型规模和充分训练量，在预训练后期才涌现；2）后训练（SFT、DPO）在最具诊断性的条件（假信念、隐式）下提升最显著；3）非事实性动词增加错误信念归因，即使是在真信念条件下；4）情境建模准确性通常先于且优于错误信念任务准确性；5）Olmo2 13b对对抗智能体知识状态的判断受目标智能体知识状态和非事实性动词影响。

Conclusion: 更大规模、充分训练的模型能按发展上合理的顺序构建部分连贯的情境模型，但表现出令人惊讶的脆弱性——强调了发展和压力测试方法对评估LLM能力的重要性。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that Large Language Models (LLMs) are sensitive to the belief states of agents described by text, as measured by the false belief task (FBT), yet persistent concerns of construct validity remain. We adopt a **developmental perspective**, tracing the pattern of mental state reasoning behavior -- and likely **preconditions** for this behavior -- across multiple training stages in the Olmo2 and Pythia language model suites. We find that above-chance FBT performance depends both on model size and sufficient training volume, emerges relatively late in pretraining, and is most improved by post-training interventions (SFT, DPO) in the condition most diagnostic of mentalizing (False Belief, Implicit). However, FBT performance is fragile: consistent with past work, the use of non-factive verbs (e.g., thinks) increases false belief attributions even in the True Belief condition. To contextualize these findings, we track the emergence of **situation modeling**: the ability to report on basic factual properties of a described scene. Situation modeling accuracy generally precedes and exceeds FBT accuracy, yet situational representations also prove surprisingly incoherent in certain respects: when asked about the knowledge states of the Antagonist agent -- who always knows the item's true location -- Olmo2 13b is consistently influenced both by the Target agent's knowledge state and the presence of non-factive verbs. Together, these results suggest that larger, sufficiently trained models build partially coherent situation models in a developmentally appropriate sequence, yet display surprising fragility -- highlighting the value of developmental and stress-testing approaches for evaluating LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [61] [A French OSCE Dialogue Dataset and Controllable Virtual Patient System for Clinical Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28526)
*Doria Bonzi,Tom Bourgeade,Fabrice Lefèvre,Irina Illina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个法语OSCE对话数据集（240次医患互动），并构建了基于可控LLM的虚拟患者生成管道，通过检索增强和反思循环提升患者真实感，同时设计了多层级LLM评估框架，实验表明可控性模块改善了患者 fidelity 和学生评估一致性，最后实现了交互式虚拟患者训练原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客观结构化临床考试（OSCE）中标准化病人（SP） availability 低，限制了医学生的临床与沟通技能训练，因此需要开发逼真的虚拟患者（VP）来弥补这一 gap。

Method: 构建法语OSCE对话数据集；设计基于可控LLM的合成对话生成管道，包含检索式grounding模块和反思循环；提出多层级评估框架（患者模拟质量、学生表现、语言质量），采用LLM-as-a-Judge方法。

Result: 可控性模块（检索grounding+反思循环）总体上提升了患者 fidelity 和学生评估一致性。

Conclusion: 基于可控LLM的虚拟患者生成方法能有效提升OSCE训练中虚拟患者的逼真度与评估一致性，并可用于构建交互式训练原型。

Abstract: The clinical and communication skills of medical students are commonly assessed through Objective Structured Clinical Examinations (OSCEs), which consist of brief scenario-driven simulations of doctor-patient interactions. However, training is often limited by the low availability of human standardized patients, motivating the development of realistic virtual patients (VPs). To address this gap, we introduce a French OSCE dialogue dataset comprising 240 student-patient training interactions. We build upon it a controllable LLM-based pipeline to generate synthetic OSCE dialogues. The pipeline integrates modular components, such as retrieval-based grounding and a reflection loop, to ensure patient fidelity, coherence, and realism. Additionally, we propose a multi-level evaluation framework assessing patient simulation quality, student performance, and linguistic quality, using an LLM-as-a-Judge approach. Experiments suggest that controllability modules generally improve patient fidelity and student evaluation consistency. Finally, we implement an interactive prototype in which students can practice with a VP and receive automatic feedback.

</details>


### [62] [Legal Domain Adaptation of Modern BERT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28538)
*Dominik Stammbach,Peter Henderson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了法律领域的BERT模型领域适应，对ModernBERT进行US court opinions的进一步预训练，发现即使ModernBERT训练数据比原始BERT多500倍，领域适应仍能带来显著提升，但从头预训练效果不如基于现有检查点继续训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索现代BERT模型（ModernBERT）在法律领域的领域适应效果，验证在数据量远超原始BERT的情况下，领域特定进一步预训练是否仍有价值。

Method: 对ModernBERT模型使用掩码语言建模目标，在全部美国法院意见数据集上进行进一步预训练，实现领域适应。

Result: 相比原始ModernBERT，领域适应后的模型在所有与美国法院意见相关的数据集上均取得显著提升；增益幅度与早期BERT领域适应工作相当；从头预训练效果不及基于现有检查点继续训练。

Conclusion: 即使在大规模预训练模型上，领域特定的进一步预训练仍然有效；模型可处理长达8,192个token的序列，适用于法律段落嵌入和搜索重排序；模型已公开发布。

Abstract: We investigate domain adaptation of modern BERT models in the legal domain. We further pre-train ModernBERT on all US court opinions using the masked language modeling objective. Although ModernBERT has been trained on roughly 500x more data than original BERT, we still find that this model benefits from further pre-training and domain adaptation in the legal domain: we report significant improvements compared to vanilla ModernBERT on all datasets connected to US court opinions. We find gains similar to those reported in early work on domain adaptation of BERT-like models. However, from scratch pre-training does not match the performance of further pre-training an existing ModernBERT checkpoint in our experiments. The resulting models are capable of processing sequences up to 8,192 tokens, and can be used to compute meaningful embeddings of legal passages, or could quickly rerank hundreds of legal passages for a given search query. We release all model checkpoints publicly.

</details>


### [63] [Turn-Averaged SAEs for Feature Discovery and Long-Context Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28548)
*Kevin Der,Harish Kamath,Ben Thompson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"轮次平均稀疏自编码器"(turn-averaged SAE)，通过重构模型在单个轮次中的平均激活值，将每个轮次表示为固定数量的特征，从而解决了标准SAE在长上下文场景下特征数量随序列长度线性增长的问题，显著简化了长文本的可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE对每个token的激活值独立提取特征，导致特征数量随上下文长度线性增长，使得分析长模型转录本变得困难。研究者希望设计一种能高效表示较长文本段落（如对话中的单个轮次）的可解释性方法。

Method: 提出turn-averaged SAE，不针对单个token激活，而是学习重构模型在一个人机对话轮次（Human或Assistant轮次）中的平均激活值，每个轮次用固定数量的特征表示。

Result: 1) 轮次平均特征比逐token特征更完整地描述了单个轮次的高层特征（经LLM评判）；2) 轮次平均SAE极大简化了常见的下游应用（如归因图）；3) 使可解释性技术在实际长上下文场景中变得实用。

Conclusion: Turn-averaged SAE通过固定长度的特征表示替代线性增长的逐token表示，有效解决了长上下文可解释性分析的扩展性问题，为理解长文本模型行为提供了更实用的工具。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a useful tool for extracting interpretable features in language models. However, standard SAE architectures operate on individual token activations, meaning that the number of active features scales linearly with context length, and studying long model transcripts becomes difficult. We introduce turn-averaged SAEs, which represent a single Human or Assistant turn with a fixed number of features by learning to reconstruct the average model activation across the turn. We find that turn-averaged features describe a single turn's high-level characteristics more completely than per-token features when judged by an LLM. We also demonstrate that turn-averaged SAEs greatly simplify common downstream uses of SAEs like attribution graphs. Broadly, turn-averaged SAEs make interpretability techniques practical at long context lengths.

</details>


### [64] [Depth-Staggered Fibonacci Spacing for Sparse Attention: Static Schedules Beat Learned Dilation and Extrapolate Where Dense Attention Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28560)
*Chad A. Capps*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了Fibonacii间隔的稀疏自注意力机制，通过每层标量α控制间隔伸缩，在21个60M参数语言模型上比较了四种α设置策略。主要发现：静态逐层交错策略优于固定和可学习α，且与基数无关；可学习α效果不显著且推理延迟高；所有稀疏变体可外推到4倍训练长度而无性能下降，而稠密基线在4倍长度时困惑度飙升201%。但最佳稀疏模型在训练长度上困惑度比稠密基线高26%，且交错增益均匀分布而非集中于长距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索稀疏自注意力机制中不同α设置策略（固定、可学习、静态交错、互质重分配）对语言模型性能和外推能力的影响，寻求在保持长序列外推能力的同时减少计算开销。

Method: 在21个60M参数、512隐藏单元、16层、426M tokens训练的语言模型上，比较四种α设置方式：固定值、每层可学习、静态线性交错、互质（抗网格化）重分配，以及一个可达匹配的2的幂次基线。每个查询关注稠密局部窗口加一组Fibonacci间隔偏移。

Result: 静态逐层交错优于固定和可学习α且基数无关；可学习α未超越静态调度且推理延迟高约5倍；所有稀疏变体可外推到4倍训练长度，稠密基线在4倍长度时困惑度上升201%；最佳稀疏模型在训练长度上困惑度比稠密高26%；交错增益均匀分布而非集中于长距离。

Conclusion: 静态逐层交错是Fibonacci间隔稀疏注意力的有效策略，可学习参数在此场景下冗余；稀疏注意力在长序列外推上具有显著优势，但以训练长度困惑度为代价；交错策略的增益机制需要进一步研究。

Abstract: We study sparse self-attention in which each query attends to a dense local window plus a set of Fibonacci-spaced offsets, with a per-layer scalar alpha that compresses or expands the spacing. Across 21 language models trained under one matched recipe (60M parameters, 512 hidden, 16 layers, 426M tokens), we compare four ways of setting alpha across depth: fixed, per-layer learned, a static linear stagger, and a coprime (anti-gridding) reassignment of that stagger, together with a reach-matched power-of-2 control. Three results stand out. First, a static per-layer stagger improves perplexity over both fixed and learned alpha, and the gain is base-agnostic: applying the same stagger to a power-of-2 base lifts it above fixed Fibonacci and to parity with learned Fibonacci attention. Second, learning per layer is inert: it does not beat the static schedule and costs roughly five times the inference latency. Third, and most consequential, all sparse variants extrapolate to four times their training length with little or no degradation, whereas a recipe-matched dense baseline collapses (perplexity rises by 201% at 4x length); we attribute this to fixed-offset attention only ever querying relative positions seen during training. We also report two honest negatives: at training length the best sparse model has about 26% higher perplexity than the dense baseline, and the staggering gain is uniform across context positions rather than concentrated at long range.

</details>


### [65] [SEAD: Competence-Aware On-Policy Distillation via Entropy-Guided Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28562)
*Chia-Hsuan Lee,Zelei Cheng,Yu Wang,Renkun Ni,Sambit Sahu,Shi-Xiong Zhang,William Campbell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对同策略蒸馏（OPD）中教师监督质量随学生能力变化导致的训练浪费问题，提出SEAD方法，利用熵作为统一探针，在三尺度（token、训练阶段、提示词）上实现自适应蒸馏，在OLMo-3模型上相比OPD平均准确率提升4.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）中教师监督质量随学生能力退化：不一致的rollout产生噪声梯度，已掌握的token产生冗余梯度，导致token、训练阶段和提示词三尺度上的浪费，而现有方法对所有样本统一监督，无法解决此问题。

Method: 提出SEAD，利用熵作为统一探针检测三尺度上的能力依赖退化：(1) 联合师生熵将token分区，分别施加定制散度或零梯度（约50%跳过的token）；(2) 余弦调度从正向KL退火到反向KL；(3) 能力门控课程式学习，从易到难引入提示词。三个组件协同必要：token选择需连贯rollout（课程），退火需单调改进（也依赖课程）。

Result: 在OLMo-3（7B到32B）上，SEAD在六个数学基准上相比vanilla OPD平均准确率提升+4.8%，消融实验证实组件间存在超加性交互。

Conclusion: SEAD通过熵引导的三尺度自适应蒸馏有效解决了OPD中教师监督质量退化的浪费问题，组件间协同作用产生超加性收益。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has a property absent in offline distillation and RL: teacher supervision quality depends on student competence. Incoherent rollouts yield noisy gradients; already-mastered tokens yield redundant ones. This creates waste at three scales (tokens, training phases, and prompts) yet existing methods supervise uniformly. We introduce SEAD, which uses entropy as a unified probe of this competence-dependent degradation at three scales: (1) joint teacher-student entropy partitions tokens into zones receiving tailored divergences or zero gradient (approx. 50% skipped); (2) a cosine schedule anneals from forward to reverse KL as competence grows; (3) a competence-gated curriculum introduces prompts easy-to-hard. These components are symbiotically necessary: token selection requires coherent rollouts (curriculum), annealing requires monotonic improvement (also curriculum). On OLMo-3 (7B to 32B), SEAD achieves +4.8 avg accuracy over vanilla OPD across six math benchmarks, with ablations confirming super-additive interactions.

</details>


### [66] [SocialPersona: Benchmarking Personalized Profiling and Response with Multimodal Social-Media Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26654)
*Qinkai Zhang,Yanyan Zhao,Xin Lu,Yulin Hu,Pengtao Han,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SocialPersona基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型能否从社交媒体时间线中推断用户偏好并在对话中应用。实验表明模型能识别广泛兴趣领域，但在细粒度、近期兴趣及将推断画像用于对话个性化时表现下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化评估多聚焦于记忆维度（模型能否记住用户明确陈述的偏好），缺乏对模型从用户自然留下的多模态痕迹中推断偏好的能力进行评测。本文旨在填补这一空白，推动更全面的个性化能力研究。

Method: 构建SocialPersona基准，包含171名真实社交媒体用户的时间线数据（文本、图像、时间戳）及2,597个人工验证的偏好标签，覆盖7个兴趣领域，区分稳定兴趣与近期兴趣。设计两个任务：从多模态上下文构建结构化用户画像，以及生成与推断画像一致的对话回复。

Result: 闭源和开源MLLM能识别广泛兴趣领域，但在细粒度兴趣和近期兴趣上性能下降；当推断画像需用于对话个性化时，性能进一步退化。文本和图像提供互补的偏好信号。

Conclusion: 跨模态、长时程的用户建模仍是关键挑战，SocialPersona可作为评测基准推动相关研究进展。

Abstract: Personalized language-model assistants are often evaluated through a memory lens: can a model recall preferences users have explicitly stated in dialogue? More comprehensive personalization demands a harder capability -- inferring what users care about from the multimodal traces they naturally leave behind. We introduce SocialPersona, a benchmark for evaluating whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can recover revealed preferences from longitudinal social-media timelines and use them in dialogue. Built from longitudinal timelines of 171 everyday, non-promotional social-media users, SocialPersona contains text, images, timestamps, and 2,597 human-verified preference tags across seven interest domains, separating stable interests from recent interests. It supports two tasks: constructing structured user profiles from multimodal context and generating responses aligned with inferred profiles. Experiments with proprietary and open-weight MLLMs show that models can identify broad interest domains, yet their performance drops on fine-grained and recent interests and degrades further when inferred profiles must be used to personalize dialogue. Together with evidence that text and images provide complementary preference signals, these results indicate that robust cross-modal, long-horizon user modeling remains a key challenge, and that SocialPersona can help measure and advance progress toward assistants that infer and act on revealed preferences.

</details>


### [67] [Correct codes for the wrong reasons? validating LLMs as measurement instruments for theoretical constructs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28574)
*Manuel Pita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLM）作为编码器时，虽然能实现与人类编码者的一致性（可靠性），但无法保证构念效度。本文提出“粒度校准”方法，通过将构念分解为从句级组件、提取性证据测试、理论推导规则组合结果，使编码过程可解释、可验证，将验证从输出比对转向过程与理论一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅关注LLM编码的可靠性（与人类一致），但无法区分真正测量了目标构念与仅通过相关因素达到一致，导致构念效度（construct validity）未被触及。缺乏能辨别LLM编码是否真正遵循理论需求的方法。

Method: 提出“粒度校准”（grain calibration）：(1) 将构念分解为从句级（clause-level）组件；(2) 对每个组件用提取性证据（extractive evidence）测试文本；(3) 通过显式的、理论推导的规则组合各组件结果。规则结构本身作为过程证据，而非仅输出。

Result: 该方法能显示哪些组件决定了编码结果；当编码错误时，可识别是遗漏了某个组件还是混淆了相邻构念。验证从将输出与标注者评分比对，转向证明工具运行在理论指定的构念上。

Conclusion: 粒度校准通过使编码过程透明化、理论化，填补了LLM编码中可靠性高但构念效度无法检验的空白，将验证范式从输出一致性转向过程与理论一致性。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) codes a construct in text as a human annotator would, that agreement makes the LLM a reliable coder. Yet reliability leaves construct validity untouched. The instrument may be theory-naive, reaching the code through a correlate that meets none of the demands the construct's theory makes, and no current method tells that apart from genuine measurement. We propose grain calibration as a method that closes the gap. It decomposes a construct into clause-level components, tests each against the text with extractive evidence, and combines the results through an explicit, theory-derived rule. Because the rule is stated rather than lodged in one opaque pass, its structure is evidence about the process rather than the output. It shows which components settled a code, and, when the code is wrong, whether a component was missed or an adjacent construct mistaken for it. Validation shifts from scoring an instrument's outputs against an annotator to showing that the instrument runs on the construct its theory specifies.

</details>


### [68] [Phonological Perception of Sign Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28667)
*Kayo Yin,Jessica Carter,Alex Xijie Lu,Annemarie Kocab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了手语识别模型对音位特征的感知能力，发现基于姿态的模型对手形敏感，基于像素的模型对位置敏感，且姿态模型的潜在表征与人类感知判断相关（r~0.49），表明模型虽具备涌现音位敏感性，但受限于架构归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习手语识别模型在翻译基准上表现出色，但尚不清楚这些模型是否真正区分抽象的音位特征，还是仅依赖低级统计相关性。因此，本文旨在探究SLR模型对音位结构的感知能力。

Method: 使用最小对（minimal pairs）探测SLR模型对手形、位置、运动等音位参数的敏感性，并通过表征对齐分析（representational alignment）比较模型潜在表征与人类行为判断数据。

Result: 1) SLR模型表现出涌现音位敏感性，但存在架构权衡：姿态模型对手形敏感，像素模型更好捕捉位置变化。2) 姿态模型的潜在表征与人类感知相似性判断相关（r~0.49）。

Conclusion: SLR模型虽能涌现出一定音位感知能力，但当前训练范式不足以使其超越架构固有的归纳偏置，提示需要更针对性的训练策略来提升模型对手语音位结构的全面理解。

Abstract: Sign languages are compositional systems where meaning arises by combining sublexical phonological parameters, such as handshape, location, and movement. While deep learning models for Sign Language Recognition (SLR) have achieved increased performance on translation benchmarks, it remains unclear whether these models distinguish abstract phonological features or merely rely on low-level statistical correlations. This work evaluates the phonological perception of SLR models trained on American Sign Language (ASL) by probing phonological sensitivity using minimal pairs and evaluating representational alignment with human behavioral data. Our results reveal that SLR models exhibit emergent phonological sensitivity, but with clear architectural trade-offs: pose-based models are sensitive to handshape contrasts, while pixel-based models better capture location changes. Furthermore, pose-based models learn latent representations that correlate with human perceptual similarity judgments (r~0.49). These findings suggest that while SLR models exhibit emergent phonology, current training paradigms are insufficient to scale them beyond their architectural inductive biases.

</details>


### [69] [AnTenA: Actionable and Explainable Tensor Analysis System with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28708)
*Dawon Ahn,Auder Der,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AnTenA方法，利用大语言模型（LLMs）的知识来解释多维数据中隐藏的模式，通过任务无关和任务相关的提示来解释张量分解提取的共聚类潜在模式，并在前向和反向推理任务上评估解释效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖标签或辅助元数据来解释多维度数据中的隐藏模式，但这些标签和辅助数据可能存在不准确（如不一致、非标准化）、不充分（如静态表格元数据无法描述时间相关记录）或不可用的问题。

Method: 提出AnTenA方法，利用大语言模型（LLMs）的知识，通过任务无关和任务相关的提示（prompts）来解释从张量分解（tensor decomposition）中提取的共聚类（co-clustered）潜在模式。

Result: 通过前向推理和反向推理任务来评估LLMs对解释效果的准确性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在缺乏准确标签或辅助元数据的情况下，有效利用LLMs的知识来解释人类叙事中的隐藏模式。

Abstract: Accurately explaining hidden patterns in multi-aspect data has typically been done by leveraging labels and/or accompanying auxiliary metadata. However, labels and auxiliary data may be inaccurate (e.g. nonstandard, inconsistent), insufficient (e.g. static tabular metadata for time-dependent recordings), or unavailable. %
We propose \fullmethod (\method), which leverages the knowledge of large language models (LLMs) to explain the hidden patterns in human narratives. \method uses task-agnostic and task-specific prompts to explain extracted co-clustered latent patterns from tensor decomposition. To evaluate these explanations, we test the LLMs on forward and backward inference tasks. % Our demo system is available at https://github.com/dawonahn/ECML_PKDD_AnTenA.

</details>


### [70] [SEATauBench: Adapting Tool-Agent-User Evaluation Into Low-Resource Southeast Asian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28715)
*My Chiffon Nguyen,Aulia Adila,Saksorn Ruangtanusak,Kittiphat Leesombatwathana,Vissuta Gunawan Lim,Patomporn Payoungkhamdee,Samuel Cahyawijaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SEATauBench，首个面向东南亚主权AI的智能体评估框架，覆盖五种语言，通过渐进式本地化设置评估智能体性能。研究发现英文智能体能力在仅改变对话语言时迁移尚可，但随着任务上下文本地化程度加深，质量和鲁棒性急剧下降，揭示了纯英文评估的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 东南亚AI发展和评估虽快速推进，但区域语言中的智能体能力仍鲜为人知，这对主权AI至关重要，现有评估缺乏针对性框架。

Method: 基于TauBench进行适配，覆盖五种语言（普通话、越南语、泰语、印尼语、菲律宾语），设计渐进式本地化设置（从仅改变对话语言到完全本地化任务域），评估三个最新模型。

Result: 英文智能体能力在仅改变对话语言时迁移良好；但随着更多任务上下文本地化，质量和鲁棒性急剧下降；完全域适配时损失最大。

Conclusion: 纯英文智能体评估无法有效衡量东南亚语言中的智能体能力；SEATauBench提供了诊断性基准和可复用的适配流程，有助于构建面向语言多样性地区的可靠多语言智能体。

Abstract: While AI development and evaluation for Southeast Asia (SEA) has grown rapidly, agent capabilities in regional languages are still poorly understood despite its importance to sovereign AI. To fill this gap, we introduce SEATauBench, the first agent-focused evaluation framework for SEA sovereign AI. SeaTau adapts TauBench to five languages -- Mandarin, Vietnamese, Thai, Indonesian, and Filipino -- and evaluates agents across progressively localized settings that vary the language of user-agent interaction, tool specifications, and task domains. Across three recent models, we find that English agent capabilities transfer reasonably well when only the conversation language changes, but quality and robustness degrade sharply as more task contexts are localized, with the largest losses in full domain adaptation. We also the limits of English-only agent assessment for measuring agent capabilities in SEA languages. More broadly, SeaTau provides a diagnostic benchmark and reusable adaptation pipeline for building reliable multilingual agents for linguistically diverse regions. Data and code can be accessed at github.com/SEACrowd/SEATauBench.

</details>


### [71] [DriftGuard: Safety-Aware Multi-Monitor Detection and Selective Adaptation for Evolving Toxicity Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28725)
*Yuting Xin,Hanyu Cai,Binqi Shen,Lier Jin,Lan Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DriftGuard是一个安全感知的自适应审核框架，通过多监测器漂移检测（全局文本漂移、身份伤害漂移、模型不确定性、毒性风险漂移、假阴性风险漂移）和硬混合适配选择性模型更新，在动态在线环境中实现更鲁棒的毒性内容审核。实验表明该方法能捕获全局漂移遗漏的安全风险，显著提升毒性召回率和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的毒性审核系统在动态在线环境中面临有害行为通过编码语言、目标转移和策略性适应来规避检测的问题。传统漂移检测方法仅关注全局分布变化，忽略了在局部伤害子空间或高风险模型错误区域中出现的安全相关漂移，导致安全相关风险被遗漏。

Method: 提出DriftGuard框架，包含：(1) 多监测器漂移检测，同时追踪全局文本漂移、身份伤害漂移、模型不确定性、毒性风险漂移和假阴性风险漂移；(2) 安全相关漂移被检测到时，采用硬混合适配集（hard-mix adaptation set）进行选择性模型更新，该集合优先选择可能假阴性、身份相关高风险示例、假阳性风险示例和不确定边界案例。

Result: 在Civil Comments时序漂移和Jigsaw-to-DynaHate跨数据集漂移实验中，安全感知监测器捕获了全局漂移遗漏的风险。硬混合适配相比无更新和随机平衡基线，显著提升毒性召回率和准确率：Civil Comments上毒性召回率达到0.8777，DynaHate上从0.7107提升至0.8523。Bootstrap分析显示DynaHate安全性增益稳定，毒性召回率提升0.1418，假阴性流行率降低0.0781。

Conclusion: DriftGuard通过将安全感知的漂移检测与有针对性的轻量级模型更新相结合，实现了更鲁棒的自适应毒性审核，证明了在动态在线环境中关注安全相关局部漂移的重要性。

Abstract: Automated toxicity moderation systems operate in dynamic online environments where harmful behavior evolves through coded language, shifting targets, and strategic adaptation to enforcement. Existing drift detection methods often focus on global distributional change, but such signals may miss safety-relevant shifts that emerge in localized harm subspaces or high-risk model-error regions. This paper introduces DriftGuard, a safety-aware adaptive moderation framework that combines multi-monitor drift detection with selective model updating. The framework tracks global text drift, identity-harm drift, model uncertainty, toxic-risk drift, and false-negative-risk drift. When safety-relevant change is detected, the model is updated using a hard-mix adaptation set that prioritizes likely false negatives, identity-related high-risk examples, false-positive-risk examples, and uncertain boundary cases. Experiments on Civil Comments temporal shift and Jigsaw-to-DynaHate cross-dataset shift show that safety-aware monitors detect risks missed by global drift alone. Hard-mix adaptation improves toxic recall and accuracy over no-update and random-balanced baselines, raising toxic recall to 0.8777 on Civil Comments and from 0.7107 to 0.8523 on DynaHate. Bootstrap analysis further shows stable DynaHate safety gains, with toxic recall increasing by 0.1418 and false-negative prevalence decreasing by 0.0781. Overall, DriftGuard links safety-aware drift detection to targeted, lightweight model updating for more robust adaptive toxicity moderation.

</details>


### [72] [5ting at SemEval-2026 Task 8: Strong End-to-End Multi-Turn RAG via LLM-Based Reranking and Faithfulness Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28737)
*Thien-Qua-T-Nguyen,Chi Hoang,Nguyen Tran,Tri Le,Khanh Truong,Chinh Trong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出5ting系统参与SemEval2026 Task 8 (MTRAGEval)，该系统结合BGE-M3稠密检索、FAISS索引、双查询合并检索、LLM重排序及角色分离生成，在多轮RAG任务中取得nDCG@5=0.4719（检索）和RL_F=0.7692（端到端）的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮RAG面临上下文漂移、查询欠指定和幻觉风险等挑战，现有方法难以有效应对，亟需一个专门针对多轮场景设计的端到端系统。

Method: 采用BGE-M3稠密检索模型结合FAISS索引进行向量检索，使用双查询合并策略增强检索覆盖度，通过LLM进行重排序优化，最后采用角色分离的生成方式，限制生成内容仅基于检索到的证据。

Result: 检索任务A上nDCG@5=0.4719；端到端任务C上调和得分为0.5597，RL_F=0.7692。

Conclusion: 5ting系统通过多组件协同（稠密检索+双查询合并+LLM重排序+角色分离生成）有效应对多轮RAG挑战，在SemEval2026评测中取得具有竞争力的性能表现。

Abstract: We introduce 5ting, our system for the SemEval2026 Task 8 (MTRAGEval), which evaluates multi-turn Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) systems. Multi turn RAG involves context drift, under specification, and hallucination risk. Our system combines BGE-M3 dense retrieval with FAISS indexing, dual-query merged retrieval, and LLM based reranking, followed by role separated generation constrained to retrieved evidence. The retriever achieved nDCG@5 = 0.4719 in Task A, while the end to end system ranked in Task C with a harmonic score of 0.5597 and RL_F = 0.7692.

</details>


### [73] [Majority Vote Silences Minority Values: Annotator Disagreement at the Hate/Offensive Boundary in HateXplain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28772)
*Joshua Muhumuza,Joab Ezra Agaba,Mercy Amiyo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有仇恨言论标注流程中，多数投票聚合标注者分歧并非中性行为——42.6%的分歧集中在仇恨/冒犯边界；硬标签和软标签模型在分歧样本上准确率暴跌22个百分点，而标准评估指标无法检测到这种失败；三种下游干预均无法恢复边界准确率，问题根源在于标注设计的结构性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示仇恨言论标注中主流采用的多数投票聚合方法并非中立，它掩盖了标注者在仇恨/冒犯边界上的系统性分歧，导致模型继承虚假的确定性，且标准评估指标无法捕捉由此产生的性能下降。

Method: 基于HateXplain数据集分析标注者分歧分布（卡方检验）；比较硬标签BERT模型（Model A）、软标签模型（Model B）和逐标注者多头模型（Model C）在一致样本与分歧样本上的准确率与置信度差异；尝试三种下游干预措施恢复边界准确率。

Result: 42.6%的分歧集中在仇恨/冒犯边界（p<0.0001）；Model A和Model B在分歧样本上准确率从~80%跌至~58%（p<0.0001）；Model C差距扩大至28个百分点，冒犯分歧样本准确率仅0.245；Model A在边界错误上置信度显著高于Model C（0.710 vs 0.495, p<0.0001）；三种下游干预均失败。

Conclusion: 问题具有结构性：多数投票将争议性判断呈现为ground truth，模型继承了这种虚假确定性；标准评估指标无法检测这种失败；有效的干预必须发生在标注设计的上游阶段，而非训练后补救。
