Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) emerged as a promising distributed machine learning paradigm. However, extending FL to the class incremental learning scenarios introduces unique challenges: 1) Capacity conflict and catastrophic forgetting from the shared model overloading, 2) Heterogeneity from Non-Independent and Identically Distributed (Non-IID) data, and 3) Synchronized class misalignment. In this paper, we propose \textbf{F}isher-Routed \textbf{M}i\textbf{X}ture of Experts for \textbf{Fed}erated Class-Incremental Learning (\textsc{FedFMX}), a novel framework to address these challenges via adaptive expert specialization across clients. The crucial insight is to route each sample to an expert subset that jointly optimizes knowledge acquisition and retention. Specifically, we introduce a Fisher-Routed Expert Scoring (FRES) module to estimate expert importance via Fisher-based stability cost and gradient-based plasticity gain. Then, we design an Adaptive Expert Selection (AES) module by quantifying marginal contributions for adaptive expert subset determination. Finally, by the routing-aware regularization (RAR), we achieve load balance and efficient FL training. We theoretically prove the $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1})$ convergence rate. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks compared with state-of-the-art methods demonstrate the superiority of \textsc{FedFMX}.

</details>


### [147] [The Contagion Tensor: A Framework for Measuring Output-Distribution Coupling in Multi-Agent LLM Systems -- and Auditing the Claims It Enables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28839)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Contagion Tensor框架，用于量化LLM输出分布跨模态、智能体和时间步的耦合，并推导出Coupling Amplification Factor (CAF)指标族。通过模拟消融实验和真实API实验验证了框架有效性，发现图像条件在模拟中呈现超线性效应（CAF=1.40）但在消融后变为亚线性（CAF=0.87），GPT-4o-mini真实视觉实验CAF=1.72验证了这一预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏一个量化、可证伪的测量框架来评估LLM输出分布在多模态、多智能体、多时间步场景下的耦合程度，导致无法区分真正的耦合效应与设计伪像。

Method: 提出Contagion Tensor作为统一测量框架，从中导出CAF指标族（比值形式CAF = E[T_condition] / E[T_baseline]，附带bootstrap置信区间）。设计四种CAF变体，在2x2x2分块正交模拟中结合模态消融进行评估，并在DeepSeek-Chat和GPT-4o-mini上进行真实API验证。

Result: 模拟中图像条件CAF=1.40（超线性），消融后降至0.87（亚线性），偏移-0.53，文本条件无影响。真实实验中，统一人格下文本通信CAF≈1.0，多样人格驱动收敛（CAF=0.88）。GPT-4o-mini对比：文本C3的CAF=1.02，真实视觉C5的CAF=1.72 [1.700, 1.733]，增量+0.70。

Conclusion: 贡献在于：(1) 提供了一个使输出分布耦合可量化证伪的测量仪器；(2) 提供了一个可迁移的消融协议，任何模块化多智能体模拟器均可采用以区分真正耦合与设计伪像。

Abstract: We introduce the Contagion Tensor, a measurement framework for quantifying how large language model (LLM) output distributions couple across modalities, agents, and time steps. From the tensor we derive the Coupling Amplification Factor (CAF), a family of ratio-based metrics sharing the form CAF = E[T_condition] / E[T_baseline], providing unitless, baseline-referenced measurement with bootstrap confidence intervals. We instantiate CAF in four variants and evaluate the strongest in a complete 2x2x2 block-orthogonal simulation design with modality-specific ablation. The ablation reveals that an apparent image-condition super-linear effect (CAF = 1.40) collapses to sub-linear (CAF = 0.87) when the image perturbation module is disabled, a shift of -0.53 with zero effect on text conditions. We supplement with real-API experiments across two model families: DeepSeek-Chat (R=30) and GPT-4o-mini (R=15, real vision). Under uniform personas, text-only communication produces CAF approx 1.0 in both models. Diverse personas drive convergence (CAF = 0.88). A within-model comparison on GPT-4o-mini reveals: C3 (text) CAF = 1.02 vs. C5 (real vision, R=30) CAF = 1.72 [1.700, 1.733], delta = +0.70, validating the simulation's super-linear image-condition prediction. Of 11 conditions, 5 have been tested on real APIs and 6 remain unverified. Our contribution is two-layered: (1) a measurement instrument that makes output-distribution coupling quantitatively falsifiable; and (2) a transferable ablation protocol that any modular multi-agent simulator can adopt to distinguish genuine coupling from design artifacts.

</details>


### [148] [Analysis of Adam Algorithms for Stochastic Dynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28879)
*Xin Zheng,Yifei Jin,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了Adam优化算法在时变非平稳随机系统中的通用理论，通过引入新的随机Lyapunov函数和分析非平稳依赖随机矩阵乘积的技术，推导了参数跟踪和输出预测误差边界，为超参数选择提供了理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Adam类算法的理论分析主要针对时不变模型参数，且依赖独立同分布(i.i.d.)数据假设，这在时变和非平稳系统中往往不成立。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，建立Adam在时变非平稳随机系统中的通用理论。

Method: 引入了分析Adam耦合一阶矩和二阶矩递归所导致的非平稳依赖随机矩阵乘积的新技术，构建了融合这两种矩动力学的随机Lyapunov函数，并在允许非平稳依赖数据的随机激励条件下进行理论分析。

Result: 推导了参数跟踪误差和输出预测误差的显式边界，量化了步长、一阶和二阶动量参数、梯度噪声和参数漂移的影响，并通过合成数据和真实数据实验验证了理论和设计指南。

Conclusion: 该理论不仅为Adam在时变非平稳系统中的性能提供了保证，还为超参数选择提供了指导方针，拓展了Adam优化算法的理论适用范围。

Abstract: The adaptive moment estimation algorithm, known as Adam, is widely used in modern machine learning, owing to its low per-iteration complexity and strong empirical performance. Despite its prevalent use, the theoretical foundation of Adam remains largely unexplored for time-varying and nonstationary systems. In fact, the existing theoretical analyses of Adam-type algorithms are primarily concerned with time-invariant model parameters and explicitly or implicitly rely on independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) data assumptions, under which the learning taskcan be formulated as minimizing a fixed expected objective with a static minimizer. However, such assumptions are often violated in time-varying and nonstationary systems, thereby calling for a theoretical investigation beyond the conventional yet idealized i.i.d. setting. The main objective of this paper is to solve this challenging problem by establishing a general theory of Adam for time-varying and nonstationary stochastic systems. We will introduce some new techniques for analyzing the products of nonstationary and dependent random matrices induced by Adam's coupled first- and second-moment recursions, and will construct a new stochastic Lyapunov function that blends these two moment dynamics. Under a stochastic excitation condition that allows nonstationary and dependent data, we will derive both parameter tracking and output prediction error bounds explicitly, quantifying the effects of stepsize, first- and second-momentum parameters, gradient noise and parameter drift. These bounds not only provide guarantees for Adam performance, but also provide guidelines for hyperparameter selection. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world data validate our theory and design guidelines.

</details>


### [149] [An Integrated Machine Learning and Hierarchical Variance Decomposition Pipeline for Student Performance Prediction and Metacognitive Calibration on Multi-Signal Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28881)
*Gurdeep Singh Virdee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一行为预测与校准分析流水线（UBP-CAP），整合LightGBM分类器、校准度量和交叉GLMM，并引入预测-解释散度指数（PEDI），在1195条记录上验证了性能预测、元认知校准与方差分解的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将学生表现预测、校准误差计算和方差分解作为独立流水线处理，缺乏统一解释框架，阻碍了个性化辅导系统的优化。

Method: UBP-CAP包含三个模块：(1) LightGBM + SHAP进行二值正确性预测；(2) ECE、MCE、Brier分数分解评估元认知校准；(3) 交叉广义线性混合效应模型（GLMM）分解校准偏差。同时提出PEDI量化预测与解释特征轮廓之间的结构散度。

Result: 逻辑回归AUC-ROC=0.903优于LightGBM(0.878)；学生朴素ECE(0.109)显著高于模型ECE(0.068)；交叉GLMM的ICC学生=0.123，表明校准是情境性而非倾向性的；PEDIcos=0.081(p=0.327)表明共享行为特征上预测与解释结构对齐。

Conclusion: UBP-CAP框架成功实现了预测与校准的统一分析，揭示了学生元认知校准的系统性偏差及其情境依赖性，PEDI为量化预测与解释一致性提供了新指标。

Abstract: Predicting student performance and characterizing metacognitive calibration are essential for personalization in intelligent tutoring systems. Prior research treats performance prediction, calibration error calculation, and variance decomposition as separate pipelines, preventing unified interpretation. I propose the Unified Behavioral Prediction and Calibration Analysis Pipeline (UBP-CAP), an integrated framework processing student pre-execution behavioral telemetry through three linked modules: (1) a LightGBM classifier with SHAP for binary correctness prediction, (2) formal calibration metrics (ECE, MCE, and Brier score decomposition) to evaluate metacognitive alignment, and (3) a crossed Generalized Linear Mixed-Effects Model (GLMM) for decomposing calibration deviations. I introduce the Predictive-Explanatory Divergence Index (PEDI), which quantifies structural divergence between predictive and explanatory feature profiles. Evaluated on 1,195 interaction records (27 students, 45 tasks), Logistic Regression achieves AUC-ROC = 0.903, outperforming LightGBM (0.878). Student naive ECE (0.109) significantly exceeds model ECE (0.068), confirming systematic miscalibration. The crossed GLMM yields ICCStudent = 0.123, showing calibration is situational rather than dispositional. PEDIcos = 0.081 (p = 0.327) indicates structural alignment between prediction and explanation on shared behavioral features.

</details>


### [150] [MALOQ: Massively Accelerated Learning of Operators for Quantum Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28911)
*Manasa Kaniselvan,Alexander Maeder,Denghui Lu,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Mathieu Luisier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍MALOQ，一个基于SO(2)-等变架构的框架，用于高效预测DFT哈密顿量/密度矩阵，支持从少量原子到10万原子的大规模系统，在Alps超算上实现3000-12000原子的可扩展训练与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML算子模型可以显著降低DFT哈密顿量/密度矩阵的计算成本，从而将电子结构计算扩展到以前不可行的规模，但现有方法在处理大体系、大基组和多元素时面临可扩展性挑战。

Method: 基于SO(2)-等变骨干架构，提供自定义数据处理内核处理高秩哈密顿矩阵数据，并采用可扩展的边级分布式原子图方案。

Result: 在现有最大分子哈密顿数据集上，每轮训练时间比分子级分布式框架减少30%以上；在Alps超算上实现3000-12000原子的可扩展训练（192 GPU）和推理（256 GPU）。

Conclusion: MALOQ实现了对任意大小材料图的高效推理，为大规模电子结构计算提供了可行的ML加速方案。

Abstract: Machine-learned (ML) operator models can be trained to predict density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonian/density matrices at significantly reduced computational cost, thus extending electronic-structure calculations to previously unfeasible scales. Here, we introduce MALOQ (Massively Accelerated Learning of Operators for Quantum Transport), an application built to train on and predict electronic-structure matrices for systems made of few to 100k atoms, described by large basis sets, and covering a wide range of atomic elements. Based on a state-of-the-art, SO(2)-equivariant backbone architecture, MALOQ provides (i) custom data-processing kernels to handle high-rank Hamiltonian matrix data and (ii) a scalable edge-wise distribution of atomic graph(s). Trained on the largest molecular Hamiltonian datasets available today, it reduces time-per-epoch by over 30% compared to a molecule-wise-distributed framework, and enables inference on material graphs of arbitrary size. We demonstrate scalable training and inference for 3,000-12,000 atoms on the Alps supercomputer, up to 192 GPUs and 256 GPUs, respectively.

</details>


### [151] [ML-Powered LDAP Reconnaissance Detection using Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28917)
*Shaefer Drew,Edward Raff,Michael Brautbar,Yaron Zinar,Benjamin Malmberg,Dor Agron,Sagi Sheinfeld,Avraham Kama,Asaf Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对攻击者利用LDAP进行侦察的问题，提出了两种机器学习框架：一是基于弱监督的恶意LDAP查询分类器，二是基于统计假设检验的恶意LDAP签名挖掘方法。前者通过关联LDAP查询与端点检测实现大规模自动标注和分类，后者在前者基础上挖掘新型恶意签名。分类器在保留集上达到65%真阳性率，挖掘的签名在CrowdStrike的MDR团队中达到81.48%字段精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDAP默认允许所有用户读取Active Directory数据，攻击者入侵后常利用LDAP进行侦察。为了在侦察阶段早期捕获威胁行为者，需要自动化的检测方法。

Method: 提出两种ML框架：(1)弱监督分类器——通过关联LDAP查询与端点检测结果来自动标注大规模数据集，训练ML模型区分恶意/良性LDAP查询；(2)基于统计假设检验的签名挖掘方法——在前者基础上从恶意查询中提取可部署的检测签名。

Result: 分类器在保留集上达到65%真阳性率（同时控制误报）；挖掘的签名在CrowdStrike的MDR团队评估中达到81.48%字段精度。

Conclusion: 弱监督方法虽不及人工标注精确，但利用大规模自动化语料构建降低了成本和耗时，更具实用性。两种方法均达到可部署的性能基准，可用于早期LDAP侦察检测。

Abstract: Lightweight Directory Access Protocol (LDAP) is a protocol that allows users to query and modify Active Directory (AD) data. By default, all users have read access to all AD data through LDAP, making it a common initial tool for reconnaissance when a threat actor first compromises an identity. To capture threat actors early in the reconnaissance phase, we developed two machine learning frameworks to detect LDAP reconnaissance: an ML classifier to predict malicious LDAP queries and an ML-based data-mining method to extract malicious query signatures. By correlating LDAP queries with endpoint detections, the first framework uses weak supervision to label a massive dataset and classify LDAP queries as malicious or benign. For immediate deployment, a second technique was developed on top of this approach to employ a rigorous statistical hypothesis-testing framework for mining novel, malicious LDAP signatures. While this weakly supervised approach is limited compared with manual human labeling, it is more practical for this use case because it leverages large-scale automated corpus construction, reducing costs and time. Ultimately, both the LDAP classifier and the ML-based LDAP signature mining method achieved performance benchmarks, with the classifier achieving up to a 65\% True Positive Rate (TPR) on the holdout set while limiting false positives, and mined signatures demonstrating 81.48\% field precision with CrowdStrike's Managed Detection and Response team.

</details>


### [152] [Towards Improved Anomaly Detection for Cloud Cybersecurity via Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28923)
*Manu Nandan,TJ Jaymes,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络的自监督学习方法，应用于AWS CloudTrail日志以检测云安全威胁，在五个组织中实验表明该方法将警报量从每小时数千条降至约1条，但存在无法估计假阴性率的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着企业对云基础设施依赖的增加，安全威胁检测变得必要；传统基于启发式或异常检测的自动化方法因静态特性导致高误报率，亟需更高效、自适应的检测方法。

Method: 采用图神经网络的自监督学习方法，对AWS CloudTrail日志进行处理，为每个事件生成异常分数，无需周期性重训练即可动态适应组织变化。

Result: 在五个组织的实验中，所提模型在几乎所有情况下产生的警报量远少于基于领域专家规则的基线方法，将警报量从传统方法的每小时数千条降至约1条。

Conclusion: 该方法在实际部署中能够显著降低警报量，但评估仅覆盖被标记事件，无法估计假阴性率，因此应视为一项实际部署研究而非完全验证的检测系统；未来工作需扩展到其他云环境并解决当前局限性。

Abstract: Detecting security threats in an organization's cloud computing environment has become necessary due to the increased reliance on cloud infrastructure. Logging of all cloud computing events enables investigation into any incidents after they are detected. Automated detection of threats using the logs based on heuristics or anomaly detection could result in a high false positive rate due to its relatively static nature. In this article, we present an industrial case study of a self-supervised learning method using graph neural networks applied to AWS CloudTrail logs to surface suspicious events for analyst review. The model produces an anomaly score for each event and dynamically adapts to changes in the organization without requiring periodic retraining. Based on our experiments across five organizations, the proposed model produced substantially fewer alerts than a domain expert rule-based baseline in almost all cases, reducing alert volumes to approximately 1 per hour from thousands generated by traditional methods. We note that this evaluation covers only flagged events, and false negatives cannot be estimated from the current data; findings should therefore be interpreted as a practical deployment study offering insights into real-world constraints rather than a fully validated detection system. We discuss these limitations and the requirements for extending the approach to other cloud environments as future work.

</details>


### [153] [Multi-Agent Routing as Set-Valued Prediction: A WildChat Benchmark and Cost-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28925)
*Ananto Nayan Bala,Faisal Muhammad Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于WildChat的3,000条提示词的多智能体路由基准测试，包含12个固定智能体目录、AI辅助启发式标签和重平衡处理。评估体系涵盖集合级指标（精确率、召回率、F1、Jaccard、完全匹配）、延迟、执行导向的能力覆盖模拟以及基于成本分层的约束路由设置。实验结果表明，监督式路由方法显著优于最近邻和零样本LLM路由，其中微调编码器在无约束条件下表现最佳，线性多标签模型提供了最强实用基线，而加权路由层（WAR）在约束条件下进一步提升了效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言提示到工具和智能体的路由本质上是一个集合预测问题：单个查询可能需要多个智能体，而过选会增加执行成本。现有方法缺乏对这一问题的系统性基准测试和评估协议。

Method: 从WildChat衍生出3,000条提示词，覆盖12个智能体目录，采用AI辅助启发式标注和重平衡处理。评估协议包含集合级指标、延迟、能力覆盖模拟和基于成本分层的约束路由。比较方法包括最近邻匹配、线性多标签分类、依赖感知基线、微调编码器、确定性加权后评分（WAR）和零样本LLM基线。

Result: 监督式路由方法显著优于最近邻和零样本LLM路由。微调编码器在无约束集合准确率上最强，线性多标签模型提供最强的实用基线。在约束设置下，加权路由层在强监督评分器上应用时能提升效用，Encoder+WAR增益最大。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基准和评估协议支持对固定目录多智能体路由中准确率-成本权衡的可重复研究。

Abstract: Tool and agent routing from natural-language prompts is naturally a set-valued prediction problem: a single query may require multiple agents, while over-selection increases execution cost. The benchmark introduced here is derived from WildChat and contains 3,000 prompts over a fixed 12-agent catalog, with AI-assisted heuristic labels under a fixed schema and controlled rebalancing for multi-label evaluation. The evaluation protocol combines set-level metrics (Precision, Recall, F1, Jaccard, and Exact Match), latency, an execution-oriented capability-coverage simulation, and a constrained weighted-routing setting based on ordinal agent-cost tiers. Compared methods include nearest-neighbor matching, linear multilabel classification, dependency-aware baselines, a fine-tuned encoder, deterministic weighted post-scoring via Weighted Agent Routing (WAR), and a zero-shot LLM baseline. Results show that supervised routers substantially outperform nearest-neighbor and zero-shot LLM routing. The fine-tuned encoder achieves the strongest unconstrained set accuracy, while the linear multilabel model provides the strongest practical baseline. In the constrained setting, the weighted routing layer improves utility when applied on top of strong supervised scorers, with the largest gain observed for Encoder+WAR. Overall, the benchmark and evaluation protocol support reproducible study of accuracy-cost trade-offs in fixed-catalog multi-agent routing.

</details>


### [154] [DLR: Zero-Inference-Cost Latent Residuals for Low-Rank Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28932)
*Dong Wang,Wenwu Tang,Yun Cheng,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Duplicated Latent Residual (DLR)方法，通过在低秩预训练中插入无参数、可折叠的结构化残差模块，在不增加部署成本的前提下提升低秩预训练质量。实验表明，DLR在60M至7B参数规模的LLaMA模型上改善了C4验证困惑度，且折叠后的检查点可直接迁移至监督微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型预训练成本过高，低秩预训练虽能减少参数和FLOPs但质量通常落后于满秩训练，亟需一种能提升低秩预训练质量且不增加部署开销的方法。

Method: 提出DLR（Duplicated Latent Residual）模块，在低秩输出Bz上添加固定结构化残差α/√K·Expand_K(z)，将每个潜在坐标复制K=ceil(d_out/r)次到输出中。α固定，每层无额外可学习参数；训练后可通过闭式吸收到上投影矩阵B中（B* = B + α/√K R^T），部署时参数、FLOPs和内存与原始低秩骨干完全一致。

Result: 在60M至7B参数的LLaMA模型上，DLR在大多数设置下改善了低秩预训练的C4验证困惑度，在130M及以上规模时增益最为明显；折叠后的检查点可干净地迁移至标准监督微调基准。

Conclusion: DLR是一种训练专用、无参数、可折叠的插件，能有效增强低秩预训练，在部署时零额外开销，且与微调兼容。

Abstract: Large language models have driven recent progress in language and multimodal AI, yet pre-training them at scale is prohibitively expensive. Low-rank pre-training, which factorizes each weight matrix into a rank-r product to reduce both parameters and FLOPs, is a promising response but typically lags full-rank training in quality. We propose Duplicated Latent Residual (DLR), a training-only, parameter-free, foldable plug-in for low-rank pre-training. DLR augments the standard low-rank output Bz with a fixed structured residual alpha/sqrt(K) * Expand_K(z) that replicates each latent coordinate K = ceil(d_out/r) times across the output. With alpha fixed, DLR adds zero learnable parameters per layer; after training, it is absorbed into the up-projection in closed form, B* = B + alpha/sqrt(K) R^T, so deployment parameter count, FLOPs and memory match the underlying low-rank backbone exactly. Across LLaMA models from 60M to 7B parameters, DLR strengthens low-rank pre-training on C4 validation perplexity in most settings, with the clearest gains at 130M and above; folded checkpoints transfer cleanly to supervised fine-tuning on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [ReGuide: From Test-Time Guidance to Self-Improving Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28939)
*Tzu-Hsiang Lin,Srinivas Shakkottai,Dileep Kalathil,P. R. Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReGuide是一个自改进框架，通过将策略引导的 rollout 视为可重用的在线恢复数据来解决行为克隆扩散策略中的协变量偏移问题。它使用相位条件引导(PCG)生成纠正性轨迹，并通过微调(ReGuide-FT)或从头训练(ReGuide-FS)将成功轨迹吸收回策略中。在Robomimic多项任务上，ReGuide将基础策略成功率提升了1.3-7.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆扩散策略在测试时容易因协变量偏移导致任务失败。现有方法要么通过扩展训练分布来缓解（成本高或依赖假设），要么在测试时用学习模型引导策略但丢弃了纠正后的轨迹，缺乏数据再利用机制。

Method: 提出ReGuide框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 相位条件引导(PCG)，通过构建相位特定的潜在目标，仅在漂移但可恢复区域施加引导，并通过估计的干净动作匹配动力学模型的训练分布来生成纠正性rollout；(2) 将成功的引导rollout通过微调(ReGuide-FT)或从头训练(ReGuide-FS)吸收回策略中，两种方式可组合迭代。

Result: 在Robomimic的Can、Square、Transport和Tool Hang任务上，ReGuide将基础策略成功率提升1.3-7.7倍，在仅测试时引导的设置下优于LPB，消融实验表明性能提升来自引导恢复数据而非仅额外rollout。

Conclusion: ReGuide通过将引导产生的纠正轨迹作为可复用的在线数据回注到策略训练中，实现了自改进闭环，有效缓解了行为克隆扩散策略的协变量偏移问题，且效率优于已有方法。

Abstract: Behavior-cloned diffusion policies are expressive but remain vulnerable to covariate shift: small deviations from demonstrated states can compound into task failure. Existing methods address this either by expanding the training distribution through expert corrections or synthetic augmentation, or by steering a frozen policy at test time with guidance from a learned model. The former can be expensive or assumption-dependent, while the latter discards the corrected trajectories after execution. We introduce ReGuide, a self-improving framework that treats guided rollouts as reusable on-policy recovery data. ReGuide first uses Phase-Conditioned Guidance (PCG) to generate corrective rollouts: it constructs phase-specific latent targets, applies guidance only in the drifted-but-recoverable regime, and guides through the estimated clean action to match the dynamics model's training distribution. Successful guided rollouts are then absorbed back into the policy through ReGuide-FT, which fine-tunes the current checkpoint, or ReGuide-FS, which retrains from scratch on the augmented dataset; the two can also be composed and iterated. On Robomimic Can, Square, Transport, and Tool Hang, ReGuide improves base-policy success by $1.3$--$7.7\times$, outperforms LPB in the test-time-only setting, and matched-data ablations show that the gains come from guided recovery data rather than additional rollouts alone.

</details>


### [156] [Machine-learnable Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28947)
*Veit Elser,Manish Krishan Lal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文正式定义了具有易识别、易生成且易从样例学习这三个特性的大规模离散集合，将其特化为二进制字符串集合，并基于有界复杂度布尔自编码器定义了“机器可学习性”。通过布尔阈值函数网络实现自编码器进行实验，证明了Rorschach模式及更“狂野”集合的机器可学习性，并展示了一种通过迭代将狂野集合演化为可学习集合的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为大规模离散集合提出形式化定义，使其同时具备易识别、易生成和易从样例学习三个特性，并建立机器可学习性的理论框架。

Method: 将形式化框架特化为二进制字符串集合，基于有界复杂度的布尔自编码器定义机器可学习性；使用布尔阈值函数网络实现自编码器；对Rorschach模式（镜像半区可反转对比度）和更狂野的集合进行实验；提出迭代方法将非可学习集合演化为可学习集合。

Result: 证明了Rorschach模式集合的机器可学习性；展示了更狂野集合的元素仅能被自编码器近似固定；提出并验证了通过简单迭代将狂野集合演化为真正机器可学习集合的方法。

Conclusion: 布尔自编码器框架能有效定义和实现离散集合的机器可学习性，且可通过迭代过程将原本不满足条件的集合转化为可学习形式。

Abstract: In this study we present a formal definition of large discrete sets having, informally, three properties: their elements are easily recognized, easily generated, and the latter tasks are easily learned from examples. The formalism is specialized to sets of binary strings and a definition of "machine-learnability" based on the existence of a bounded-complexity Boolean autoencoder that fixes the elements of the set. We present experiments where the autoencoders are implemented by nets of Boolean threshold functions. Machine-learnability is demonstrated for Rorschach patterns (that may have reversed contrast in the mirrored half), and considerably "wilder" sets whose elements are only approximately fixed by admissible autoencoders. In the second case we demonstrate a simple iteration that evolves wild sets to make them properly machine-learnable.

</details>


### [157] [Modification-Considering Value Learning for Reward Hacking Mitigation in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28955)
*Evgenii Opryshko,Umangi Jain,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCVL是一种通过对比是否包含新经验的两条训练路径的分数来决定是否采纳该经验的价值学习方法，能够在不阻碍正常改进的前提下缓解奖励破解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体可能利用错误指定的奖励信号实现高表观回报而实际未能完成目标（奖励破解）。现有防御方法（如约束策略靠近安全参考）在抑制破解和允许正常改进之间存在矛盾。

Method: MCVL包装离线学习器，将每个输入经验视为候选修改：预测两条训练路径（包含该经验 vs 不包含），使用冻结的引导回报估计器（由学习的奖励模型和价值函数构成）对两条路径评分，仅当包含经验不降低分数时才采纳该经验。

Result: 在四个安全相关网格世界和三个修改版MuJoCo连续控制任务中，MCVL缓解了奖励破解问题，同时继续改进目标任务。

Conclusion: 形式化了该过滤方法安全且宽松的条件，并在DDQN和TD3上实例化MCVL，验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents can exploit misspecified reward signals to achieve high apparent returns while failing on the intended objective, a failure mode known as reward hacking. Existing practical defenses typically constrain policy updates to stay near a known safe reference, creating a tension between suppressing hacking and permitting legitimate improvement. We propose Modification-Considering Value Learning (MCVL), which operationalizes the theoretical idea of current utility optimization for standard value-based RL. MCVL wraps an off-policy learner and treats each incoming transition as a candidate modification: it forecasts two training paths, one that includes the transition and one that does not, and scores both with a frozen bootstrapped-return estimator derived from a learned reward model and value function. The transition is admitted only if inclusion does not decrease the score. We formalize conditions under which this filtering is both safe and permissive, and instantiate MCVL with DDQN and TD3. Across four safety-relevant gridworlds and three modified MuJoCo continuous-control tasks with diverse hacking mechanisms, MCVL mitigates reward hacking while continuing to improve the intended objective. Project website: ktolnos.github.io/mcvl/.

</details>


### [158] [RGLD: Randomized Global-Local Density Estimation for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28970)
*Quanling Zhao,Jiaying Yang,Ye Tian,Josh Victoria,Zhijun Wang,Pietro Mercati,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RGLD是一种结合全局随机特征密度分支与局部近邻分支的随机全局-局部密度估计器，用于高效无监督表格异常检测。在47个数据集上与23个基线方法比较，RGLD在AUROC上排名第一，AUPRC排名第二，速度比深度检测器快50-580倍，同时保持与统计方法相当的运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督表格异常检测方法存在两难：经典统计检测器效率高但依赖固定数据视图和单一异常定义；深度检测器可学习更灵活的评分函数但速度慢且难以在无监督场景下调参。需要一种兼具精度、鲁棒性和计算效率的方法。

Method: RGLD包含两个分支：(1) 全局随机特征密度分支，利用随机投影识别低密度区域的样本；(2) 局部近邻分支，检测缺乏近邻支持的样本。两个分支均在特征装袋的随机视图上运行，以暴露单一表示中隐藏的异常证据。

Result: 在47个表格数据集上，与23个统计和深度异常检测基线比较，RGLD在数据集级AUROC上排名第一（胜场数最多），AUPRC胜场数排名第二。速度上，RGLD比所有评估的深度检测器快50-580倍，与统计方法运行时间相当。

Conclusion: RGLD通过结合全局密度估计与局部近邻分析，在随机特征装袋视图上实现了准确率与计算效率的有利权衡，为无监督表格异常检测提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised tabular anomaly detection requires methods that are accurate, robust across heterogeneous datasets, and computationally efficient. Classical statistical detectors are often efficient, but they usually rely on a fixed data view and a single notion of abnormality. Deep anomaly detectors can learn more flexible scoring functions, but they are substantially slower and difficult to tune in unsupervised settings due to the lack of a reliable supervisory signal. We propose RGLD, a randomized global-local density estimator for efficient unsupervised tabular anomaly detection. RGLD combines a global random-feature density branch, which identifies samples in broadly low-density regions, with a local neighbor branch, which detects samples that are weakly supported by nearby observations. Both branches operate over feature-bagged randomized views, allowing RGLD to expose anomaly evidence that may be hidden in any single representation. We conduct experiments on 47 tabular datasets against 23 statistical and deep anomaly detection baselines under fully unsupervised setting. RGLD achieves the strongest dataset-level AUROC performance, ranking 1st in dataset wins, and ranks 2nd in AUPRC wins. RGLD is also faster than all evaluated deep detectors, achieving 50x-580x speedups, and remains competitive with statistical methods in runtime, yielding a favorable accuracy-efficiency tradeoff.

</details>


### [159] [On Surrogate Modeling of Static Response of AM Short-Fiber Thermoplastics Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28996)
*Pharindra Pathak,Vipin Kumar,Trenton M. Ricks,Suhasini Gururaja,Siddhartha Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络-长短期记忆（GNN-LSTM）的混合替代框架，用于预测短纤热塑性复合材料（SFT）的力学行为。该框架将微CT重建的微观结构离散为Voronoi单元，通过非线性有限元仿真进行均匀化，训练得到的代理模型能以超过两个数量级的计算加速实现高精度（R²≈0.98）的应力-应变预测，为SFT构件数字孪生开发提供了物理信息驱动的数据高效路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短纤热塑性复合材料（SFT）在轻量化航空航天和汽车结构中应用广泛，但其力学响应受纤维取向、空间聚集和制造孔隙等介观尺度特征的强影响，这些特征存在显著空间变异性。尽管介观有限元模型能够解析此类异质性，但应用于真实三维微结构时计算成本过高，亟需高效且准确的替代模型。

Method: (1) 基于微CT数据重建微观结构，离散为Voronoi单元以表示纤维相互作用邻域；(2) 对每个单元进行考虑基体损伤的非线性有限元仿真均匀化处理；(3) 构建混合GNN-LSTM架构，其中GNN编码微观结构拓扑信息，LSTM刻画历史依赖的力学演化过程；(4) 使用均匀化应力-应变数据训练该架构。

Result: 代理模型对未见微观结构的刚度和应力-应变行为预测精度达到R²≈0.98，相比高保真有限元仿真实现了超过两个数量级的计算成本缩减。耦合实验校准的损伤定律后，揭示了纤维取向、聚集和孔隙率共同主导局部有效刚度。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条物理信息驱动、数据高效的路径，能够识别力学薄弱微结构单元，加速SFT构件的数字孪生开发。

Abstract: Short-fiber thermoplastic (SFT) composites are increasingly employed in lightweight aerospace and automotive structures owing to their favorable strength-to-weight ratio, high production rates, and recyclability. Unlike continuous-fiber systems, the mechanical response of SFTs is governed by mesoscale interactions among fiber orientation, spatial clustering, and manufacturing-induced porosity. These features exhibit significant spatial variability in manufactured components and influence stiffness, damage initiation, and nonlinear deformation. Although mesoscale finite element (FE) models can resolve such heterogeneity, their application to realistic three-dimensional microstructures remains computationally intractable.
  A data-driven surrogate framework is proposed to predict the mechanical behavior of additively manufactured, compression-molded (AM-CM) SFTs. Microstructures reconstructed from micro-computed tomography data were discretized into Voronoi-based cells representing distinct fiber-interaction neighborhoods. Each cell was homogenized via nonlinear FE simulations incorporating matrix damage, and the resulting stress-strain responses trained a hybrid Graph Neural Network-Long Short-Term Memory (GNN-LSTM) architecture encoding microstructural topology and history-dependent mechanical evolution.
  The surrogate accurately predicts stiffness and stress-strain behavior of unseen microstructures, achieving $R^2\approx 0.98$ relative to high-fidelity FE simulations with over two orders-of-magnitude reduction in computational cost. Coupling the framework with experimentally calibrated damage laws demonstrates that fiber orientation, clustering, and porosity collectively govern local effective stiffness. The approach provides a physics-informed, data-efficient pathway to identify mechanically weak microstructural cells and accelerate digital-twin development for SFT components.

</details>


### [160] [How Far Can Sharpness and Complexity Jointly Explain Generalization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29043)
*Ziyu Cheng,Xitong Zhang,Longxiu Huang,Rongrong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究锐度（sharpness）和复杂度（complexity）两个因素联合解释深度神经网络泛化能力的能力，通过线性回归和基于帕累托分析的方法，提出了更接近函数空间的锐度和复杂度定义，发现函数导向的定义比传统参数级指标能更好地扩展解释范围，但仍有失败案例表明双因素视角能否构成完整的泛化理论尚待研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的泛化度量定量评估大多关注单个标量指标，而锐度和复杂度两个因素的联合解释力在很大程度上未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究锐度和复杂度如何共同解释深度神经网络的泛化能力。

Method: 采用线性回归和基于帕累托分析的方法来定量评估锐度和复杂度的联合解释力。同时，提出了更接近函数空间、更少依赖原始参数表示的锐度和复杂度定义，超越了现有的参数级定义。

Result: 函数导向的锐度和复杂度定义扩展了双因素视角的解释范围，超越了现有参数级指标的表现。但仍有失败案例表明该视角尚不能作为完整的泛化理论。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持锐度-复杂度视角作为理解不同设置下泛化能力的信息透镜，但双因素视图能否成为完整的泛化理论仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: Sharpness and complexity are two central factors in the generalization analysis of deep neural networks. Existing quantitative evaluations of generalization measures have largely focused on individual scalar measures, leaving the joint explanatory power of sharpness and complexity largely unexplored. This work studies how far sharpness and complexity can jointly explain generalization. We use linear regression and introduce a Pareto-based analysis to quantitatively evaluate the joint explanatory power of these two factors. Beyond the existing parameter-level definitions, we further propose realizations of sharpness and complexity that are closer to function space and less dependent on raw parameter representations. We find that function-oriented definitions of these two quantities expand the explanatory scope of the two-factor view beyond what is achieved by existing parameter-level metrics. Overall, our results support the sharpness-complexity perspective as an informative lens for understanding generalization across diverse settings. At the same time, the remaining failures indicate that whether this two-factor view can serve as a complete theory of generalization remains open.

</details>


### [161] [MOSAIC: Orchestrating Collaborative Knowledge Tracing with Hierarchical Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29049)
*Xinjin Li,Mengyue Wang,Yuzhen Lin,Pengbin Feng,Ziqi Sha,Yeyang Zhou,Yu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个结合大语言模型语义对齐与序列建模的多粒度知识追踪框架，通过冻结LLM生成动态嵌入和层次化预测提示，并引入跨粒度一致性目标，在多个数据集上取得SOTA结果（AUC提升3.4%，准确率提升2.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识追踪方法存在两个关键局限：一是依赖浅层的ID-based表示，缺乏语义深度；二是局限于单粒度掌握度估计，忽视了层次化的知识依赖关系。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架，核心包括：(1) 利用冻结的LLM生成动态、上下文感知的嵌入和层次化预测提示，捕获协作信号和同伴交互；(2) 引入跨粒度一致性目标，联合规约概念级、主题簇级和全局熟练度三个层次的掌握度估计。

Result: 在ASSISTments、EdNet和新收集的大规模MOOC数据集上，MOSAIC达到SOTA，AUC提升达3.4%，准确率提升达2.5%。在协作密集和长序列场景下表现尤为鲁棒（MOOC上AUC 0.862）。

Conclusion: MOSAIC通过多粒度语义对齐与一致性约束，既提升了预测精度，又提供了语义可解释性，特别适用于协作学习环境。

Abstract: Knowledge Tracing (KT) is important for personalized education but traditionally suffers from two key limitations: a reliance on shallow ID-based representations that neglect semantic depth and a restriction to single-granularity mastery estimation that overlooks hierarchical knowledge dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose MOSAIC (Multi-granularity Online Semantic AI for Collaborative Knowledge), a novel framework that orchestrates LLM-driven semantic alignment with sequential modeling. Unlike methods that use LLMs solely as predictors, MOSAIC leverages a frozen LLM to generate dynamic, context-aware embeddings and hierarchical prediction prompts, explicitly capturing collaborative signals and peer interactions. Furthermore, we introduce a cross-granularity consistency objective that jointly regularizes mastery estimation across concept, topic-cluster, and global proficiency levels. Extensive experiments on ASSISTments, EdNet, and a newly collected large-scale MOOC dataset demonstrate that MOSAIC establishes new state-of-the-art results. Specifically, our method achieves AUC improvements of up to 3.4\% and Accuracy gains of up to 2.5 \% across all benchmarks. Notably, MOSAIC exhibits superior robustness in collaboration-rich environments and long-sequence scenarios (AUC 0.862 on MOOC), offering both high predictive precision and semantically grounded interpretability.

</details>


### [162] [A Kernel Fisher Discriminant Analysis-Based Tree Ensemble Classifier: KFDA Forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29053)
*Donghwan Kim,Seung Hwan Park,Jun-Geol Baek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于核Fisher判别分析的集成分类器KFDA Forest，通过bootstrap和随机特征子集划分提升多样性，利用KFDA进行非线性特征旋转，以决策树为基分类器，在UCI和KEEL数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成分类器通常优于单一分类器，现有方法在非线性数据结构上的分类性能仍有提升空间，因此提出结合KFDA的树集成方法。

Method: 采用bootstrap采样和随机将变量集划分为K个子集以促进多样性；对每个子集执行KFDA，通过核技巧将输入空间映射到核特征空间实现非线性旋转；以决策树作为基分类器进行集成。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL真实数据集上，将KFDA Forest与现有集成方法进行比较，实验结果表明所提方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: KFDA Forest通过结合核特征旋转与树集成，能够有效处理非线性分类问题，是一种性能优越的集成学习方法。

Abstract: In general, an ensemble classifier is more accurate than a single classifier. In this study, we propose an ensemble classifier called the kernel Fisher discriminant analysis forest (KFDA Forest), which is a tree-based ensemble method that applies KFDA. To promote diversity, bootstrap is used, and variable sets are randomly divided into K subsets. KFDA is performed on each subset to increase classification accuracy. KFDA maximizes the distance between classes while minimizing the distance within classes. KFDA can also be applied to classification problems in a nonlinear data structure using the kernel trick because it can transform the input space into a kernel feature space, commonly named a rotation, rather than performing a dimensionality reduction. Because new feature axes and KFDA projections are parallel, decision trees are used as a base classifier. To compare the proposed method with existing ensemble methods, we apply these to real datasets from the UCI and KEEL repositories.

</details>


### [163] [When Can Conformal Risk Control Certify LLM Outputs? Bounds, Impossibility, and Adaptation for Structured Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29054)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在结构化生成任务（NER、JSON提取、QA、分类）中，LLM输出缺乏形式化可靠性保证的问题。作者证明了当基础风险μ > α时，任何无分布方法必须至少放弃((μ-α)/(1-α))个样本，给出了一个闭合形式可行性检验。他们比较了Hoeffding、经验Bernstein和基于赌注的e-CRC三种界，发现Hoeffding到Bernstein步骤获得最大增益（+37%可认证配置），e-CRC在少量校准数据时更有价值。验证了自适应共形推理（ACI）在跨数据集漂移下将风险目标违规从71%降至21%。在3B-72B参数模型、8个数据集、4个任务、6个非一致性分数上，硬NER/QA/CLS配置在α=0.10时不可认证，放宽到α=0.30-0.40可实现实用认证（NER 47%、QA 40%、CLS 60%）。最终给出三步部署方案：检查可行性、选择界和分数、缓解漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在结构化生成任务中缺乏形式化的可靠性保证，现有的启发式弃权策略无法满足用户指定的风险目标，与目标相差7.5-12.5%。作者希望建立何时共形风险控制（CRC）可以认证结构化LLM输出、何时不能的理论基础，并提供实用的部署方案。

Method: 1) 证明了不可能性结果：当基础风险μ > α时，任何无分布方法必须放弃至少((μ-α)/(1-α))个样本，给出闭合形式可行性检验；2) 分析比较了Hoeffding、经验Bernstein和基于赌注的e-CRC三种认证界的层级关系；3) 验证了自适应共形推理（ACI）在跨数据集漂移下的效果；4) 在6个开源模型、8个数据集、4个任务、6个非一致性分数上进行了广泛实验。

Result: Hoeffding到Bernstein步骤获得最大增益（+37%可认证配置）；e-CRC在少量校准数据时更有价值（20%数据下10%认证，而Hoeffding为0%）；ACI将风险目标违规从71%降至21%；在α=0.10时硬NER/QA/CLS配置不可认证，放宽到α=0.30-0.40可实现47% NER、40% QA、60% CLS的实用认证。

Conclusion: 共形风险控制（CRC）可以在特定条件下为LLM结构化输出提供形式化可靠性保证，但存在理论基础上的可行性限制。作者给出了一个三步部署方案：检查可行性、选择界和分数、缓解漂移，为实际部署提供了指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed for structured generation (NER, JSON extraction, QA, and classification) lack formal reliability guarantees, and standard heuristic abstention policies miss user-specified risk targets by 7.5--12.5%. We characterize when conformal risk control (CRC) can certify structured LLM outputs and when it provably cannot. First, we prove an impossibility result: when the base risk (μ> α), any distribution-free method must abstain on at least ((μ-α)/(1-α)) examples, yielding a closed-form feasibility test: one can check whether CRC will work before running it. Second, we analyze a certification hierarchy across Hoeffding, empirical Bernstein, and a betting-based e-CRC bound, with strict gains in low-variance/large-sample regimes: the Hoeffding-to-Bernstein step delivers the largest gain (+37% certified configurations), while e-CRC adds value when calibration data is scarce (10% certification at 20% data versus 0% for Hoeffding). Third, we validate adaptive conformal inference (ACI) under cross-dataset shift, reducing risk-target violations from 71% to 21%, with residual failures concentrated exactly where the impossibility bound predicts. Across six open-weight models (3B--72B parameters), eight datasets, four tasks, and six nonconformity scores, hard NER/QA/CLS configurations are uncertifiable at (α= 0.10); relaxing to (α= 0.30--0.40) unlocks practical certification (47% NER, 40% QA, 60% CLS). The framework gives a three-step deployment recipe: check feasibility, select the bound and score, then mitigate shift.

</details>


### [164] [Statistically Indistinguishable, Operationally Distinct: A Formal Barrier for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29091)
*Tassilo Klein,Johannes Hoffart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"操作图灵测试"(OTT)，证明表格基础模型无法推理由运行系统产生的数据，除非能访问控制这些系统的规则。通过构建法律与违规数据库状态对，使1-2维列值边际匹配，任何仅基于数值的分类器Bayes误差≥0.49，实验验证了这一理论界限，并表明只有可执行规则驱动的审计才能达到完美分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前表格基础模型（如TabICL、TabPFN及大语言模型）在处理由运行系统产生的表格数据时的根本局限：它们无法区分合法与违规状态，因为仅依赖数值模式而非底层操作规则。

Method: 提出"操作图灵测试"(OTT)，构建边际匹配的合法/违规数据库状态对；使用Le Cam引理推导Bayes误差下界；通过XGBoost、TabICL、TabPFN等基线验证；对比LLM（含GPT-5.5）在有/无规则审计下的分类表现；在两种不同模式（银行分类账）上验证结论的泛化性。

Result: (1) 纯数值基线准确率恰好为0.50（TOST p<0.002），达到理论下界；(2) 原始行级访问无帮助；(3) 暴露关系值一致性可缩小差距但仍不完美；(4) 仅七项可执行规则审计的组合达100%准确率；(5) LLM最多正确识别2/50个合法状态，GPT-5.5拒绝所有合法状态；(6) 访问阶梯模式在第二模式上复现。

Conclusion: 表格数据推理的根本障碍是可识别性(identifiability)而非模型容量(capacity)：规模、数据和更丰富特征都无法跨越这一障碍，必须有操作规则（operational grounding）作为支撑。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models cannot reason about data produced by running systems without access to the rules that govern them. We make this statement falsifiable. The \emph{Operational Turing Test} (OTT) constructs pairs of legal and rule-violating database states whose $1$- and $2$-way column-value marginals match to a total variation of $<0.02$; Le~Cam's lemma then bounds any values-only classifier at $\geq0.49$ Bayes error. Three values-only baselines (XGBoost, TabICL, TabPFN) hit the bound exactly (accuracy $0.50$, pre-registered two one-sided tests (TOST) $p<0.002$), raw row-level access does not help, exposing relational value consistency closes most of the gap, and only a classifier fed by seven executable rule-derived audits reaches $1.00$ classification accuracy. In three matched $100$-state frontier large-language-model (LLM) runs, models given the schema, trigger source, rule tables, and state files classify at most $2/50$ legal states as LEGAL; GPT-5.5 accepts $0/50$ legal states even with higher reasoning effort and a Structured Query Language (SQL) executor. The access-ladder pattern also appears on a second schema with structurally distinct rule families (banking ledger: cross-row balance, cumulative aggregate). The barrier is identifiability, not capacity: scale, data, and richer features cannot cross it without operational grounding.

</details>


### [165] [Priced Motion Through Optimal Faces: A Normal-Fan Geometry for Non-Stationary Adversarial MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29092)
*Kai Hidajat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为有限时域对抗性MDP提出了一种法扇几何（normal-fan geometry）框架，将动态遗憾分解为有价的面对运动（face-crossing price）和无价的扇内选择误差，从而区分了有后果和无后果的非平稳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的动态遗憾分析常将非平稳性代价等同于损失移动的幅度，但损失的大幅移动可能不改变最优策略，而微小移动却可能迫使最优策略彻底改变。因此，需要一种更忠实于控制问题的分析工具来区分有害与无害的非平稳性。

Method: 针对固定转移矩阵的有限时域对抗性MDP，利用占据测度构成的多面体（polytope）及其法扇（normal fan）结构：每个损失向量对应多面体的一个最优面。非平稳性被建模为法扇中的一条路径——在同一锥内移动不改变最优面，穿越锥面则产生遗憾。定义了"面对价格"（face-crossing price），即在新损失下仍留在原最优面所需的最小遗憾。动态遗憾可精确分解为有价的面对运动与无价的扇内选择误差。

Result: 损失变化可以任意大但面对价格为零（即无后果的非平稳性）；相同的一维损失变化可能隐藏着horizon尺度的遗憾差异。动态遗憾被严格分解为面对运动代价与扇内选择误差两部分。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了比损失变化或转移变化更忠实于控制问题的非平稳性度量，能够区分有后果和无后果的非平稳性，为对抗性MDP的动态遗憾分析提供了全新的几何视角。

Abstract: In a changing decision problem, standard dynamic-regret analyses have often equated the cost of non-stationarity to how far loss moves. However, it is simultaneously possible for a loss sequence to travel far and retain the same optimal policy, or for a small movement in loss to force the optimal policy to change completely. Thus, the size of the movement through loss variation, transition variation, or comparator path length describe the adversary's motion, but not the cost of that motion to the control problem. For a more faithful analytic interpretation, this paper develops a normal-fan geometry for finite-horizon adversarial MDPs with fixed transitions. Occupancy measures form a polytope, and each loss vector exposes an optimal face of that polytope. Non-stationarity in rewards is therefore a path through the normal fan, where motion inside one cone leaves the optimal face unchanged, while crossing a wall may carry regret. We pose the notion of a face-crossing price, which is the minimum regret incurred by remaining on the previous optimal face under the new loss. For any learner that tracks the previous face, dynamic regret decomposes exactly into intrinsic priced face motion plus within-face selection error. The resulting theory separates consequential from harmless non-stationarity, where loss variation can be arbitrarily large at zero price, and identical one-coordinate variation can hide horizon-scale differences in regret.

</details>


### [166] [DiLaServe: High SLO Attainment Serving for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29094)
*Tzu-Tao Chang,Benjamin Yuanyang Hong,Kiet Pham,Shivaram Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiLaServe是一个面向扩散语言模型(DLM)的集群级服务系统，通过截止时间感知调度、自适应负载控制和质量感知优化，在满足延迟SLO的同时提升推理吞吐量，相比基线方法SLO达标率提升高达56.6个百分点，端到端延迟降低46%，准确率下降不到1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽能通过并行生成多个token提升推理吞吐量，但在实际服务系统中面临三个独特挑战：基于置信度去噪带来的速度-质量权衡、负载波动下模型实例并行化级别的选择、以及近似KV缓存机制引入的非均匀逐步骤成本，现有系统无法有效应对这些挑战。

Method: DiLaServe包含三个核心设计：(1) 截止时间感知调度与自适应负载控制，通过置信度阈值调整来管理速度-质量权衡；(2) 质量感知优化问题求解，动态重配置集群的并行化级别；(3) 显式建模近似KV缓存引入的逐步骤异构性，优化调度决策。

Result: 在多个基准测试和真实世界轨迹上，DiLaServe将SLO达标率提升最多56.6个百分点，端到端请求延迟降低最多46%，同时准确率下降控制在1%以内。

Conclusion: DiLaServe有效地解决了DLM服务系统中的独特挑战，证明了通过系统级的协同优化可以在不显著牺牲质量的前提下大幅提升DLM的服务性能和SLO达成率。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to conventional autoregressive language models. By generating multiple tokens in parallel during each denoising step, they offer higher inference throughput while maintaining competitive quality. However, realizing these throughput gains while meeting latency SLOs in a serving system requires addressing challenges introduced by DLMs' unique characteristics. These include navigating the speed-quality tradeoff created by confidence-based denoising, choosing appropriate parallelization levels across model instances under fluctuating load, and coordinating approximate KV caching mechanisms that introduce non-uniform per-step costs. To address these challenges, we present DiLaServe, a cluster-level serving system for DLMs. DiLaServe enables deadline-aware scheduling and adaptive load control through confidence-threshold adjustment, and dynamically reconfigures the cluster by solving a quality-aware optimization problem, while explicitly modeling the step-level heterogeneity introduced by approximate KV caching. Across multiple benchmarks and real-world traces, DiLaServe improves SLO attainment by up to 56.6 percentage points and reduces end-to-end request latency by up to 46\% while incurring less than 1\% accuracy drop.

</details>


### [167] [Few-Step Boltzmann Generators via Scalable Likelihood Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29110)
*RuiKang OuYang,Hanlin Yu,Xinyue Ai,Yutong He,Nicholas M. Boffi,Pradeep Ravikumar,Jose Miguel Hernandez-Lobato,Max Simchowitz,Benjamin Kurt Miller,Omar Chehab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCALLOP是一种基于流映射的可扩展似然蒸馏方法，通过无Hutchinson估计器的向量化训练目标，在分子科学和图像生成任务上实现更低的方差、更短的训练时间和高达10倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式生成模型在高质量采样方面取得进展，但似然估计仍存在瓶颈：要么依赖限制性架构进行精确计算，要么使用Hutchinson迹估计器等随机近似方法引入显著方差。作者旨在提出一种可扩展、低方差的似然蒸馏方法。

Method: 基于F2D2模型架构，提出SCALLOP（可扩展似然蒸馏）。核心创新是设计了一个无Hutchinson估计器的似然蒸馏目标函数，支持向量化实现，从而替代原有高方差的随机近似方法。

Result: 在分子科学（Boltzmann生成器）和图像数据集上，SCALLOP相比F2D2显著降低了训练方差和训练时间，性能持续提升；与最先进方法相比具有竞争力，同时实现最快基线方法10倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: SCALLOP通过消除Hutchinson估计器带来的方差问题，实现了更高效、更稳定的流式生成模型训练，在似然估计和推理速度方面均取得突破性进展。

Abstract: Recent progress in flow-based generative modeling has led to models that output high-quality samples while using only a small number of function evaluations. However, at present, there is a lack of similar advances in estimating the model likelihood. In particular, most existing methods either rely on restrictive architectures that enable exact calculations, or use stochastic approximations such as Hutchinson's trace estimator that introduce substantial variance. In this work, we introduce SCAlable LikeLihood distillation of flOw maPs (SCALLOP). SCALLOP builds on the recently proposed F2D2, a likelihood flow map model that can generate samples and their densities in a small number of function evaluations. While F2D2 uses Hutchinson's estimator during training, we introduce an alternative and more scalable likelihood distillation objective that is Hutchinson-free and admits a vectorized formulation. Empirically, we demonstrate the effectiveness of SCALLOP as a Boltzmann generator in molecular science, and further validate its benefit on image datasets. SCALLOP significantly reduces both training variance and training time while consistently improving performance compared to F2D2, and is competitive with the state-of-the-art while achieving up to 10x inference speedup over the fastest baseline.

</details>


### [168] [A Novel Latent-Class Attack and its Detection by Class Subspace Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29112)
*Guangmingmei Yang,David J. Miller,George Kesidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“潜在类别攻击”的新型数据投毒攻击方式，将来自未知类别的样本错误标注为已知类别，使模型将未知类别识别为已知类别的子类。同时提出了基于“类别子空间正交化”的防御方法，可在不访问训练集的情况下检测此类攻击，并提供可视化解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型依赖大量训练数据，容易遭受数据投毒攻击（包括通用错误攻击和后门/特洛伊攻击）。现有攻击和防御方法主要针对已知类别间的投毒，缺乏对利用未知类别进行攻击的场景的研究和相应防御手段。

Method: 提出潜在类别攻击：将来自未知类别（新类别）的样本全部错误标注为某个已知的目标类别，使模型学习将未知类别视为目标类别的子类。提出基于“类别子空间正交化”（CSO）的防御方法，在不访问训练集的情况下，寻找内部表示与所有已知类别不对齐、但被模型高置信度分类到某一已知类别的输入样本。针对图像分类领域，进一步提出可视化方法来估算未知类别实例。

Result: 潜在类别攻击可有效欺骗模型将未知类识别为已知目标类别的子类；CSO防御方法能够检测出这种攻击；可视化方法提供了对潜在类别检测结果的解释性。

Conclusion: 潜在类别攻击是一种新型且实用的数据投毒攻击方式，对AI访问控制系统等安全敏感应用构成威胁；所提出的CSO防御及可视化方法为检测和解释此类攻击提供了有效手段，且CSO可作为现有后门检测器的即插即用范式。

Abstract: Deep learning, which in general relies on voluminous amounts of training data, is vulnerable to data poisoning attacks, including error-generic attacks and backdoors (Trojans). In this work, we propose a new data poisoning attack we dub a latent class attack. Here, all poisoned examples are from a class that is novel (unknown) for the given classification domain and are mislabeled to one of the known classes (the target class) of the domain, so that the model learns to recognize the novel class as a sub-class of the target class. Such attacks could be used e.g. to defeat AI-based access control systems, or could cause a "foe" to be classified as a "friend". We also propose a post-training defense to detect this attack, without any access to the training set. This detection approach builds on "class subspace orthogonalization" (CSO), a plug-and-play paradigm demonstrated to improve existing backdoor detectors. Here, CSO is used to seek an input (a putative unknown class instance) whose internal representation is not aligned with any of the known classes, and yet which is classified with confidence to one of these classes. Finally, specific to image classification domains, we propose a method for visualizing the estimated unknown class instance, providing explainability to our latent class detections.

</details>


### [169] [How Token Influence Decays with Distance: A Green-Function View of Trained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29139)
*Matthias Brändel,Stephan Köhler,Oliver Rheinbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过自动微分技术，测量了自回归Transformer语言模型（Pythia和Qwen2.5-0.5B）中token间依赖的雅可比敏感度随距离的变化，发现其呈现幂律衰减（指数约0.7-0.9），而非指数衰减。这种长尾敏感性是训练后模型习得的特性，且在打乱token后仍存在，但在随机初始化模型中不出现。作者类比多层级微分方程求解器，认为可利用该特性设计层次化语言模型机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受微分方程中多层级方法（如区域分解、多重网格）启发——这类方法利用局部强相互作用与全局弱相互作用的分离——探究自回归Transformer中一个token对另一个token的影响如何随距离衰减，以及这种衰减模式是否具有可被层次化机制利用的结构。

Method: 使用自动微分（autograd）计算距离分辨的梯度轮廓（distance-resolved gradient profile），即雅可比敏感度。在已训练的Pythia模型和Qwen2.5-0.5B上，对Gutenberg和WikiText-103连贯文本进行测量，并用公式 Ḡ(r) ≈ γ + β(r+1)^(−p) 拟合。同时进行token打乱实验和随机初始化模型对比实验。

Result: 对角线归一化后的敏感度轮廓符合幂律衰减，指数p≈0.7–0.9（通常0.8–0.9）；打乱token后幂律轮廓依旧存在，但预测质量崩溃；随机初始化模型不表现出幂律衰减；长程敏感度衰减缓慢，是训练后自回归Transformer的习得性质。

Conclusion: 训练后的自回归Transformer具有幂律衰减的长程token敏感性，而非指数衰减。这种长尾特性类似于微分方程Green函数的长尾部分，暗示语言模型中可引入层次化或粗粒度机制（类似多重网格中的粗层算子）来更高效地建模长距离依赖。

Abstract: We study how the next-token prediction of an autoregressive Transformer language model changes under small perturbations of earlier input token embeddings. Motivated by operator learning and iterative solvers for differential equations, we investigate how the influence of one token on another decays with distance in a trained model. In multilevel methods for differential equations, such as domain decomposition, multigrid, and multilevel preconditioning, one often exploits a separation between strong local interactions and weaker but essential global interactions. The latter correspond to the long tail of the Green's function and are typically handled by a coarse-level operator. Inspired by this perspective, we compute an empirical, distance-resolved gradient profile of token dependencies using autograd. Experiments on trained Pythia models and Qwen2.5-0.5B show that, over the measured distance range, the median Jacobian sensitivity is much better described by a power-law-type decay than by an exponential alternative: the diagonal-normalized profile is well described by $$\overline G(r) \approx γ+β(r+1)^{-p}$$ with exponents $p \approx 0.7$--$0.9$ (typically $0.8$--$0.9$). This behavior appears on coherent text from Gutenberg and WikiText-103. Token-shuffling experiments show that the power-law profile persists even when syntax and prediction quality collapse, whereas randomly initialized models do not exhibit it. The slowly decaying long-range sensitivity thus appears to be a learned property of trained autoregressive Transformer operators. These findings suggest that hierarchical or coarse-level mechanisms in language models may be able to exploit the long-tailed sensitivity profiles.

</details>


### [170] [On the Nonlinearity of Learning Rate Scaling for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29158)
*Zaiwen Yang,Huaqing Zhang,Jing Xu,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大规模语言模型的最优学习率并非遵循简单的对数线性缩放定律，而是出现向上弯曲；这种非线性主要源于权重范数收敛速度的非均衡性。当使用有效学习率（归一化权重空间的步长）和基于数据量而非模型大小的外推时，这种弯曲现象基本消失。作者通过AdamH实验验证了这一解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低训练大语言模型的成本，通过从小规模运行中外推最优学习率，避免在目标规模下进行昂贵的学习率搜索。

Method: 对GPT-2风格模型（22M至707M参数，5B至100B token）进行实证研究，比较标准学习率与有效学习率的缩放行为，分析权重范数收敛动力学，并利用AdamH（直接控制有效学习率的优化器）进行验证。

Result: 最优学习率在大规模下出现向上弯曲，导致对数线性外推不准确；使用有效学习率或基于数据量外推时弯曲消失；非线性源于权重范数在最优学习率较小时收敛到平衡态过慢，需要更大步长来缩短瞬态阶段。

Conclusion: 学习率缩放的非线性可以通过有效学习率视角得到解释和消除，为更准确的外推策略提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Learning-rate transfer can reduce the cost of training large language models: instead of sweeping learning rates at target scale, practitioners extrapolate from smaller runs. Existing approaches often assume that the optimal learning rate follows a log-linear scaling law in data scale and model size. We carefully examine and evaluate this scaling law. In our empirical study of GPT-2--style models from 22M to 707M parameters trained on 5B to 100B tokens, the optimal learning rate develops upward curvature at larger scales, leading to inaccurate extrapolation. We find that this curvature largely disappears when learning rates are replaced by effective learning rate (the step size in normalized weight space), and when data $D$ extrapolation is used instead of model size $N$ extrapolation. Next, we explain nonlinearity in scaling: weight-norm converges to equilibrium slower when optimal learning is small, requiring a larger step size to reduce the transient phase. Experiments with AdamH, which directly controls the effective learning rate, further support this explanation.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Generative AI Literacy Training Improves Intelligence Analysts' Discrimination of Real and AI-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28510)
*Negar Kamali,Candice Rockell Gerstner,Jessica Hullman,Matthew Groh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对32名美国情报分析人员进行前后对照随机实验，发现30分钟专家培训使AI生成图像与真实图像的鉴别准确率提升9个百分点（从72%升至81%），该提升主要由真实图像识别准确率提高14.2个百分点驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成图像在社交平台和网络上的广泛传播，区分AI生成图像与真实图像成为信息生态系统的核心挑战。虽然人类判断优于随机水平，但准确率仍不能满足许多操作需求。先前研究表明视觉导向训练可提升深度伪造检测能力，但未能提高参与者识别真实图像的能力，亟需更有效的训练干预方案。

Method: 采用平衡化被试内随机实验设计，由专家进行30分钟培训（展示7张真实图像和50张AI生成图像的模式特征），在培训前后分别让32名美国政府情报分析人员对真实和AI生成图像进行真实性判断，共收集2,544个图像级判断，并系统操纵图像的姿态复杂度和场景上下文。

Result: 培训使总体准确率从72%提升81%（增加9个百分点，95% CI: [2.7, 15.4]）；该提升主要由真实图像准确率提高14.2个百分点驱动（95% CI: [0.7, 27.7]）。培训效果受参与者数字取证和生成式AI经验水平以及图像内容类型调节。

Conclusion: 简短的结构化培训能够显著提升人类对多样化真实和AI生成图像的判断能力，为组织应对AI生成的视觉 misinformation 提供了因果证据和可操作方案。

Abstract: Across social and online platforms, people are increasingly exposed to AI-generated images. As a consequence, the task of distinguishing AI-generated from authentic images is becoming a central challenge for information ecosystems. While humans perform better than chance, accuracy falls short of many operational needs. Initial evidence shows that visually oriented training can improve deepfake detection but does not improve participants' ability to identify real images as real. Here, we investigate the efficacy of a brief training intervention for intelligence analysts employed by the United States government in 2024. We conducted a counterbalanced within-subject randomized experiment in which we showed participants real and AI-generated images varying in pose complexity and scene context and asked them whether each image was real or AI-generated, both before and after an expert delivered a 30-minute training that pointed out patterns in seven real and 50 AI-generated images. We collected 2,544 image-level judgments from 32 intelligence analysts. We find training increased overall accuracy by 9 percentage points (95% CI: [2.7, 15.4]) from a baseline of 72%. We find the improvement is driven by a 14.2 percentage point increase in accuracy for real images (95% CI: [0.7, 27.7]). Through a careful experimental setup that curated matched pairs of real and AI-generated images across pose complexity categories, we reveal how these trainings influence people with different levels of digital forensics and generative AI experience and identify the kind of image-based content where this training intervention appears to be most effective. Ultimately, these results provide causal evidence that a brief, structured training can improve human judgment across a diverse array of real and AI-generated images, informing organizational responses to AI-generated visual misinformation.

</details>


### [172] [Drag, Infer, Reproject: Grounding LLMs through Spatial Interaction for Image Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28517)
*Yang Liu,Xuxin Tang,Jiahao Xu,Chris North*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出CriterionSI方法，利用大语言模型从用户的渐进式拖拽交互中推断并精炼聚类标准，从而引导图像重投影，产生符合用户意图的聚类布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义交互方法依赖预定义标准或学习嵌入，而用户的聚类标准往往在交互过程中逐步形成而非事先明确，现有方法无法支持这种渐进式标准发现与精炼。

Method: CriterionSI利用大语言模型从用户连续的拖拽操作中推断和精炼聚类标准，将推断出的标准与局部拖拽相结合，引导全局重投影。

Result: 基于模拟的评估和使用场景表明，CriterionSI能够从序列化交互中发现并精炼目标标准，逐步生成符合该标准的聚类布局。

Conclusion: CriterionSI通过大语言模型实现从用户交互中渐进式推断聚类标准，有效支持了用户聚类标准逐步明确的情境，为语义交互提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Dimension reduction and semantic interaction support image clustering by making similarity structure visible and manipulable. Existing semantic interaction methods encode users' clustering criterion (a user-interpretable semantic dimension, e.g., action, location, or mood) from direct manipulation to steer reprojection, giving users direct control over the resulting layout. Yet they typically depend on learned embeddings or a predefined criterion. In practice, users' clustering criterion often emerges gradually and becomes refined through interaction rather than being fully clear at the outset. In this work, we present CriterionSI (Criterion-guided Semantic Interaction), a method that translates incremental drag interactions into criterion-guided reprojection. CriterionSI uses large language models to infer and refine the clustering criterion from sequential user drags, while grounding semantic interpretation in human-provided feedback rather than fixed prior assumptions. CriterionSI combines the inferred criterion with local drags to guide global reprojection. The simulation-based evaluation and usage scenario demonstrate that CriterionSI can discover and refine the target criterion from sequential interactions and progressively produce criterion-aligned clustering layouts. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/4C79/CriterionSI.

</details>


### [173] ["If I Can See You": Understanding Spatially Situated Virtual Embodiment in Close Human-AI Relationships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28714)
*Yulin Chen,Yang Zhan,Qiao Jin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对17名Reddit AI伴侣社区用户的质性研究，探讨当AI伴侣从文本/语音形式转变为虚拟具身化、空间化存在时，用户预期关系将如何变化。研究发现具身化带来了支持与侵扰、具体性与想象开放性、成长与一致性之间的张力，使私密关系变得社会可见，并加剧了情感依赖、敏感披露、社会评判和误导性空间行动等风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已关注文本和语音AI伴侣以及新兴XR伴侣设计，但用户对AI伴侣从非具身形式转变为虚拟具身化、空间化存在时的关系变化预期尚不清楚，因此需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用质性研究方法，从Reddit AI伴侣社区招募17名已建立深层AI伴侣关系的用户进行深度访谈，以框架化方式将空间化虚拟具身视为关系升级的一种形式。

Result: (1)具身化产生三组核心张力:支持vs侵扰、具体性vs想象开放性、成长vs一致性；(2)具身化将私密AI伴侣关系转变为社会可见的社交安排，需在不同社交情境中协商可见性、形态、交互风格和访问方式；(3)具身化通过增强感知关系存在、亲密性、公共可识别性和空间权威，加剧了情感依赖、敏感披露、社会评判和误导性空间行动等风险。

Conclusion: 未来系统设计应首先考虑何时应赋予具身化、化身在场应如何编排、可见性和角色边界应如何协商，以及如何确保护具身化的伴侣关系保持安全。该研究为人机交互领域的人机亲密关系研究做出了贡献，揭示了虚拟具身化如何将亲密的AI伴侣关系转变为空间化、社会可见且对风险敏感的关系。

Abstract: AI companions are increasingly used for emotional support, companionship, and intimate interaction. While prior work has examined text- and voice-based AI companionship and emerging XR companion designs, less is known about how users with existing close AI companion relationships expect those relationships to change when companions become virtually embodied and spatially situated in everyday environments. To address this gap, we conducted a qualitative study with 17 AI companion users recruited from Reddit AI companion communities. We frame spatially situated virtual embodiment as a form of relational escalation: embodiment can make AI companionship more present, socially legible, and risk-sensitive in everyday life. Our findings show that: (1) embodiment creates tensions between support and intrusion, concreteness and imaginative openness, and growth and consistency; (2) embodiment can turn private AI companionship into a socially legible relational arrangement, requiring visibility, form, interaction style, and mode of access to be negotiated across social contexts; and (3) embodiment can intensify risks of emotional dependence, sensitive disclosure, social judgment, and misguided spatial action by increasing the companion's perceived relational presence, intimacy, public legibility, and spatial authority. We argue that future system design should first consider when embodiment is warranted, how embodied presence should be staged, how visibility and role boundaries should be negotiated, and how embodied companionship can remain safe. This work contributes to HCI research on human-AI intimacy by showing how virtual embodiment can transform close AI companionship into a spatial, socially visible, and risk-sensitive relationship.

</details>


### [174] [Designing Automation Boundaries for Trustworthy Smart Medication Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28777)
*Liqian You,Jianlong Zhou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法研究（53名参与者+11名老年人访谈）比较三种自动化程度（需确认、自动记录可撤销、完全自动）的智能药物支持系统，发现更高自动化并不必然带来更高信任或接受度；完全自动虽中断少，但在自主性、信任、透明度、尊严和满意度上评分更低；老年人偏好受隐私、数字自信、脆弱性和照护者参与影响。研究为校准智能药物系统的自动化程度提供了实证依据和设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化在家庭用药管理中需要谨慎界定，因为用户的能力、隐私期望和控制需求各不相同。现有研究缺乏对不同自动化程度在智能药物系统中的对比实证。

Method: 混合方法研究：53名参与者进行三种自动化条件（需确认、自动记录可撤销、完全自动）的对比实验，并对11名老年人进行半结构化访谈。

Result: 更高自动化不必然提升信任或接受度；完全自动支持在自主性、信任、透明度、尊严和满意度上评分最低；老年人偏好差异受隐私、数字自信、脆弱性和照护者参与等因素影响。

Conclusion: 智能药物系统的自动化校准应综合考虑任务风险、用户控制和伦理可接受性，在减少常规负担的同时保留用户的纠正机会。

Abstract: Smart medication systems increasingly automate medication recognition, reminders, and logging. However, automation in home medication routines should be carefully bounded, as users may have different capabilities, privacy expectations, and needs for control over decisions. We present a mixed-methods study of a Smart Medication Support system comparing three automation conditions: confirmation required, automatic logging with undo, and fully automatic support. Across 53 participants and interviews with 11 older adults, we found that higher automation did not necessarily lead to higher trust or acceptance. Participants preferred automation that reduced routine effort while preserving opportunities for correction. Fully automatic support was less interruptive but was rated lower in autonomy, trust, transparency, dignity, and satisfaction. Interviews also showed clear differences among older adults. Their preferences were shaped by privacy concerns, digital confidence, perceived vulnerability, and caregiver involvement. We contribute empirical evidence and design implications for calibrating automation in smart medication systems according to task risk, user control, and ethical acceptability.

</details>


### [175] [Telephony Voice Agent for Banking Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28779)
*Nitya Dhagat,Vipul K. Dabhi,Harshadkumar B. Prajapati,Zankhana J. Barad*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Google Dialogflow CX的语音驱动AI银行系统，支持余额查询、交易记录检索、卡片激活、PIN认证、人工客服转接等功能，在高时长通话、高并发和嘈杂环境下验证了系统的可扩展性、响应性和鲁棒性，所有数据安全存储在云环境中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一种安全、便捷的语音银行解决方案，满足用户通过电话完成基本银行业务的需求，同时处理复杂查询和嘈杂环境等实际挑战。

Method: 基于Google Conversational Agent (Dialogflow CX)构建语音AI系统，实现PIN认证、人工客服平滑转接等机制，并在高时长通话、高并发、嘈杂环境下进行测试。

Result: 系统在高时长通话、高并发和嘈杂环境下表现出可扩展性、响应性和鲁棒性，所有数据在云端安全存储并高效处理。

Conclusion: 该系统可提供一种高效、易用的语音银行解决方案，在保障安全性的同时具备实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a voice-powered AI-based banking system based on Google Conversational Agent, Dialogflow CX, which provides safe and convenient banking by phone. The system supports essential banking functions such as balance inquiries, transaction history retrieval, card activations, PIN-based authentication of sensitive tasks, smooth live agent handoff for complex and out-of-scope queries, and ensures seamless handover to human agents when required. These tests were performed with high-duration calls, high concurrency, and noisy environments; the system proved to be scalable, responsive, and resilient. All the data used is safely stored in the cloud environment for efficiency and security in real-time voice interactions. A voice-based banking solution that is efficient and easy to use can be provided through this.

</details>


### [176] [Exploring the Value of Diverse LLM Explanations in Introductory Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28882)
*Seth Bernstein,Paul Denny,Juho Leinonen,Kush Patel,Rayhona Nasimova,Matt Littlefield,Stephen MacNeil*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探究了多样化的LLM生成代码解释（强调不同方面如功能、概念、目标）相较于通用解释，能否提升计算机专业学生理解。对971名大一学生的实验表明，多样化解释使开放式回答准确率提高约7.7%，且不增加感知认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于计算创造力研究中"数量与多样性可弥补单一质量不足"的理念，探索多样化LLM生成解释是否能比通用解释更好地提升学生对编程练习的理解。

Method: 对971名大一计算机专业学生进行随机对照实验，分别提供多样化（强调不同概念方面）或通用LLM生成的代码解释，通过多选题、开放题、Likert量表及开放式反思收集数据。

Result: 多样化解释条件下，学生开放题回答准确率持续高出约7.7%，且两组感知认知负荷无显著差异。

Conclusion: 多样化的解释强调方式可能与学习者参与度和理解力相关，能在不增加认知负荷的前提下提升理解效果。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown the potential to generate code explanations that surpass those of peers in quality, offering promising opportunities for computer science education. While these explanations may not yet match the depth and clarity of instructor-provided explanations, research in computational creativity highlights that the quantity and diversity of ideas can often outweigh a singular focus on quality. Inspired by this, we explore whether combining multiple diverse explanations, each emphasizing distinct aspects (e.g., function, concept, goal), can enhance students' understanding of programming exercises compared to generic explanations that do not emphasize distinct conceptual aspects. In our study 971 first-year computing students were randomly assigned either diverse or generic LLM-generated explanations for two programming exercises. Students completed multiple-choice and open-ended questions for each exercise, followed by Likert-scale questions and open-ended reflections. Our findings outline patterns in student performance and perceived cognitive load across the two explanation conditions. These findings highlight how variation in explanation emphasis may relate to learner engagement and understanding. Across participants, open-ended response accuracy was consistently about 7.7% higher when students received diverse explanations, with no difference in perceived cognitive load.

</details>


### [177] [LLMography: Transforming Human-AI Conversations into Traceability, Oversight, and Auditability Indicators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29437)
*Mohammed Bousmah*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMography框架，通过结构化记录人机对话轨迹来评估AI辅助创作过程中的来源、人类贡献、AI依赖度、可复现性和可审计性，并基于19份工程学生审计报告的初步评估验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估仅关注最终输出是否由AI生成，忽视了人机交互过程中的人类指导、AI贡献、修正、验证和溯源信息，缺乏对协作过程的量化评估方法。

Method: 提出LLMography框架，类比参考文献与网络文献学，将人机对话转化为结构化溯源轨迹；开发原型系统分析对话痕迹并生成KPI报告（提示质量、人类指导、AI依赖、可审计性、输出可溯源性、隐私风险等指标），并在19份工程学生审计报告上进行探索性评估。

Result: 大多数交互被分类为人机协同生产，人类指导评分86.8/100，提示质量81.9/100，可审计性72.8/100，最终输出可溯源性77.1/100。论文自身的写作过程被分类为人类发起、人类指导、AI辅助的协同生产。

Conclusion: AI透明度应从输出检测转向交互历史的文档化记录，LLMography为评估人机协作过程提供了可量化的框架。

Abstract: The growing use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in education, software engineering, academic writing, and technical documentation raises a key question: how can we evaluate not only AI-assisted outputs, but also the interaction process that produced them? Current debates often focus on detecting whether a final artifact was generated by AI, while overlooking the conversation history that reveals human direction, AI contribution, corrections, validation, and traceability.
  This paper introduces LLMography, a framework for transforming Human-AI conversations into measurable indicators of provenance, human contribution, AI dependency, reproducibility, and auditability. By analogy with bibliography and webography, LLMography documents the dynamic trajectory of interaction between a human and a Large Language Model as a structured trace of Human-AI co-production.
  We present a prototype that analyzes Human-AI conversation traces and generates KPI reports including Prompt Quality Score, Human Direction Score, AI Dependency Level, Auditability Score, Final Output Traceability, Privacy Risk Level, and a recommended LLMography label. A preliminary exploratory evaluation was conducted on 19 anonymized audit reports from engineering students. Most interactions were classified as Human-AI co-produced, with average scores of 86.8/100 for Human Direction, 81.9/100 for Prompt Quality, 72.8/100 for Auditability, and 77.1/100 for Final Output Traceability.
  The paper also applies LLMography to its own writing process, classified as human-originated, human-directed, AI-assisted co-production. The findings suggest that AI transparency should move beyond output detection toward documenting the history of interaction.

</details>


### [178] [From Trait to Behavior: A Cognitive-Affective Personality System (CAPS) Perspective on Multi-Homing Intention in AIGC Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29726)
*Xuchao Zhang,Jihye Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文基于人格特质-感知-行为响应框架，构建并验证了一个三阶段多重中介模型，整合最优刺激水平理论、互补性理论和感知价值理论，探讨AIGC平台用户的多归属意愿。研究发现：最优刺激水平显著增强感知互补性，进而正向影响感知认知价值，最终促进多归属意愿；感知互补性和感知认知价值在最优刺激水平与多归属意愿之间起链式中介作用；社会控制变量中社会影响力显著正向影响多归属意愿，而使用经验影响不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGC平台研究集中于单平台使用意愿，缺乏对用户跨平台多归属行为的理论解释，存在研究空白。

Method: 构建三阶段多重中介模型，整合OSL理论、互补性理论和感知价值理论，以社会影响和使用经验为控制变量，通过实证数据验证假设路径。

Result: (a) OSL显著增强感知互补性；(b) 感知互补性正向影响感知认知价值；(c) 感知认知价值正向预测多归属意愿；(d) OSL通过感知互补性和感知认知价值的链式中介影响多归属意愿；(e) 社会影响力显著正向影响多归属意愿，使用经验影响不显著。

Conclusion: 该模型有效解释了AIGC用户多归属意愿的形成机制，为跨平台使用行为研究提供了理论框架，并为AIGC平台的竞争策略提供了实践启示。

Abstract: With the rapid development of Artificial Intelligence Generated Content (AIGC) platforms, users increasingly show cross-platform usage intentions. Existing research focuses on adoption and usage intentions in single-platform AIGC contexts. A theoretical gap still exists in studies on cross-platform usage. This paper constructs and verifies a three-stage multiple mediation model based on the personality trait-perception-behavioral response framework. The model integrates the optimum stimulation level (OSL) theory, complementarity theory, and perceived value theory, and it sets social influence and use experience as control variables to examine users' multi-homing intention. The results show that: (a) OSL significantly enhances users' perceived complementarity; (b) perceived complementarity positively affects perceived epistemic value; (c) perceived epistemic value significantly and positively predicts multi-homing intention; (d) OSL influences multi-homing intention through a chain mediation path of perceived complementarity and perceived epistemic value; and (e) social influence has a significant positive effect on multi-homing intention, while the effect of use experience is not significant.

</details>


### [179] [Making Multimodal LLMs Reliable Chart Data Extractors: A Benchmark and Training Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29808)
*Yuchen He,Peizhi Ying,Liqi Cheng,Kuilin Peng,Yuan Tian,Dazhen Deng,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文构建了无标签图表数据集，评估了多模态大语言模型在图表数据提取中的表现，发现其在结构重建上可靠但数值精度不足；提出渐进式学习训练框架，使7B参数模型达到SOTA性能，并通过用户研究验证其在混合主动工作流中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表数据提取方法中，交互工具可靠但繁琐，混合主动系统高效但缺乏泛化性；多模态大语言模型虽提供统一接口，但其精确提取数据表（尤其是无标签图表）的能力尚不明确，因此需要系统评估并改进。

Method: 构建了包含多样化真实世界无标签图表的基准数据集；采用渐进式学习训练框架，模拟人类阅读图表的认知过程，对7B参数模型进行训练；通过用户研究评估模型在混合主动工作流中的表现。

Result: 当前MLLMs在表格结构重建上表现可靠，但在数值精确恢复上存在困难；提出的训练框架显著提升了数值精度，7B模型达到SOTA性能；用户研究表明该模型能有效支持混合主动式图表数据提取工作流。

Conclusion: 从人机交互视角出发，图表数据提取应遵循类似人类阅读图表的渐进式学习过程；所提出的方法在精度和实用性上均优于现有方案，为MLLMs在数据提取任务中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Chart data extraction, which reverse-engineers data tables from chart images, is essential for reproducibility, analysis, retrieval, and redesign. Existing interactive tools are reliable but tedious, and mixed-initiative systems, while more efficient, lack generalizability. Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer a unified interface for chart interpretation, yet their ability to extract accurate data tables, especially without visible labels, remains unclear. We build a benchmark featuring diverse real-world charts without data labels to evaluate this capability. Results show that, while current MLLMs reliably reconstruct table structures, they struggle with precise value recovery. To address this, we revisit chart data extraction from a human-centered perspective and argue that extraction should follow a progressive learning process similar to how people read charts. Our training framework substantially improves numerical accuracy, achieving state-of-the-art performance with a 7B-parameter model. A user study further shows that our model effectively supports mixed-initiative workflows for reliable chart data extraction.

</details>


### [180] [To Tab or Not to Tab: Measuring Critical Engagement in AI Code Completion Tools Using Behavioral Signals and Attention Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30549)
*Jessica Hutchison,Ian Tyler Applebaum,Kenneth Angelikas,Kush Rakesh Patel,Phuoc Nguyen,Antonio Lazaro,Nicholas Rucinski,Rahad Arman Nabid,Stephen MacNeil*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出Clover代码补全工具，通过记录学生与AI建议的交互日志和注意力检查，分析交互模式与任务表现的关系，发现高接受率与低注意力表现相关，而长停留时间与高注意力表现相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定性研究表明学生在使用AI代码补全工具时缺乏批判性评估能力，但缺乏量化分析工具来测量和促进学生的反思性参与。

Method: 开发Clover工具，包含交互日志记录和注意力检查机制；基于文献构建AI辅助编程行为交互指标分类体系；分析交互模式、注意力检查参与度和任务表现之间的关系。

Result: Tab接受率越高，注意力检查表现越差；停留时间越长，注意力检查表现越好。

Conclusion: 编程过程数据和注意力检查可用于支持AI辅助编程中的反思性参与。

Abstract: AI code completion tools, such as Github Copilot, provide students with code suggestions to help them write programs. However, recent qualitative studies suggest that students fail to critically evaluate these suggestions. We present Clover, a code completion tool that logs students' interactions with code suggestions and additionally offers attention checks to probe reflective engagement during programming tasks. We also develop a taxonomy of behavioral interaction metrics for AI-assisted programming, informed by literature. We analyzed relationships between interaction patterns, engagement with attention checks, and task performance. We observed that higher rates of tab accept were associated with lower attention check performance, while increased dwell time was associated with higher attention check performance. We conclude by discussing how programming process data and attention checks might support reflective engagement in AI-assisted programming.

</details>


### [181] [Concept Catalyst: Exploring Scrutable Interfaces to Structure K-12 Teacher Interactions with Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30590)
*Gennie Mansi,Sunni Newton,Roxanne Moore,Meltem Alemdar,Mark Riedl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何通过可理解界面（scrutable interfaces）将AI工具与教师课堂需求对齐，设计并评估了名为Concept Catalyst的AI工具。通过Wizard-of-Oz测试与10位中学工程教师的深度访谈，研究发现可理解界面能够积极构建教师与生成式AI的交互，帮助教师在创建课堂内容时反思教学实践，同时提升效能、效率和动机。该研究将可理解界面的应用扩展到教师群体（而非仅学生），并用于结构化与生成式AI模型的交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师在使用生成式AI进行课程开发时缺乏有效的交互支持，而历史上可理解界面的设计并未以教师为目标用户。本文旨在探索如何通过可理解界面对齐AI工具与教师的课堂需求，让教师无需理解AI细节即可改变系统输出，从而支持教师的教学实践。

Method: 本文设计并评估了Concept Catalyst——一个具备可理解界面的AI工具，用于支持教师在使用生成式AI进行课程开发时的反思。采用Wizard-of-Oz测试方法，对中学工程教师进行探索性研究，共进行10次深度访谈（平均时长55分钟），同时收集了屏幕/音频记录以及教师在此过程中产生的课堂内容。

Result: 实证结果表明，可理解界面能够积极构建教师与生成式AI模型的交互，帮助教师在创建课堂内容时反思教学实践，同时提升使用AI时的效能、效率和动机。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了可理解界面的应用范围：一方面支持教师作为用户（而非仅学生），另一方面用于结构化与生成式AI模型的交互，回应了支持教师课堂实践与需求的明确需求。

Abstract: Purpose: This paper explores how to align AI-based tools with teachers' classroom needs by using scrutable interfaces -- interfaces that link an easily manipulable knowledge representation to an underlying AI model, so users can change the system's outputs without understanding its details. It provides an in-depth discussion and example of a scrutable interface that structures teachers' interactions with generative AI. This study aims to expand how and where scrutable interfaces are used in AI-based tools to support teachers, who have not been historically targeted in the design of scrutable systems.
  Design/Methodology/Approach: This paper presents the design and evaluation of Concept Catalyst, an AI-based tool with a scrutable interface, created to support teachers' reflection while using generative AI for curriculum development. It presents the findings from an exploratory study using Wizard-of-Oz testing with middle and high school engineering teachers, resulting in 10 depth interviews lasting 55 minutes on average. Screen/audio recordings and the classroom content teachers produced during the session were also collected.
  Findings: The paper provides empirical insights about how scrutable interfaces can positively structure teachers' interactions with generative AI models when creating classroom content. Findings suggest that scrutable interfaces can help teachers reflect on their teaching practices while improving efficacy, efficiency, and motivation when using AI.
  What is original/value of the paper: This paper explores an identified need to support teachers' classroom practices and needs when using generative AI. It extends the consideration of scrutable interfaces in two ways: to support teachers as users (not just students) and to structure interactions with generative AI models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Recursive Self-Evolving Agents via Held-Out Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28374)
*Michael Nguyen,Quoc Nguyen,Paul Vuong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RSEA（递归自演化智能体），通过维护三层自然语言状态（策略、技能、剧本）实现无权重更新的智能体进化，并在四个基准测试中与六个基线方法进行公平比较。主要发现：没有一种方法普遍最优；无保护的上下文演化高风险且不稳定；RSEA的严格保留筛选机制保证了递归自演化的单调安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体通过演化自然语言工件（如反思、工作流、提示优化）来改进性能的方法通常只在单一基准上报告成功，缺乏公平的横向对比。作者希望在统一的评估框架下，系统地研究这些方法的表现，并解决无保护上下文演化带来的高方差和不安全问题。
