Result: 研究发现：（1）当平民部署自主多智能体网络系统时，伤害由人类脱离后系统生成的决策产生，不符合"一个因果步骤"标准；（2）"必要组成部分"要求预设了可独立分类的下游人类贡献者，但在AI中介操作中这一链条断裂，无法延伸适用；（3）该框架因此默认将此类部署视为间接参与，与其旨在捕获直接参与敌对行动的平民的目的事实上相悖；（4）目标规范粒度是"必要组成部分"测试中具体性要求赖以运作的隐含属性；（5）现有AI治理工具（如模型卡、系统卡等）未记录或报告该属性。

Conclusion: 现行国际人道法框架在应对AI中介的平民网络行动时存在结构性漏洞，需要法律与治理工具的协同更新。在技术层面，现有AI治理工具需要纳入目标规范粒度等关键参数的记录；在法律层面，可能需要重新审视直接因果关系标准的适用条件，特别是"一个因果步骤"和"必要组成部分"要求在AI自主决策场景下的适用性。

Abstract: International humanitarian law protects civilians from direct attack unless and for such time as they take direct part in hostilities, with the ICRC's 2009 Interpretive Guidance operationalising this rule through a three-criterion cumulative test. This paper argues that AI-mediated civilian cyber operations challenge the direct causation element of this test in a structurally specific way: when a civilian deploys an autonomous multi-agent cyber system of the kind recently demonstrated in offensive AI research, the "one causal step" standard fails because harm is produced by system-generated decisions made after human disengagement, and the integral-part requirement does not extend because it presupposes downstream human contributors whose conduct can be independently classified. The framework therefore defaults to treating such deployments as indirect participation, in tension with its purpose of capturing civilians who personally take part in hostilities. Beyond the doctrinal analysis, this paper identifies goal-specification granularity as the property on which the integral-part test's concreteness component implicitly turns, classifies AI-mediated operations along a five-level spectrum, and argues that existing technical AI governance instruments do not log or report this property.

</details>


### [217] [Selective Memory Retention for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29178)
*Pranath Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究记忆增强型LLM代理中的保留策略问题，提出TraceRetain轻量级框架。在干净ALFWorld环境下，外部记忆优于无记忆，但不同保留策略差异不显著；在受控噪声写入压力下，无界记忆和FIFO-K50性能大幅下降，而TraceRetain-CEM几乎不变，保持97/100任务成功率。有界保留在饱和干净基准上节省内存和步数效率，仅在噪声流中区别于缓存启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究记忆增强型LLM代理中，何时保留策略才能真正影响性能，以及在有界外部记忆条件下如何有效管理记忆条目。

Method: 提出TraceRetain框架，通过多重可解释特征（成功率、年龄、访问频率、冗余度、特异性、相似度、下游效用）对记忆条目评分，在容量上限时驱逐最低分条目。

Result: 干净环境下不同有界保留策略差异不显著；噪声写入下无界记忆Precision@5从20.2%降至12.4%，FIFO-K50从15.8%降至3.8%，TraceRetain-CEM仅从16.9%微降至16.6%，任务成功率97/100。

Conclusion: 有界保留在干净场景下提供内存和步数效率优势且不损失任务成功，仅在含噪声数据流中才展现对缓存启发式方法的差异优势。

Abstract: When does retention matter for memory-augmented LLM agents? We study this with TraceRetain, a lightweight framework for bounded external memory in frozen LLM agents that scores entries by interpretable features (success, age, access frequency, redundancy, specificity, similarity, downstream utility) and evicts the lowest-scoring ones at capacity. On clean ALFWorld with gpt-5-mini, external memory robustly improves over no memory across two seeds, but differences among bounded retention policies fall within Wilson 95% CIs: clean ALFWorld at T=100 to T=200 does not naturally exhibit the memory pollution retention is designed to address. Under a controlled noisy-write stress (75% synthetic distractors), unbounded memory and FIFO-K50 degrade on Precision@5 (20.2% to 12.4% and 15.8% to 3.8%) while TraceRetain-CEM is essentially unchanged (16.9% to 16.6%) and preserves 97/100 task success. The mechanism: unbounded memory has the highest mean similarity (0.87) but lowest precision, indicating failed distractors close to the query in embedding space. Held-out in-distribution evaluation shows memory-augmented policies solving 47 to 49 of 50 tasks vs. 39/50 for no memory. Bounded retention buys memory and step efficiency on saturated clean benchmarks at no task-success cost, and only differentiates from cache heuristics when streams contain noise.

</details>


### [218] [Measuring Graph-to-Graph Semantic Similarity in Knowledge Graphs: An Empirical Evaluation of Knowledge Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29180)
*Seungryeol Baek,Wooseok Sim,Hogun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究知识图谱间的图级语义相似性问题，提出基于知识图谱嵌入的EmbPairSim和AvgEmbSim两种评分函数，在WikiText-2和CC-News数据集上，EmbPairSim在MRR指标上比Sentence-BERT提升高达5.3个百分点，且参数量更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG嵌入方法主要关注实体、关系和三元组层面的表示，缺乏对图级语义信息的捕获；而传统图级方法仅基于结构模式比较，结构相似性无法保证语义相似性。因此需要研究如何评估KG间的图级语义相似度。

Method: 首先构建语义匹配数据集：修改文本文档，从原始和修改后文档中提取KG，将文档间的已知对应关系迁移至KG对。然后比较基于文本、基于结构和基于KG嵌入的方法。对于KG嵌入方法，提出两种评分函数：EmbPairSim（最大成对实体相似度）和AvgEmbSim（频率加权质心）。

Result: 在WikiText-2和CC-News数据集上，EmbPairSim相比Sentence-BERT在MRR上提升最高达5.3个百分点，且参数量显著更少。

Conclusion: KGE表示可以作为紧凑且有效的信号用于知识图谱间的图级语义相似性比较。

Abstract: A Knowledge Graph (KG) represents facts as structured triples and is widely used to organize relational knowledge across diverse domains. Just as textual information ranges from words and sentences to complete documents, KG information can be interpreted at multiple levels, from entities, relations, and triples to subgraphs and entire KGs. However, existing KG embedding methods mainly focus on entities, relations, and triples, leaving graph-level semantics largely unaddressed. Conventional graph-level methods, which typically compare graphs based on structural patterns, are also insufficient because structural similarity alone cannot guarantee semantic similarity between KGs. To evaluate how well different methods capture such graph-level semantic information, we study graph-to-graph semantic similarity, which determines whether a pair of KGs represents semantically corresponding underlying information. To obtain reliable ground-truth correspondences, we construct a semantic matching dataset by modifying text documents, extracting KGs from both original and modified documents, and transferring their known correspondences to KG pairs. We compare text-based, structure-based, and KG embedding-based approaches on each dataset. For the KG embedding-based approach, we introduce two scoring functions: \textit{EmbPairSim}, which uses maximal pairwise entity similarity, and \textit{AvgEmbSim}, which uses a frequency-weighted centroid. Experiments on WikiText-2 and CC-News show that \textit{EmbPairSim} achieves up to 5.3 pp higher MRR than Sentence-BERT while using substantially fewer parameters. These results suggest that KGE representations can serve as compact and effective signals for graph-to-graph semantic similarity in KGs. Our code is available at https://github.com/SeungRyeolBaek/KG-to-KG-Semantic-Similarity.

</details>


### [219] [Evidence-Informed LLM Beliefs for Continual Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29182)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Reece Adamson,Andrew McCallum,Peter Clark,Ashish Sabharwal,Bodhisattwa Prasad Majumder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM科学发现中静态惊奇度与人类非平稳认知不匹配的问题，提出证据更新的非平稳惊奇度计算方法，结合信念更新过滤与多样性最大化搜索策略，使累积非平稳惊奇度平均提升30.62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoDiscovery方法将惊奇度视为静态量，而人类推理中的惊奇度是非平稳的——相对于随经验演化的信念定义，这是持续科学发现的前提条件。当前方法无法捕捉这种动态性。

Method: 1) 采用基于嵌入的检索增强生成（RAG）更新先验信念以计算非平稳惊奇度；2) 引入信念更新过滤（剔除37.5%的虚假静态惊奇度）；3) 引入多样性最大化搜索策略。

Result: 在五个发现领域平均提升累积非平稳惊奇度30.62%，识别出37.5%的静态惊奇度为虚假信号。

Conclusion: 持续科学发现不仅需要更好的信念测量方法，还需要避免冗余、鼓励多样性的搜索机制。

Abstract: Open-ended scientific discovery with large language models (LLMs) increasingly operates as a long-horizon loop of hypothesis search and verification, where a reward signal guides which hypotheses to test next. A notable recent example is AutoDiscovery, which uses "Bayesian surprise" - the belief shift an LLM undergoes after observing evidence for a hypothesis - as both a discovery metric and a reward for search. We first observe that AutoDiscovery treats surprisal as a static quantity, while surprisal in human reasoning is non-stationary - it is defined relative to beliefs that evolve with experience, a prerequisite for continual scientific discovery. We address this mismatch with evidence-informed LLM beliefs: priors updated with evidence from previous hypotheses to compute non-stationary surprisal for new hypotheses. We compare in-context belief-updating mechanisms and find that embedding-based retrieval-augmented generation over prior discoveries best anticipates eventual posteriors, identifying 37.5% of static surprisals as spurious. We then modify search to avoid these spurious rewards and prioritize hypotheses that remain surprising under non-stationary beliefs. Concretely, we introduce two complementary changes to the original search procedure: belief-update filtering and diversity maximization. Across five discovery domains, our method increases accumulated non-stationary surprisal by 30.62% on average compared to the original search procedure, demonstrating that continual scientific discovery with LLMs requires not only better belief measurement but also search procedures that avoid redundancy and encourage diversity.

</details>


### [220] [AI Trading's Alpha Singularity: Emergent Market Reasoning through Agent-to-Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29194)
*Yuqi Li,Siyuan Liu,Bingjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出封存联合搜索（SJS）框架，通过结构化的信息流约束防止自确认偏差，并在Agora系统中实证：五个LLM智能体通过多通道通信演化技能库，在CSI 1000上实现91天2026年样本外夏普比率+1.87，远超基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化因子挖掘固定评分函数并变化搜索算法，导致搜索对评分函数无法惩罚的部分过拟合，造成样本外泛化差距。作者试图将评分函数也视为搜索构件，研究何种条件能使这种联合搜索可容许。

Method: 提出SJS框架，包含角色分解、类型化角色间通信、来源封存读取、版本化存储和基质本地晋升等结构条件。实现Agora系统：五类LLM智能体通过三通道通信，演化八个技能库，因子库基于AlphaGen算子。三位评估者撰写报告汇总为简报，保留分歧而非投票。

Result: Agora在CSI 1000上运行100轮，91天2026年样本外夏普比率+1.87；最佳基线在有利种子下+1.334，跨种子均值-0.755。预加载两个指标的冷冻库消融仅恢复+2.25夏普差距中的+0.40，加入PPO而无库演化使差距恶化。两个指标是涌现而非设计的。

Conclusion: 封存联合搜索能有效避免过拟合和自确认偏差，在量化因子挖掘中显著提升样本外表现。限制包括：单种子运行、空头侧信号集中、目标为多空策略。

Abstract: Automated alpha mining holds the scoring function fixed and varies the search algorithm over it. A search that converges against a fixed scorer overfits whatever the scorer cannot penalize, a primary cause of the out-of-sample generalization gap. We treat the scoring function as a search artifact alongside the alpha factors and study what conditions make this joint search admissible. Sealed Joint Search (SJS) is a framework: a set of structural conditions on information flow in an autonomous-discovery system that prevent joint search from collapsing into self-confirmation while keeping the evaluator sealed. Conditions cover role decomposition, typed inter-role communication, provenance-sealed reads, versioned stores, and substrate-local promotion. Agora tests SJS empirically: five LLM agent classes communicate via three channels, evolving eight skill libraries, with alpha libraries built on AlphaGen operators. Three evaluators write reports aggregated into one brief, carrying forward disagreement instead of voting. We run Agora for 100 rounds on CSI 1000 and evaluate on a 91-day 2026 holdout sealed from all LLM inputs. Agora achieves holdout Sharpe +1.87; best baseline +1.334 at favorable seed and -0.755 cross-seed mean. Pre-loading Agora's two metrics into a frozen-library ablation recovers only +0.40 of the +2.25 Sharpe gap, and adding PPO without library evolution worsens the gap. The two metrics emerge rather than being designed. Caveats: single-seed run, short-side concentrated signal, intended for long-short.

</details>


### [221] [A Cognition-Emotion-Personality Framework for Modeling Human-Like Awareness and Behavior in Emergency Evacuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29212)
*Zoi Lygizou,Michalis Zervas,Helena G. Theodoropoulou,Vasilis Zafeiropoulos,Dimitris Kalles,Chairi Kiourt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成认知、情感、社会与人格机制的扩展疏散框架，通过事件确定性水平、记忆型出口知识、连续恐惧模型和OCEAN人格表征，更真实地模拟紧急疏散中的人类行为不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的疏散模拟多依赖完美事件感知、完整出口知识和完全理性决策等理想化假设，缺乏对认知局限、情感动态和人格差异的真实建模。

Method: 构建统一框架，包含：1) 基于连续事件确定性水平的动态感知机制；2) 基于记忆的出口知识表征（含获取、遗忘与回忆）；3) 连续恐惧模型（恐慌作为高密度状态）；4) OCEAN人格模型（神经质融入情感模型影响恐惧产生、升级、社会传染与恢复）；5) 个性化决策阈值捕捉行为异质性。

Result: 仿真实验表明，认知、情感和人格驱动过程显著影响疏散动力学，降低疏散效率，产生延迟、混乱、伤亡和社会影响行为等真实人群现象。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了更真实的人类行为表征，支持系统研究认知、情感、人格与人群动力学之间的交互作用。

Abstract: Agent-based evacuation simulations are widely used to study crowd behavior during emergencies, but many models rely on assumptions such as perfect event awareness, complete exit knowledge, and fully rational decision-making. This paper presents an extended evacuation framework that integrates cognitive, emotional, social, and personality-related mechanisms into a unified model of human behavior under uncertainty. The framework incorporates a dynamic event-awareness mechanism based on a continuous Event Certainty Level, a memory-based representation of exit knowledge subject to acquisition, forgetting, and recall, a continuous fear model in which panic emerges as a high-intensity state, and an OCEAN-based personality representation. Neuroticism is explicitly integrated into the emotional model, influencing fear generation, escalation, social contagion, and recovery. Behavioral heterogeneity is further captured through individualized decision thresholds that affect responses to perceived risk. The framework is evaluated through simulation experiments examining the effects of spatial familiarity, memory robustness, decision sensitivity, emotional dynamics, and personality variation. Results show that cognitive, emotional, and personality-driven processes substantially influence evacuation dynamics, reducing evacuation efficiency and generating realistic crowd phenomena such as delays, confusion, injuries, and socially influenced behaviors. The proposed framework provides a more realistic representation of human behavior in emergency evacuations and supports systematic investigation of the interactions between cognition, emotion, personality, and crowd dynamics.

</details>


### [222] [PolicyGuard: A Dialogue-Grounded Sub-Agent Verifier for Policy Adherence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29225)
*Seongjae Kang,Taehyung Yu,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出POLICYGUARD，一个子智能体验证器，通过完整对话上下文、策略自推理和对话级修复反馈，提升LLM代理在多轮交互中的策略遵循能力，在tau^2-BENCH基准上PASS4指标提升6-12个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将策略遵循视为外部检查的安全问题，但实际工作流涉及多轮交互、用户确认、前置阅读和对话内容依赖，现有验证方法（仅检查单个参数）无法满足这些需求。

Method: 提出POLICYGUARD子智能体验证器，三个关键能力：(i) 完整对话上下文，(ii) 对策略和当前对话进行自推理，(iii) 生成对话级可操作修复反馈，指导代理下一轮动作。

Result: 在tau^2-BENCH航空数据集上，针对三个供应商（GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Pro），每个设置四次试验，POLICYGUARD将PASS4提升+12.0/+6.0/+12.0个百分点。每次调用分析显示POLICYGUARD实现了更高的策略违规召回率，同时拦截频率约为参数级守卫的一半。

Conclusion: 策略遵循需要超越参数级检查的细粒度对话理解，POLICYGUARD通过共享对话视图、上下文推理和可操作反馈有效提升了代理的策略合规性。

Abstract: LLM agents handle user requests on behalf of organizations through tool calls and must follow the company policies stated in their system prompts. Prior work approaches this as a safeguarding problem -- external checks that block non-compliant agent actions. We argue that policy adherence is a broader problem: real workflows unfold across many turns, require explicit user confirmation and prerequisite reads, and hinge on the content of the dialogue rather than on any single argument value. Meeting this bar requires (i) full conversation context, (ii) self-reasoning over the policy and the current dialogue, and (iii) conversation-specific remediation that guides the agent's next turn -- three capabilities that prior safeguard work has often underestimated. We introduce POLICYGUARD, a sub-agent verifier that shares the agent's view of the dialogue, reasons over the policy in context, and provides actionable feedback for the agent's next turn. On tau^2-BENCH airline across three vendors (GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Pro) with four trials per setting, POLICYGUARD improves PASS4 by +12.0 / +6.0 / +12.0 pp. Per-call analyses show POLICYGUARD achieves higher policy-violation recall while blocking roughly half as often as argument-level guards.

</details>


### [223] [SurgVLA-Bench: Towards Evaluating Vision-Language-Action Models for Laparoscopic Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29247)
*Jiashuo Sun,Yue He,Wenxuan Liu,Tao Mao,Jiazheng Wang,Xiang Chen,Min Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SurgVLA-Bench是首个针对腹腔镜手术机器人的VLA模型综合评估基准，基于SurRoL仿真平台构建了从原子动作到完整手术的分层任务体系，并系统评估了自回归模型和流匹配模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA基准大多面向通用机器人领域，缺乏专门针对手术机器人场景的标准化评估平台，而手术机器人领域对VLA模型的评估需求日益增长。

Method: 基于SurRoL仿真平台构建分层任务分类体系（原子动作→完整手术流程），设计多维度评估框架（动作准确性和语义一致性），系统评估自回归模型（OpenVLA）和流匹配模型（π₀, π₀.₅, SmolVLA）两类代表性范式。

Result: 自回归模型在语义理解方面表现更优，而流匹配模型在任务精度方面更高但可能面临泛化权衡。然而，所有最佳模型的表现仍远未令人满意，内窥镜视野受限、视角限制和频繁遮挡是根本性的物理瓶颈。

Conclusion: SurgVLA-Bench为手术机器人VLA模型提供了首个标准化评估平台，揭示了当前模型在手术场景中的关键局限性，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models represent a promising direction for embodied intelligence in surgical robotics. Despite the prevalence of VLA benchmarks for general robotics, standardized evaluation platforms specifically designed for surgical contexts remain absent. To address this limitation, we present SurgVLA-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating VLA models in laparoscopic surgical robotics. Leveraging the SurRoL simulation platform, we construct a hierarchical task taxonomy ranging from atomic actions to complete surgical procedures, complemented by a multi-dimensional evaluation framework assessing action accuracy and semantic consistency. We then systematically evaluate two representative paradigms, including autoregressive models such as OpenVLA, and flow matching models such as $π_{0}$, $π_{0.5}$, and SmolVLA. Our experiments show that autoregressive models tend to excel in semantic understanding, while flow matching models often achieve higher task precision but may face generalization trade-offs. However, even the best-performing models remain far from satisfactory, as the constrained endoscopic field of view, restricted viewing angles, and frequent occlusions persist as fundamental physical bottlenecks. The code and data are available at https://github.com/VCL-HNU/SurgVLA

</details>


### [224] [When Summaries Distort Decisions: Information Fidelity in LLM-Compressed Financial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29251)
*Hoyoung Lee,Suhwan Park,Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Jaehoon Lee,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,CheolWon Na,Zhangyang Wang,Zach Golkhou,Minkyu Kim,Sotirios Sabanis,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Dhagash Mehta,Soonyoung Lee,Chanyeol Choi,Wonbin Ahn,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在金融领域进行上下文压缩时的信息保真度问题，发现压缩虽能保持流畅性和事实准确性，却可能改变基于原始信息的投资决策。作者提出了两种保真度损失模式（去语境化和模型依赖性），并设计了代理式上下文压缩方法，通过生成多个候选压缩并审计其与原始源的分歧来缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融决策者面临海量信息，需要上下文压缩来提高效率，但LLM在压缩金融原始材料时可能改变原始源所支持的投资判断。现有评估多关注压缩的效率和事实性，忽略了对决策相关性的保持能力。

Method: 通过金融文件和财报电话会议记录构建实验，分析LLM压缩导致的决策变化。识别两种保真度损失模式：去语境化（保留显著证据但脱离必要的限定条件）和模型依赖性（不同压缩器对同一源材料呈现不同视角）。提出Agentic Context Compression方法，生成多个候选压缩并审计其与原始源的分歧。

Result: LLM压缩虽然能生成流畅且事实合理的压缩上下文，但会改变下游决策。去语境化和模型依赖性是导致保真度损失的两种典型模式。提出的审计方法能有效检测和缓解压缩过程中的决策信息丢失问题。

Conclusion: 金融领域的上下文压缩评估不应仅关注效率和事实性，还应考察其对决策相关上下文的保持能力。代理式上下文压缩通过多重候选与分歧审计，为提升压缩保真度提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Financial decision-makers face more information than they can directly inspect, making context compression necessary. Yet when large language models (LLMs) compress financial source material, they can alter the investment judgment supported by the original source. We frame this problem as information fidelity: compression loses fidelity when it changes the decision induced by the source. In agentic systems, such losses may recur across intermediate steps and amplify throughout the decision process. Across financial filings and earnings-call transcripts, we find that LLM-based compression can produce fluent and factually plausible compressed contexts that nevertheless alter downstream decisions. We analyze two diagnostic patterns associated with fidelity loss: decontextualization, where salient evidence is retained but separated from the caveats and contextual qualifiers needed for correct interpretation, and model dependency, where different compressors expose different views of the same source. We then propose Agentic Context Compression, which generates multiple candidate compressions and audits their disagreements against the original source. Our results suggest that financial compression should be evaluated not only by efficiency or factuality, but also by its ability to preserve decision-relevant context.

</details>


### [225] [The Complexity Ceiling Benchmark: A Multi-Domain Evaluation of Sequential Reasoning Under Depth Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29278)
*Shubh Chapra,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Yash Sinha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CCB基准测试通过控制任务语义、仅改变推理深度（N=5~50），在三种结构不同的推理场景中评估LLM的推理衰减。发现几何级数的每步衰减模式，不同领域天花板差异显著：前两个任务强模型在N=50时仍保持pd>0.92，而第三个任务最佳模型在N=5时即崩溃（H0.5~4.7步）。约14.5%的正确答案通过错误的中间推理得出，强制详细状态追踪无法提升天花板，首次发散步数k*比参数量更能预测领域内准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估未能将任务语义内容与推理深度解耦，无法系统测量大语言模型在长时间推理链条中的性能衰减规律。

Method: 设计了CCB基准，固定语义内容仅变化推理深度N∈[5,50]，覆盖三种结构不同的推理场景（空间状态追踪、抽象符号指针操作、传递关系推理）；在6000次试验中测试五个前沿和开源LLM，引入几何衰减模型和TFBC痕迹级指标。

Result: 发现一致的几何级数每步衰减模式，但领域天花板差异巨大；14.5%正确答案来自错误中间推理；强制详细状态追踪无效（McNemar p=1.000）；首次发散步数k*比参数量更能预测准确率。

Conclusion: CCB和几何衰减模型可将模型的长程推理能力简化为每个任务族的一个可解释数字，揭示了当前模型在深层推理步骤中的系统性退化。

Abstract: We introduce the Complexity Ceiling Benchmark (CCB), a controlled evaluation of how language-model reasoning decays as the number of required sequential steps grows. CCB fixes the semantic content of a task and varies only its depth N in {5,...,50} across three structurally distinct regimes: grounded spatial state-tracking, abstract symbolic pointer manipulation, and transitive relational inference. Across 6,000 trials over five frontier and open-weight LLMs we find a consistent pattern of geometric per-step decay with widely separated domain ceilings: on the first two regimes the strongest models retain pd>0.92 across N=50; on the third every model collapses by N=5, with the best model's 50%-success horizon at H0.5~4.7 steps despite pd=0.863. A trace-level metric (TFBC) shows that 14.5% of correct answers across the benchmark are reached via incorrect intermediate reasoning. Forced verbose state-tracking does not move the ceiling (McNemar p=1.000), and the mean step at which reasoning first diverges, k*, predicts within-domain accuracy better than parameter count. CCB and the geometric decay model together reduce a model's long-horizon reasoning profile to one interpretable number per task family.

</details>


### [226] [Process Advantage Signal Shaping: A Paradigm-Agnostic Middleware for Process-Supervised RL in LLM Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29296)
*Chao Wang,Hongtao Tian,Tao Yang,Yunsheng Shi,Ting Yao,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PASS（过程优势信号塑造）中间件，用于修复GRPO在过程监督强化学习中存在的三种结构性问题：通道污染、分辨率不匹配和累积陷阱。PASS通过优势融合、按值分块和长度归一化三个组件，在两个领域和两种过程信号范式下均显著优于GRPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在过程监督强化学习中存在三个结构性病理：1) 通道污染：组标准化时混合了过程、结果和格式流；2) 分辨率不匹配：过程信号的粒度与被归因的逻辑决策粒度不一致；3) 累积陷阱：GRPO的return-to-go求和导致长度膨胀或截断探索。

Method: PASS中间件包含三个组件：1) Advantage Fusion：在组内独立标准化三个流；2) Chunk-by-Value：从信号本身导出值同质块并在块内广播信用；3) Divide-Length：将累积目标转换为平均价值密度分数。

Result: 在数学推理（学习型PRM）和多跳问答（策略蒸馏KL信号）两个领域，以及两种组标准化算子下，PASS在所有设置中均取得一致的pass@1增益，优于对应GRPO基线。

Conclusion: PASS作为一种紧凑中间件，在不改变核心GRPO算法框架的前提下，通过修复三个结构性问题，显著提升了过程监督强化学习的效果，验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is a default recipe for process-supervised reinforcement learning of LLM reasoners, and dense process supervision -- via learned process reward models (PRMs) or on-policy-distillation KL signals -- is a common way to densify its otherwise weak outcome reward. Layering such a step-level signal on top of GRPO's group-standardized advantage, however, exposes three structural pathologies: \emph{channel contamination} between the pooled process, outcome, and format streams at group standardization; \emph{resolution mismatch} between the granularity of the process signal and the granularity of the logical decisions being credited; and a \emph{cumulative trap} by which GRPO's return-to-go sum surfaces either length inflation or truncated exploration depending on the sign regime of the signal. We propose \textbf{PASS} (\emph{Process Advantage Signal Shaping}), a compact middleware that sits between any scalar step-level process signal and GRPO's clipped surrogate and addresses the three pathologies in turn: \emph{Advantage Fusion} standardizes the three streams independently within each group, \emph{Chunk-by-Value} derives value-homogeneous chunks from the signal itself and broadcasts credit within each chunk, and \emph{Divide-Length} converts the cumulative objective into an average-value-density score. We validate PASS across two domains and two process-signal paradigms -- a learned PRM on mathematical reasoning and an on-policy-distillation KL signal (with a generalized variant) on multi-hop question answering -- and under two group-standardization operators. In every regime PASS delivers a consistent pass@1 gain over the corresponding GRPO baseline.

</details>


### [227] [Hierarchical Experimentalist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29315)
*Abhranil Chandra,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Utsav Dhanuka,Varun Gandhi,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HExA框架，让LLM通过主动实验（而非依赖先验知识）在物理环境中迭代学习可复用的组合技能，在Interphyre基准上将Claude Sonnet 4.6的成功率从2%提升至77%，并能迁移到未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理依赖参数化知识、检索或搜索，在需要主动探索和实验的复杂物理系统及新颖领域中的长时任务上表现不佳，无法仅从先验知识中推导出答案。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Experimentalist Agents (HExA)，一种上下文内自我改进框架，通过迭代设计实验、从经验中学习可复用的组合技能库、整合实验证据来回答问题或采取行动。无训练、无外部监督、兼容黑盒模型。

Result: 在Interphyre基准上，Claude Sonnet 4.6基线仅2%成功率，HExA提升至77%；HExA同样提升了开源模型性能，优于ReAct和Reflexion等基线；仅用从较简单关卡学习的技能进行迁移，无需主动实验即可达44%成功率。

Conclusion: 通过主动实验学习能够帮助代理发现有用知识、获取可复用技能，并在新颖长时任务上取得高效进展，证明了主动实验范式在LLM代理中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to take actions in the real world and support human decision-making, yet most agents rely on parametric knowledge, fixed post-training data, retrieval, or search. This paradigm breaks down in novel domains and for sophisticated queries that cannot be answered from prior knowledge alone. Knowing the laws of physics, for instance, does not by itself enable LLMs to answer queries or complete long-horizon tasks in a complex physical system. To address this, we introduce Hierarchical Experimentalist Agents (HExA), an in-context self-improvement framework to learn from active experimentation. HExA iteratively designs and refines query-relevant experiments, learns a reusable library of composable skills from experience, and integrates experimental evidence to answer queries or take actions. HExA is training-free, compatible with any black-box model, and does not require external supervision, oracles, or offline data. To evaluate active experimentation, we introduce Interphyre, a tool-calling benchmark built on the PHYRE 2D procedural physics environment, where agents propose interventions and test hypotheses through simulation APIs. Experiments show that current LLM agents struggle in these settings, especially on the hardest levels of Interphyre. Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves only 2% success, while HExA improves the same model to up to 77% success. HExA also improves open-weight models and outperforms agentic baselines such as ReAct and Reflexion. Moreover, using only skills learned from easier levels and transferred without active experimentation, HExA achieves 44% success, demonstrating the reusability and generalization of its learned skills. Overall, HExA shows that learning through active experimentation can help agents discover useful knowledge, acquire reusable skills, and make efficient progress on novel long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [228] [PHF: Privileged Hidden Flow for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29340)
*Yuhan Li,Mingxu Zhang,Dazhong Shen,Ying Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Privileged Hidden Flow (PHF)方法，通过蒸馏特权教师模型的隐藏状态流动轨迹（而非仅输出分布）来改进自蒸馏训练，在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B上分别提升约+2.2、+1.5、+1.7个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy self-distillation (OPSD)方法仅监督输出分布，导致特权教师提供的上下文信息仅通过token级散度影响训练，未能直接监督产生该分布的内部计算过程。

Method: PHF在OPSD基础上额外对齐教师与学生隐藏状态在相同rollout上的token间转移方向和轨迹几何结构，而非逐点匹配隐藏向量；采用全层配方，包括相邻层关系计算，无需逐点隐藏状态模仿。

Result: 在100步训练计划下，PHF在Qwen3-1.7B、4B、8B上相比复现的OPSD基线分别提升约+2.2、+1.5、+1.7个Average@12指标点。

Conclusion: PHF作为OPSD的紧凑隐藏流扩展方案有效，其传输目标对共享轨迹偏移具有精确不变性，局部几何项对转移方向的正交变换也不变，消融实验证实其优于逐点隐藏匹配等替代方案。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a reasoning model on rollouts sampled from its own policy by matching a privileged teacher that also sees verified reference solutions. Existing OPSD objectives supervise only the output distribution, so privileged context affects training through a token-level divergence without directly supervising the internal computation that produced that distribution. We propose Privileged Hidden Flow (PHF), which additionally distills how a privileged teacher's hidden states move along the same rollout. Rather than forcing each student hidden vector to match the teacher vector at the same token position, PHF aligns token-to-token transition directions and trajectory geometry over selected generated positions. The all-layer recipe also includes an adjacent-layer relation computed from these same transitions, without pointwise hidden-state imitation. Under the same 100-step training schedule, PHF improves the Average@12 aggregate over our reproduced OPSD baseline on Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B, with observed gains of about +2.2, +1.5, and +1.7 points. The transport objective is exactly invariant to shared trajectory offsets; its local geometry term is also invariant to orthogonal transformations of transition directions. Ablations distinguish the fixed PHF recipe from pointwise hidden-state matching, single-channel transition losses, and layer-subset choices, supporting PHF as a compact hidden-flow extension to OPSD.

</details>


### [229] [When LLMs Develop Languages: Symbolic Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29354)
*Zhengqi Pei,Qingming Huang,Shuhui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLSR提出一种测试时框架，让多个LLM智能体自主发明和演化紧凑的符号语言框架（LSF），并通过路由器自适应组合以优化准确率与token消耗的权衡，相比标准CoT在保持准确率的同时减少3-6倍生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-Thought虽然提升了LLM在复杂推理任务上的表现，但其产生的长自然语言推理链在机器推理视角下效率低下，与高效机器推理的token成本需求不匹配。

Method: 提出Communicative Language Symbolism Routing (CLSR)框架，包含：(1) 多个LLM智能体自主发明、演化和共享紧凑的Language Symbolism Frameworks (LSFs)，每个LSF作为可复用的符号协议包含紧凑符号、使用规则和消息传递契约；(2) 基于正确性和token成本的进化循环优化LSF；(3) 无隐变量的路由器根据查询自适应选择单一低成本LSF、集成多个LSF或多轮LSF组合协议。

Result: 在多个挑战性基准上，相比标准CoT，CLSR将延迟导向的生成token减少3-6倍，同时保持准确率。此外，推导了任意符号体系下token成本的信息论下界，并在解释器可实现前提下证明多轮LSF协议有条件地包含程序执行流水线。

Conclusion: CLSR通过符号语言框架的自适应路由与进化优化，有效平衡了LLM推理的准确率与效率，为高效机器推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) improves large language models (LLMs) on difficult reasoning tasks, but it often incurs long natural-language rationales that are poorly aligned with efficient machine reasoning. We propose Communicative Language Symbolism Routing (CLSR), a test-time framework in which multiple LLM agents autonomously invent, evolve, and share compact Language Symbolism Frameworks (LSFs), while a latent-free router adaptively selects and composes these languages per query to optimize the accuracy-token trade-off. Unlike prompt optimization that refines surface instructions, CLSR treats each LSF as a reusable symbolic protocol with compact symbols, usage rules, and a message-passing contract, and improves it through an evolutionary loop driven by correctness and token cost. At inference time, the router may invoke a single low-cost LSF call, ensemble multiple LSFs, or execute a multi-round LSF composition protocol on harder queries. Across challenging benchmarks, CLSR reduces latency-oriented generated token completion by $3\sim 6\times$ compared to standard CoT while maintaining accuracy. We further derive an information-theoretic lower bound on token cost under arbitrary symbolism and show that, under an interpreter-realizability premise, multi-round LSF protocols conditionally subsume program-execution pipelines. Code is publicly available (https://github.com/pzqpzq/LSF_MDia).

</details>


### [230] [Diagnosing and Repairing Factual Errors in RAG under Budget Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29377)
*Soroush Hashemifar,Havva Alizadeh Noughabi,Fattane Zarrinkalam,Ali Dehghantanha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: D2R-RAG是一种模型无关且资源感知的检索增强生成框架，通过轻量级故障诊断和自适应修复策略，在有限计算预算下提升RAG系统的可靠性，并在FEVER和HotpotQA数据集上取得优于现有基线的准确率-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在实际部署中存在脆弱性，故障源于证据缺失、弱相关或生成内容未忠实反映检索结果。当前方法依赖微调、内部模型信号或资源不敏感的升级策略，在黑盒和预算受限场景下实用性有限。

Method: 提出D2R-RAG框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 轻量级故障诊断——从查询、检索证据和生成响应中提取可观察信号，推导可解释的故障签名；(2) 自适应修复——在显式延迟和显存约束下，从一组纠错动作中选择最优修复策略。

Result: 在FEVER和HotpotQA数据集上，D2R-RAG相比近期基线方法提升了可靠性，并在多个计算预算下实现了更好的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: D2R-RAG通过模型无关、资源感知的"诊断-修复"范式，有效解决了黑盒和预算受限场景下RAG系统的可靠性问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves the factuality of large language models by grounding responses in external evidence, yet real-world deployments remain fragile. Failures often stem from missing or weakly relevant evidence, as well as from generation that does not faithfully reflect the retrieved context. Many existing approaches rely on fine-tuning, privileged access to internal model signals, or resource-insensitive escalation strategies, which limits their practicality in black-box and budget-constrained settings. We propose D2R-RAG (Diagnose-to-Repair RAG), a model-agnostic and resource-aware framework that combines lightweight failure diagnosis with adaptive repair. D2R-RAG derives interpretable failure signatures from observable signals in the query, retrieved evidence, and generated response, and then selects from a small set of corrective actions under explicit latency and VRAM constraints. Experiments on FEVER and HotpotQA show that D2R-RAG improves reliability over recent baselines and achieves better accuracy--efficiency trade-offs across multiple compute budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/CyberScienceLab/D2R-RAG/.

</details>


### [231] [LLM-Guided Planning for Multi-hop Reasoning over Multimodal Nuclear Regulatory Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29399)
*Mingyu Jeon,Bokyeong Kim,Suwan Cho,Jae Young Suh,Yonggyun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM Agent的核监管文档审查系统，通过规划驱动的多跳推理在文档树上进行浏览、读取和搜索，并维护动态知识图谱作为状态。在NuScale最终安全分析报告的200个问题基准上达到81.5%准确率和0.93 RAGAS Faithfulness，核心性能优势来自规划机制（比无状态基线高38个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核监管文档审查需要跨数万页文档进行多跳推理，判断依赖于跨多个章节的证据整合，传统方法难以高效处理这种复杂检索与推理任务。

Method: 将任务建模为规划问题：LLM Agent基于已收集证据决定下一步访问的文档片段，在无向量化的文档树上使用browse、read、search三种工具操作，并维护动态知识图谱作为状态来追踪已收集证据。

Result: 在200个问题的基准测试中准确率达81.5%，RAGAS Faithfulness为0.93；比无状态基线PageIndex高38个百分点；优于LightRAG(73.0%)、HippoRAG(70.5%)、GraphRAG(49.5%)，与RAPTOR(75.5%)相当；边推理增加2.8倍成本但不提升准确率；7391条推断边中仅3条(0.04%)出现违规。

Conclusion: 规划是复杂文档审查任务中检索性能的主导因素；动态知识图谱可有效作为Agent状态；边推理可作为可审计的可追溯性模块而非推理增强手段。

Abstract: Reviewing nuclear regulatory documents requires multi-hop reasoning across tens of thousands of pages, where judgments depend on evidence assembled across multiple chapters. We frame this task as planning: an LLM-based agent observes the evidence collected so far, picks the next document fragment to inspect, and stops when the evidence is sufficient. The agent operates over a vectorless document tree using browse, read, and search tools, and maintains a dynamic knowledge graph (KG) as state. On a 200-question benchmark over NuScale Final Safety Analysis Report (FSAR) documents, the system reaches 81.5% accuracy with a RAGAS Faithfulness of 0.93. The dominant performance factor is planning: against PageIndex, which uses the same document tree without state-conditioned action selection, the gap is +38.0pp (43.5% to 81.5%, p<0.001). The system also outperforms LightRAG (73.0%, p<0.05), HippoRAG (70.5%, p<0.01), and GraphRAG (49.5%, p<0.001), and matches RAPTOR (75.5%, p=0.11) without offline indexing. Edge inference adds 2.8x cost without raising accuracy; we retain it as a traceability module. Of 7,391 inferred edges, 3 Violates edges (0.04%) flag scope boundaries (Q058) and partial conformance (Q176) as typed annotations that a human reviewer can audit.

</details>


### [232] [Mixture of Debaters: Learn to Debate at Architectural Level in Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29425)
*Dayong Liang,Kaisong Gong,Yi Cai,Changmeng Zheng,Xiao-Yong Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoD是一个统一框架，通过混合专家（MoE）范式在单一模型内实现动态自我辩论，解决传统多智能体辩论系统静态架构和高计算开销的问题，在多项多模态基准测试中以3.7倍更低延迟和87%更少token消耗取得更优准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有（multi-agent debate）框架存在两个关键局限：一是依赖静态架构，智能体角色和协调模式在设计时即固定；二是需要实例化多个模型副本，产生大量计算开销。作者希望突破这些限制，实现更高效、灵活的辩论式推理。

Method: 提出MoD框架，将混合专家（MoE）范式适配到辩证推理场景，核心包含三个技术：1）双路由（dual-routing），将角色分配与流程解耦，动态决定何时辩论、何时综合；2）动量切换（momentum switching），利用局部上下文平滑token级路由，减少专家切换抖动；3）统一自我辩论（unified self-debate），将不同辩论角色封装为轻量级专家模块，消除智能体间通信开销同时保留行为多样性。

Result: 在多模态基准测试上，MoD在准确率上超越单模型基线和传统多智能体系统，同时延迟降低3.7倍，token消耗减少87%。

Conclusion: MoD通过单一模型内的动态自我辩论机制，有效解决了多智能体辩论框架的效率和灵活性瓶颈，为高效辩证推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent debate frameworks suffer from two critical limitations: they rely on static architectures where agent roles and coordination patterns are fixed at design time, and they require instantiating multiple model copies, incurring substantial computational overhead. We propose Mixture of Debaters (MoD), a unified framework that enables dynamic self-debate within a single model by leveraging the Mixture-of-Experts paradigm. We address three key challenges in adapting MoE for dialectical reasoning: (1) dual-routing that decouples role allocation from process flow, dynamically determining when to debate versus when to synthesize; (2) momentum switching that smooths token-level routing with local context, reducing expert-switch jitter; and (3) unified self-debate that encapsulates diverse debating personas into lightweight expert modules, eliminating inter-agent communication while preserving behavioral diversity. Extensive experiments on multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that MoD outperforms both single-model baselines and conventional multi-agent systems, achieving superior accuracy with 3.7x lower latency and 87% reduction in token consumption.The source code can be accessed at https://github.com/YongLD/MoD.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Auditing LLM-Governed Social Robots with Culture-Specific Moral Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28345)
*Carmen Ng,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM驱动的社交机器人在多语言、多文化背景下的道德权衡行为，提出了一种基于梯度的审计框架。通过对8,000+篇论文的跨领域分析，构建了对称控制的优先级排序困境场景，并在四个LLM、四种语言、四种提示策略下进行了57,600次决策测试。研究发现，LLM存在持续的文化不对称梯度跟踪失败，西方语言决策质量显著优于中文和日文，多数优先场景的高度确定性抹除了跨文化梯度，提示策略效果不均且不可靠。研究呼吁将多语言、多元主义审计作为LLM-机器人部署前的必要环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM道德审计主要集中在英语场景，缺乏对具身化语境（如社交机器人）和多元文化规范的测试。随着LLM驱动的社交机器人在全球部署，不同文化对年龄、地位、群体规模的优先级规范差异巨大，未能进行多元校准可能导致资源分配的系统性不平等，存在紧迫的诊断空白。

Method: 基于9项跨领域社交机器人综述（8,000+篇论文），设计了对称控制的"先帮助谁"道德困境场景，涵盖关怀、教育、服务领域，保留"多数vs少数、年轻vs年长、高地位vs低地位"的身份权衡。在4个LLM、4个国家-语言对、4种提示策略下进行57,600次决策测试，以国家特定的道德机器实验（MME）偏好梯度为基准，采用序数一致性检验评估文化区分能力，并通过治理类型学映射梯度区分、方向倾向和审慎推理的脆弱性。

Result: LLM存在持续的文化不对称梯度跟踪失败：西方语言决策质量比中文和日语决策高出近两倍；多数优先的高度确定性抹除了跨文化梯度；对年龄和地位规范的敏感性部分失效，可能边缘化少数群体。提示策略效果不均，仅对比示例提示带来一致改进，纯推理提示反而可能恶化跟踪效果。

Conclusion: 当前LLM无法可靠地实现多元文化校准，且提示工程无法有效弥补。研究建议将多语言、多元主义审计作为LLM-机器人部署前的必要门控环节，并提出模型层面的改进比提示策略更能从根本上解决问题。

Abstract: LLM-governed social robots increasingly decide who receives real-world assistance first. As prioritization norms vary across cultures by age, status, and group size, failure to calibrate pluralistically can scale into unequal access. Yet LLM moral audits remain English-centered, rarely test embodied contexts, leaving pluralistic calibration as an urgent diagnostic gap amid intensifying LLM-robot deployment. We introduce a gradient-based audit framework for multilingual evaluation of LLM moral trade-off behavior against cultural preference gradients. Grounded in nine cross-domain social robotics reviews (>8,000 papers), we derive symmetry-controlled scenarios across care, education, and services, translating the Moral Machine Experiment's "whom to spare" into "whom to assist first" dilemmas with preserved identity trade-offs (many vs. few; young vs. old; higher vs. lower status). We audit four LLMs across four country-language pairs in four prompting regimes (57,600 decisions), benchmarked against country-specific MME preference gradients. Ordinal concordance tests whether models differentiate cultural contexts; a governance typology maps vulnerabilities in gradient differentiation, directional tendency, and deliberation. We find persistent, culturally asymmetric gradient tracking failures that prompting alone cannot reliably correct: quality calibration is nearly twice as strong for Western-language decisions as for Chinese and Japanese; high determinism in majority-first trade-offs often erases cross-cultural gradients; partial sensitivity to age- and status-based norms risks sidelining minorities. Prompting effects are uneven; only contrastive exemplars yield consistent gains, while reasoning-only prompts can worsen tracking. Our results motivate multilingual, pluralistic audits as an LLM-robot pre-deployment gate and suggest model factors are a more robust lever than prompting alone.

</details>


### [234] [A Query-Driven Communication-Efficient Digital Twins Design for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28384)
*Nuocheng Yang,Longyu Zhou,Sihua Wang,Changchuan Yin,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种查询驱动的数字孪生架构，通过主动请求所需环境数据替代传统实时状态同步，在降低通信开销40%的同时将自动驾驶规划位置误差减少24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数字孪生方法依赖车辆实时状态同步，构建高保真数字孪生时会产生大量冗余数据，导致不可接受的计算和通信消耗。

Method: 1) 提出查询驱动的数字孪生架构，使DT能基于仿真结果主动向车辆请求所需环境数据；2) 构建考虑DT保真度和通信约束的优化问题，以最小化自动驾驶位置误差为目标；3) 设计跨时间步进式查询机制以进一步提升通信效率。

Result: 相比传统方法，规划位置误差降低24%，通信开销减少40%。

Conclusion: 查询驱动方法能有效平衡数字孪生保真度与通信效率，适用于自动驾驶等确定性高可靠性场景。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) have become a potential technology to perform risk-free simulation of physical entities for deterministic and high-reliability services in diverse scenarios such as autonomous driving and low-altitude economy. In the autonomous driving scenario, traditional DT methods that rely solely on vehicle's real-time state synchronization, however, might lead to unacceptable computing and communication consumption for construction of high-fidelity DT with redundant data. To address this issue, we first propose a query-driven DT architecture to enable the DT to actively request the desired environment data from vehicles based on its simulation result. Then, we formulate an optimization problem whose goal is to minimize autonomous driving position error while accounting for DT fidelity and communication constraints. We also design a cross-time-step progressive query mechanism to further improve communication efficiency. The simulation results show that our proposed method achieves a 24% reduction in planning position error compared to traditional methods, while reducing communication overhead by 40%.

</details>


### [235] [RoboGaze: Evaluating Robot World Models via Structured Vision-Language Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28385)
*Minh-Loi Nguyen,Nghiem Tuong Diep,Hung Khang Nguyen,Minh Le,Doanh Le Thien,Hoang H. Tran,Dung D. Le,Vu N. Duong,Daniel Sonntag,An Thai Le,Duy Minh Ho Nguyen,Vien Anh Ngo,Tran Van Nhiem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboGaze 是一个无需训练的、基于多智能体 VLM 的框架，用于对机器人操作生成视频进行结构化、可解释的评估。它通过三阶段流水线（任务-场景对齐、维度特定专家路由、评判验证）输出细粒度的故障报告，在包含 382 个视频片段的数据集上显著优于零样本基线，将描述 F1 提升高达 43 个点，并将干净视频的准确率从低于 25% 提升至超过 80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人世界模型生成的合成视频虽然在视觉上逼真，但常违反物理规律、时间一致性和任务逻辑。现有评估方法（传统指标和单一 VLM 评判）难以泛化或提供精确的诊断价值，因此需要一种结构化、可解释的评估工具。

Method: RoboGaze 是一个多智能体 VLM 框架，采用三阶段流水线：(1) 任务-场景对齐，将任务指令与视频内容对齐；(2) 维度特定专家路由，针对 6 个维度（30 种类型）的故障类型由专门专家模型进行检测；(3) 基于评判的验证，缓解误报问题。无需额外训练。

Result: 在 382 个视频片段（模拟和真实世界多视角操作）的数据集上，使用 8 种开源和闭源 VLM 骨干，RoboGaze 将描述 F1 提升高达 43 个点，时序对齐（F1 × IoU）提升高达 37 个点，缩小了约 85% 与人类评分之间的差距。干净视频准确率从低于 25% 提升至超过 80%。

Conclusion: RoboGaze 提供了一种可扩展、高可解释性的诊断工具，用于机器人世界模型的严格评估，有效解决了现有评估方法的泛化性差和误报问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot world models enable synthetic video generation for embodied prediction and planning. However, evaluating these videos is challenging: visually realistic outputs often violate physical laws, temporal consistency, or task logic, while conventional metrics and monolithic Vision-Language Model (VLM) judges fail to generalize or provide precise diagnostic value. We present RoboGaze, a training-free, multi-agent VLM framework that provides structured, interpretable evaluation for generated robot-manipulation videos. Given a task instruction and video, RoboGaze operates via a three-stage pipeline: task-scene grounding, dimension-specific specialist routing, and critic-based verification. It outputs temporally localized glitch reports categorized under a novel 6-dimension, 30-type robotics-specific taxonomy. To benchmark RoboGaze, we introduce a human-validated dataset of 382 clips spanning simulated and real-world multi-view manipulation. Evaluating eight open-source and proprietary VLM backbones, RoboGaze dramatically outperforms zero-shot baselines, improving description-F1 by up to +43 points and temporal alignment (F1 x IoU) by up to +37 points, closing approximately 85% of the gap to the human ceiling. Furthermore, its critic verifier mitigates the "cry-wolf" false-positive flaw of standard VLMs, lifting clean-clip accuracy from under 25% to over 80%. RoboGaze offers a scalable, highly interpretable diagnostic tool for the rigorous evaluation of robot world models.

</details>


### [236] [Event-Conditioned Diagnostics of Kinematic, Contact, and Object-Permanence Fields in Passive Object-State World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28455)
*Yang Liu,Yuming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受控诊断协议，用于研究被动目标状态世界模型中的事件条件化潜在物理结构。通过平衡的受控生成器数据集（包含自由运动、碰撞和遮挡事件），评估了循环、注意力及潜在状态空间转移模型。结果显示，隐藏状态支持可靠的事件状态读取，事件上下文系统地重新加权运动学、接触和物体恒存性场读取，且抑制场对齐方向会降低事件相关预测的性能。这支持了事件条件化组织和固定时间视野的功能敏感性，但未表明存在显式物理模块或上下文不变的滑动窗口泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型能预测未来物理状态，但预测精度本身并不能解释物理信息如何在潜在动力学中被组织和利用。本文旨在揭示潜在动态中物理信息的组织方式和功能作用。

Method: 提出受控诊断协议，使用平衡的受控生成器数据集（自由运动、碰撞、遮挡事件），评估循环、注意力和潜在状态空间转移模型，通过事件状态读取、物理场重新加权分析、时间对齐和方向一致性分析，以及固定时间视野投影因果场效应（CFE）等方法。

Result: 模型学到有用的预测动力学，隐藏状态支持可靠的事件状态读取；事件上下文系统地重新加权运动学、接触和物体恒存性场读取；时间对齐和方向一致性分析显示相位相关的场重点转移；CFE表明抑制场对齐方向会降低事件相关预测性能，接触对齐结构在碰撞-接触窗口中证据最强，物体恒存性对齐结构在硬遮挡隐藏窗口中有较有条件的证据。

Conclusion: 结果支持事件条件化组织和固定时间视野的功能敏感性，但未表明存在显式物理模块、隔离的因果回路或上下文不变的滑动窗口泛化。

Abstract: World models can predict future physical states, but prediction accuracy alone does not explain how physical information is organized and used inside their latent dynamics. We introduce a controlled diagnostic protocol for studying event-conditioned latent physical structure in passive object-state world models. The protocol tests whether hidden representations encode event-regime information, whether event contexts reweight non-exclusive physical field readouts, and whether field-aligned representational components have functional consequences for prediction. Using a balanced controlled-generator dataset with free-motion, collision, and occlusion events, we evaluate recurrent, attention-based, and latent state-space transition models under a fixed-horizon forecasting setup. The models learn useful predictive dynamics and their hidden states support reliable event-regime readout. Event contexts systematically reweight kinematic, contact, and object-permanence field readouts: free motion is kinematic-dominant, collision combines kinematic and contact structure, and occlusion combines motion-related and object-permanence structure. Time-aligned and directional-consistency analyses further show phase-related shifts in field emphasis. Finally, fixed-horizon projection causal field effect (CFE) shows that suppressing field-aligned directions can degrade event-relevant prediction, with strongest evidence for contact-aligned structure in collision-contact windows and more qualified evidence for object-permanence-aligned structure in hard-occlusion hidden windows. These results support event-conditioned organization and fixed-horizon functional sensitivity of latent physical fields, while not implying explicit physical modules, isolated causal circuits, or context-invariant sliding-window generalization.

</details>


### [237] [When May I Help You? On The Effect of Proactivity on Group Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28469)
*Thomas Vitry,Vanessa Maeder,Kieran Edgeworth,Asihati Hazaiti,Doga Deniz Ates,Connor Gäde,Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Dennis Becker,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究在多人人类-机器人协作的密室逃脱任务中，比较了反应式（仅在被叫时回应）与主动式（持续监听、自主贡献并定期重新发起互动）两种机器人交互模型。主动模型显著增加了互动频率，但反应模型在成功率上更高（92.86% vs 71.42%），且效果受用户先前经验（如LLM经验、机器人经验）和人格特质（如内向性）的调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人协作中人机交互中机器人主动性的设计挑战：主动贡献可提供及时支持但可能干扰协调与话轮转换；等待被叫可保持人类控制但可能错失协助机会。

Method: 在协作密室逃脱环境中，让参与者成对与类人机器人交互，分别采用反应式模型（仅在被叫时回应）和主动式模型（持续监听、自主贡献、定期重新发起互动），通过谜题解决表现、互动频率及Godspeed和RoSAS量表评分进行评估。

Result: 主动模型显著增加互动频率；反应模型具有描述性更高的成功率（92.86% vs 71.42%）。有LLM经验的参与者在反应条件下解谜更快；有机器人经验的参与者和内向型参与者对两种模型的评价出现差异。

Conclusion: 机器人主动性的效果受用户先前经验、人格特质以及群体需求的共同调节，表明设计需考虑个体差异和情境因素。

Abstract: Robot initiative is a central challenge in multi-party human-robot collaboration. A robot that contributes without being addressed may provide timely support, but it may also disrupt coordination, divide attention, or interrupt turn-taking; a robot that waits to be addressed may preserve human control, but it may also miss opportunities to assist. We investigate this design challenge in a collaborative escape room in which pairs of participants work with a humanoid robot under either a reactive interaction model, where the robot responds only when addressed, or a proactive model, where it listens continuously, contributes autonomously, and periodically re-initiates interaction. We evaluate both models using puzzle-solving performance, interaction frequency, and participant ratings on the Godspeed and RoSAS scales. The proactive model substantially increases interaction frequency, whereas the reactive model shows a descriptively higher overall success rate (92.86% vs. 71.42%). The strongest differences emerge when prior experience and personality are taken into account: participants with LLM experience solve the early puzzles faster in the reactive condition, and participants with prior robot experience show modified evaluations of proactive and reactive interaction as do introverted participants. These findings demonstrate that the effects of robot initiative are simultaneously shaped by users' prior experience, personality traits and more generally by the needs of the group.

</details>


### [238] [Improvement of Robot's Simultaneous Localization and Mapping Using an Effective Transformation to Achieve Linear Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28475)
*Seyed Farzad Bahreinian,Maziar Palhang,Mohammad Reza Taban,Hasan Enami Eraghi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性模型卡尔曼滤波（LMKF）的SLAM方法，通过引入简单罗盘和有效变换将非线性模型线性化，从而解决了EKF-SLAM因非线性误差导致的发散问题，在精度、收敛性和计算复杂度上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EKF-SLAM存在发散问题，主要原因是运动模型和观测模型的非线性以及线性化误差，现有方法效果有限，需要更稳定的解决方案。

Method: 引入简单罗盘（compass）并应用有效变换，将非线性状态空间模型转换为线性模型，然后对线性模型应用标准卡尔曼滤波（KF），从而得到LMKF SLAM方法。

Result: LMKF SLAM在精度、收敛性和计算复杂度上显著优于EKF-SLAM等现有方法，对传感器值不确定性和系统参数变化具有更高的稳定性。

Conclusion: LMKF SLAM通过线性化处理从根本上解决了EKF-SLAM的发散问题，是一种更优的SLAM方案，实验结果验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Nowadays mobile robots have wide engineering applications. Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) is an important task of these robots. The major and common algorithms used for this task are based on extended Kalman filter (EKF). One of the main problems in EKF-based SLAM is its divergence. The nonlinearity of motion and observation models and linearization error are the main reasons for the divergence. There have been some efforts to address this problem with limited success. In this paper, by applying a simple compass and using an effective transformation, we transform the non-linear state space model into a linear model. Then, by applying the original KF to this model, we reach a new method, which is called LMKF SLAM. We show that the LMKF SLAM is significantly superior to the state-of-the-art methods, especially EKF-based SLAMs, both in accuracy, convergence, and computational complexity. The proposed method is also more stable with respect to the uncertainty of sensors values and changes in system parameters. Experimental results verify these points.

</details>


### [239] [FADA: Few-Shot Domain Adaptation via Dynamics Alignment for Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28476)
*Angchen Xie,Nikhil Sobanbabu,Ishayu Shikhare,Alan Wang,Max Simchowitz,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FADA（Few-Shot Domain Adaptation via Dynamics Alignment），一种三阶段规划器-逆动力学模型（Planner-IDM）框架，用于人形机器人的少样本域适应。该方法通过冻结规划器、仅微调IDM（约2分钟目标域数据），利用标准监督学习对齐动力学，在动力学偏移场景下优于上下文适应和端到端适应基线，并在真实人形机器人上实现了多样化高精度全身任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度人形控制受限于目标域动力学失配问题——在地形、负载或执行器响应变化下，相同的控制目标会诱发不同的实际运动。现有方法要么追求零样本迁移（域随机化）或上下文适应（缺乏目标域特化），要么需要大量适应流程（模型标定、残差学习或策略重训练）。

Method: FADA采用三阶段Planner-IDM框架：1）训练一个具有特权信息的oracle策略；2）通过DAgger将oracle行为蒸馏为可部署的Planner-IDM学生模型；3）部署时冻结规划器，仅使用约2分钟目标域 rollout 的标准监督学习微调IDM，无需最优示范或奖励信号，直接利用 rollout 中观测到的动作-观测对作为监督。

Result: 实验表明，FADA在动力学偏移下任务性能优于上下文适应和端到端适应基线，并在真实人形机器人上成功执行了多种高精度全身任务。

Conclusion: FADA通过少样本动力学对齐，以轻量级微调方式实现了人形机器人在目标域中的高效适应，为高精度控制提供了一种实用的域适应方案。

Abstract: High-precision humanoid control is limited by target-domain dynamics mismatch, where the same control objective can induce different realized motions under changes in terrain, payload, or actuator response. Existing methods either pursue zero-shot transfer through domain randomization or in-context adaptation without target-domain specialization, or require heavy adaptation pipelines that leverage target-domain data, such as model calibration, residual learning, or policy retraining. In this paper, we present FADA (Few-Shot Domain Adaptation via Dynamics Alignment), a three-stage Planner-Inverse Dynamics Model (Planner-IDM) framework for few-shot adaptation in humanoid control. FADA first trains an oracle policy with privileged information and then distills the oracle behavior into a deployable Planner-IDM student through DAgger. At deployment, FADA freezes the planner and finetunes only the IDM using approximately 2 minutes of target-domain rollouts with standard supervised learning. Rather than requiring optimal demonstrations or rewards, FADA uses the paired actions and observations that are observed during these rollouts as supervision, aligning the IDM's action generation with target-domain dynamics. Experiments show that FADA outperforms both in-context and end-to-end adaptation baselines, improving task performance under dynamics shifts and enabling real humanoid robots to execute diverse high-precision whole-body tasks. Implementation details and qualitative hardware rollout videos are available at https://lecar-lab.github.io/FADA-humanoid/.

</details>


### [240] [The Speedup Paradox: Rethinking Inference Speed-Quality Trade-off in Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28529)
*Yujin Wang,Junli Chen,Yixuan Li,Shunan Dong,Huazhong Yang,Yongpan Liu,Hongyang Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TISED框架，用于分析有损推理优化技术在具身任务中的影响，发现了三个反直觉现象：静态任务中优化可能延长完成时间、动态任务中适度优化可提升成功率、硬件配置影响优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效推理研究主要关注静态ML任务的单步计算成本和延迟，缺乏对具身任务中闭环执行特性的分析，未能充分刻画推理优化在具身任务层面的综合影响。

Method: 提出TISED分析框架，统一量化、剪枝、异步推理等有损优化技术，将效果分解为静态任务和动态任务两个维度进行系统分析。

Result: 发现三个反直觉结果：(1)静态任务中优化虽降低单步延迟却可能延长总完成时间；(2)动态任务中适度有损优化可提升任务成功率；(3)优化效果的单调性和最优区间随硬件配置变化。

Conclusion: 为适应具身任务特点调整推理优化技术提供了新视角，指出需要重新审视传统高效推理方法在具身场景中的适用性。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models have recently been widely used to improve robot generalization and task success rates. Previous works apply lossy efficient-inference techniques such as quantization, pruning, and asynchronous inference, accepting small action quality degradation in exchange for lower per-step computation cost and inter-action latency. However, unlike traditional static ML tasks, embodied tasks involve repeated interaction with the environment, and task-level performance is determined not only by per-step cost, but also by closed-loop effects unique to embodied execution, which remain insufficiently characterized in current efficient-inference studies. In this work, we propose TISED (\underline{T}ask-level \underline{I}nference \underline{S}peedup \underline{E}ffect \underline{D}ecomposition), an analytical framework that unifies diverse lossy inference optimization techniques and decomposes their effects on static and dynamic tasks, and uncovers some paradoxical effects on task-level performance: (1) on \textit{static tasks}, optimization sometimes can lengthen end-to-end per-task completion time even as per-step latency drops; (2) on \textit{dynamic tasks}, moderate lossy optimization can raise task success rate even above the baseline; and (3) the monotonicity and sweet-spot location of both effects can shift with hardware configuration. Together, our findings provide a new perspective on adapting inference optimization techniques to embodied tasks.

</details>


### [241] [Robotic Arm-Based Spectral Sensing for Strawberry Positioning and Non-Destructive Sweetness Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28555)
*Yi Yang,Mark Cardamis,Wen Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于机械臂的草莓光谱传感系统，集成了YOLOv11s检测、RGB-ToF标定、手眼标定和闭环趋近策略，实现了草莓的非破坏性甜度评估，端到端成功率达88.10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统甜度评估方法依赖破坏性采样且难以规模化，亟需一种非破坏性、可扩展的甜度评估方案用于农业品质控制。

Method: 系统采用YOLOv11s实时检测草莓，通过RGB-ToF标定和掩码-深度对齐实现几何一致的目标定位；设计了定制化眼在手手眼标定流程估计夹爪与相机间的刚体变换；并采用基于航路点的搜索和增量式闭环趋近策略，将传感器定位至最佳工作距离进行甜度感知。

Result: 端到端42次试验成功率88.10%，检测率95.24%，目标检测后趋近成功率100%。手眼标定对比显示Park方法跨样本一致性优于Andreff方法。失败主要集中在甜度估计阶段的有效区域提取环节。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了RGB-ToF感知、机械臂操作与非破坏性传感集成的可行性，为草莓品质评估提供了可扩展的基线方案，并为未来集成视觉-语言-动作模型等学习策略奠定了基础。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of sweetness is essential for quality control in agriculture, yet conventional methods rely on destructive sampling and are difficult to scale. This thesis presents a robotic arm-based spectral sensing system for strawberry detection, localization, approach, and non-destructive sweetness estimation.
  The system integrates perception, calibration, and robotic control in a closed-loop pipeline. A YOLOv11s detector is adopted for real-time strawberry detection, while RGB-ToF calibration and mask-to-depth alignment are used to obtain geometrically consistent target localization. A custom eye-in-hand hand-eye calibration workflow is developed to estimate the rigid transform between gripper_link and cam_front, enabling reliable transformation of fruit targets into the robot base frame. Based on these estimates, the robot executes a waypoint-based search and an incremental closed-loop approach strategy to position the sensor at optimal working distance for sweetness sensing.
  Experimental results show strong end-to-end performance (88.10% success over 42 trials), with robust detection (95.24%) and successful approach execution once a target is detected (100% conditional success). Hand-eye calibration comparisons indicate that although Andreff yields the smallest translation norm in single-run results, the Park method provides better cross-sample consistency and therefore more stable downstream robot behavior. The residual failures are concentrated in the sensing stage, especially valid-region extraction for sweetness estimation under difficult depth/reflectance conditions.
  Overall, this work demonstrates the feasibility of integrating RGB-ToF perception, robotic manipulation, and non-destructive sensing for practical strawberry quality assessment, and provides a scalable baseline for future integration of learning-based policies such as Vision-Language-Action models.

</details>


### [242] [Embodiment Meets Environment: Toward Context-Aware, Safe Physical Caregiving Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28592)
*Zhanxin Wu,Ruofei Tong,Jiaying Fang,Tapomayukh Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出E²-CARE框架，通过将护理技能表示为可在线重塑交互模板，并利用统一的3D动态场景图显式建模环境、机器人和人的交互上下文，实现跨不同环境和机器人形态的零样本、安全且自适应的物理护理任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理护理机器人系统大多紧密耦合于特定环境和机器人形态，未能显式建模或约束与人的交互，缺乏对由环境与机器人形态共同涌现的上下文的适应性。

Method: 将基本护理技能表示为交互模板，在线重塑其执行；通过统一3D动态场景图显式建模环境、机器人和人的交互上下文；合成任务特定约束以在运行时约束技能执行。

Result: 在数百个模拟家庭环境及多种机器人形态上评估了四种日常生活活动，并在真实环境中通过两种机器人的两项护理任务进行了用户研究，结果展示了一致且成功的适应性。

Conclusion: E²-CARE框架能够实现跨不同环境和机器人形态的零样本安全复用与自适应，有效应对物理护理中的上下文变化挑战。

Abstract: Physical caregiving robots need to assist different users with different tasks in diverse environments, and they come in many embodiments. While substantial progress has been made on individual caregiving tasks, most existing systems remain tightly coupled to specific environments and robot embodiments, and often do not explicitly model or constrain interactions around people, despite humans being special agents in the environment. This motivates a focus on adapting to context that emerges from the joint interaction between the environment and the robot's embodiment. We propose $E^2$-CARE, a framework that enables context-aware adaptation by representing primitive caregiving skills as interaction templates whose execution is reshaped online. $E^2$-CARE represents the environment, the robot, and the human within a unified 3D dynamic scene graph that models these interaction contexts explicitly, and synthesizes task-specific constraints to govern how each skill is executed. By enforcing these constraints at runtime, the same skill templates can be reused zero-shot and safely across diverse environments and robot embodiments. We evaluate $E^2$-CARE across four activities of daily living in hundreds of simulated household environments, including assistive home settings, and across diverse robot embodiments, and validate it through user studies on two caregiving tasks with two robots in various real-world environments. Results demonstrate consistent and successful adaptation across these environments and embodiments. Website: https://emprise.cs.cornell.edu/e2care

</details>


### [243] [Fast and Accurate Outlier-Aware LiDAR Super-Resolution for SLAM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28607)
*Christos Anagnostopoulos,Alexandros Gkillas,Nikos Piperigkos,Aris S. Lalos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度展开的超分辨率模型，通过集成离群点去除模块，在保证实时性能的同时提升低分辨率LiDAR传感器的点云质量，从而显著改善SLAM中的位姿估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低分辨率LiDAR传感器在SLAM应用中存在点云稀疏、结构信息不足的问题，导致位姿估计精度受限；而现有超分辨率方法计算开销大，难以满足实时性要求。

Method: 提出一种Deep Unrolling-based Super-Resolution (SR)模型，集成离群点去除模块以保证结构完整性；采用基于模型的优化方法，在重建高分辨率点云的同时最小化计算开销。

Result: 在LiDAR SLAM框架中评估，该SR模型相比现有最先进超分辨率方法，在位姿估计精度和效率上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度展开超分辨率模型能有效增强低分辨率LiDAR传感器在SLAM中的表现，兼顾精度与实时性，为低成本LiDAR在SLAM中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This work tackles the challenge of enhancing low-resolution LiDAR sensors for SLAM applications through a novel Deep Unrolling-based Super-Resolution (SR) model. We integrate an outlier removal module to ensure structural integrity while maintaining real-time performance. By leveraging a model-based optimization approach, our method efficiently reconstructs high-resolution point clouds while minimizing computational overhead. The proposed SR model is evaluated within a LiDAR SLAM framework, demonstrating significant improvements in pose estimation accuracy and efficiency compared to state-of-the-art SR methods.

</details>


### [244] [When Stopping Fails: Rethinking Minimal Risk Conditions through Human-Interactive Autonomous Driving for Safe Transportation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29115)
*Yash Tandon,Giovanni Tapia Lopez,Marcus Blennemann,Mohan Trivedi,Ross Greer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了自动驾驶汽车（AV）在真实部署中因停车等被动安全策略引发的交通阻塞、应急干扰和可达性问题，提出当前安全框架缺乏理解人类权威、响应多模态指令和适应动态社会交通的能力，主张AV安全应从被动避险转向人机交互式自主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的AV安全框架主要围绕碰撞避免和最小风险条件（MRC）行为（如不确定时停车）设计，但实际部署表明停车行为并不足以确保AV安全融入人类主导的交通系统，导致了交通阻塞、干扰应急响应和通行障碍等问题，因此需要重新审视现有安全范式的不足。

Method: 对公共记录和市政报告中涉及AV停车行为及人机交互失败的公开事件进行分析，按照感知、规划和控制三个方面的架构限制对事件进行分类，并识别现有安全范式中的关键缺陷，最后综述支持人机交互感知、语言引导与可达性感知规划以及远程辅助控制等新兴研究方向。

Result: 通过分类分析，识别出当前AV安全框架在解读人类权威、响应多模态指令以及适应动态社会交通条件方面存在关键能力缺失，并指出人机交互式感知、语言引导的规划、远程辅助控制等方向有望弥补这些不足。

Conclusion: 可靠的AV城市部署需要超越被动避险策略，转向人机交互式自主，增强AV与人类主体及基础设施协作的能力。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) are increasingly deployed in urban environments, yet their safety frameworks remain primarily designed around collision avoidance and minimal risk condition (MRC) behaviors such as slowing or stopping when uncertainty arises. Although effective in reducing immediate crash risk, real-world deployments indicate that stopping alone does not guarantee safe integration into human-governed roadway systems. Incidents reported by municipalities and public records show that AV fallback behaviors can obstruct traffic, interfere with emergency response operations, and create accessibility challenges for passengers and pedestrians. This paper presents an analysis of publicly documented incidents involving AV stopping behavior and human-AV interaction failures. We categorize these incidents according to limitations in perception, planning, and control within current AV architectures. Using this taxonomy, we identify key gaps in existing safety paradigms, particularly the lack of mechanisms for interpreting human authority, responding to multimodal instructions, and adapting to dynamic, socially regulated traffic conditions. We then review emerging research directions that support human-interactive perception, language-grounded and accessibility-aware planning, and assisted control through remote guidance and teleoperation. The analysis highlights the need to augment current AV safety frameworks with capabilities that enable cooperative interaction with human agents and infrastructure. These findings suggest that reliable urban deployment of AVs requires moving beyond passive fallback strategies toward human-interactive autonomy.

</details>


### [245] [PinNet: Keypoint-Aware Learned Local Descriptors with Geometric Embedding for Loop Closure in LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28637)
*Yanlong Ma,Nakul S. Joshi,Christa S. Robison,Philip R. Osteen,Brett T. Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PinNet是一个基于点云的局部几何描述子神经网络，通过平面几何自注意力机制增强关键点间空间关系建模，在多种LiDAR数据集上实现了强地点识别、精确姿态估计和单次定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖几何信息（如LiDAR）进行回环检测具有挑战性，因为难以构建具有判别性的几何特征。现有方法在生成区分性强的局部描述子方面存在不足，需要更有效的方案来减少大规模环境建图中的漂移并构建全局一致地图。

Method: 提出PinNet网络，包含两个核心组件：(1) 神经网络生成关键点及其对应的局部几何描述子；(2) 基于平面的几何自注意力模块，通过对关键点间空间关系建模来增强描述子的判别性，用于回环检测和点云配准。

Result: 在多种不同LiDAR传感器采集的数据集上进行了全面评估，实验结果表明PinNet在地点识别、相对位姿估计和单次定位任务中均表现优异。

Conclusion: PinNet通过几何自注意力机制有效提升了点云描述子的判别能力，在回环检测和点云配准任务中取得了优秀性能，证明了该方法在不同环境和传感器下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Loop closure is essential to reduce drift and build globally consistent maps in large-scale environments. However, reliable loop closure with only geometric information from, e.g., a LiDAR sensor, remains challenging due to the difficulty of constructing discriminative geometric features. We present PinNet, a neural network that produces local geometric descriptors from point clouds for place recognition and scanto-scan registration. PinNet incorporates a neural network that generates keypoints and their corresponding descriptors, together with a plane-based geometric self-attention module that models inter-keypoint spatial relationships to enhance descriptor discriminability for loop-closure detection and point-cloud registration. The approach is comprehensively evaluated on multiple datasets collected with different LiDAR sensors. Experimental results demonstrate strong place-recognition performance, precise relative pose estimation, and successful single-shot localization in different environments.

</details>


### [246] [J-LAW: Joint Localization and Actionable World Modeling via Coupled Latent Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28712)
*Guanqun Cao,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: J-LAW提出了一种联合因子图框架，将经典SLAM的度量位姿估计与条件世界模型的潜在动力学统一优化，通过位姿-潜在耦合因子实现定位与可行动世界模型的相互增强，在PushT和WildGS数据集上显著降低了潜在预测误差和轨迹漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SLAM生成度量位姿和几何地图，但缺乏用于规划的可行动预测模型；而条件世界模型学习紧凑潜在动力学用于规划，却忽略全局度量一致性并在开环推演中累积漂移。作者认为这两个问题本质上是同一估计问题的两个视角，需要联合优化。

Method: 构建耦合因子图（J-LAW），联合优化度量物体位姿、潜在世界状态和潜在地标嵌入。引入位姿条件潜在编码器和可学习的位姿-潜在耦合因子作为桥梁，将观测、动作条件预测、度量里程计、位姿-潜在耦合、潜在闭环和潜在地标观测均作为概率因子纳入单一最大后验（MAP）目标中。

Result: 在PushT和WildGS数据集上的实验表明：耦合图校正显著降低了潜在预测RMSE和端点漂移（相比开环推演），潜在闭环改善了全局轨迹一致性。

Conclusion: J-LAW生成的地图同时具备度量属性（位姿）和可行动属性（用于规划的潜在地标），实现了定位与可行动世界模型的统一。

Abstract: Classical SLAM estimates metric poses and a geometric map but produces no actionable predictive model for planning. Action-conditioned world models learn compact latent dynamics for planning but ignore global metric consistency and accumulate drift under open-loop rollout. We argue these are two views of the same estimation problem and propose J-LAW (Joint Localization and Actionable World Modeling) in this letter: a coupled factor graph that jointly optimizes metric object poses, latent world states, and latent landmark embeddings. The bridge is a pose-conditioned latent encoder and a learned pose--latent coupling factor, so that better localization improves the world model and vice versa. We cast observation, action-conditioned prediction, metric odometry, pose--latent coupling, latent loop closure, and latent landmark observation as probabilistic factors in a single MAP objective. Real-data experiments on PushT and WildGS show that coupled graph correction substantially reduces latent prediction RMSE and endpoint drift relative to open-loop rollout, while latent loop closure improves global trajectory consistency. J-LAW yields a map that is simultaneously metric (poses) and actionable (latent landmarks for planning).

</details>


### [247] [CubifyGS: Object-Centric 3D Gaussian Splatting for Lifelong Dynamic Scene Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28720)
*Bohan Ren,Dianyi Yang,Shiyang Liu,Yu Gao,Jiadong Tang,Zhilin Lai,Yi Yang,Mengyin Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CubifyGS提出了一种面向物体级别的终身场景建图框架，将动态维护从被动重优化转变为主动资产管理，通过将可移动实例建模为可复用的高斯资产，并采用事件触发的自适应优化策略，有效解决了物体重排场景下的伪影和恢复慢问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性物体重排场景下的终身建图存在伪影持续和恢复缓慢的问题，现有3DGS的基元级更新方式难以高效应对动态变化。

Method: 将可移动物体建模为可复用的高斯资产；通过资产检索、刚性变换和显式剪枝实现地图更新，而非从头重建；提出事件触发的自适应优化策略，聚焦受影响区域进行计算。

Result: 在构建的高保真动态基准上，CubifyGS在伪影抑制和维护效率方面优于现有代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: 物体级别的主动资产管理范式比传统基元级被动重优化更适用于物体重排场景下的终身建图。

Abstract: Lifelong scene mapping under rigid object rearrangement remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity modeling, primitive-level updates often cause persistent ghosting and slow recovery. We propose CubifyGS, an object-level mapping framework that shifts dynamic maintenance from passive re-optimization to active asset management. CubifyGS models movable instances as reusable Gaussian assets, detects object appearance and disappearance, and updates maps through asset retrieval, rigid transformation, and explicit pruning rather than reconstruction from scratch. To address geometric voids and local photometric mismatch after such edits, we further propose an event-triggered adaptive optimization strategy that focuses computation on affected regions. We validate our approach on a newly constructed high-fidelity dynamic benchmark, demonstrating that CubifyGS improves artifact suppression and maintenance efficiency over representative reproducible baselines in the evaluated object-rearrangement setting.

</details>


### [248] [He3-Seeker: Robotic Information Planning for Lunar Helium-3 Distribution Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28746)
*Dong Li,Yujie Zheng,Chengdeng Cao,Siyu Teng,Yuchen Li,Yang Gao,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出He3-Seeker，一种基于主动机器人的月球氦-3勘探方法，通过多点钻探采样和机器人信息规划（RIP）实现高精度、高分辨率的氦-3分布制图，解决了现有遥感方法精度低、可靠性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球氦-3是深空探测和太空采矿的关键战略资源，但现有遥感间接测量方法存在精度低、可靠性差、空间分辨率不足的问题，亟需更有效的主动勘探方法。

Method: 首先形式化定义了主动氦-3勘探问题；进而提出He3-Seeker框架，基于多点钻探采样与原位分析，利用机器人信息规划（RIP）指导自主导航和主动感知；并基于低分辨率轨道遥感数据生成可靠的氦-3分布参考数据用于算法评估。

Result: 仿真实验验证了He3-Seeker能够实现快速、高保真的氦-3分布制图，为资源勘探任务提供了可靠解决方案。

Conclusion: He3-Seeker是一种有效的主动机器人勘探方法，能够克服传统遥感方法的局限性，为月球氦-3资源勘探提供高精度、高可靠性的制图能力。

Abstract: Lunar helium-3 is a highly valuable strategic resource, pivotal to the advancement of both deep-space exploration and space mining. Existing lunar helium-3 exploration methodologies rely primarily on indirect measurements via remote sensing, which are often characterized by limited precision, low reliability, and insufficient spatial resolution. In this paper, we introduce He3-Seeker, an active robotic exploration method for helium-3 distribution mapping. First, we provide a formal definition of the active helium-3 exploration problem. Subsequently, we developed the He3-Seeker framework, which is conceptually based on multi-point drilling, sampling, and in situ analysis. In particular, we use robotic information planning (RIP) to guide autonomous robot navigation and active sensing. Additionally, to thoroughly evaluate the proposed algorithm, we introduce a reliable method for generating reference data of lunar helium-3 distribution based on low-resolution orbital remote sensing measurements. Simulation experiments verify that He3-Seeker achieves both rapid and high-fidelity mapping of helium-3 distribution, providing a reliable solution for resource exploration tasks. Our code and simulation environment will be publicly accessible at https://github.com/OpenSpace-Lab/He3-Seeker.

</details>


### [249] [Legible Shared Autonomy: Implicit Communication of Robot Belief through Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29846)
*Jinwei Liu,Pengfei Li,Shaofeng Chen,Tao Wang,Yun-Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将可读运动引入共享自主系统，通过置信度感知的自适应权责分配，让机器人动作既能朝目标推进又能清晰揭示推断目标，从而提升用户对机器人信念的理解并减少控制负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共享自主系统中，机器人对用户目标的内部信念不可观察，当多个目标方向相似时，辅助运动存在歧义，用户无法感知机器人是否理解正确，导致两个问题：1）机器人正确推断目标时用户继续控制而浪费精力；2）机器人误解目标时用户无法及时检测错误，需要大量修正输入。

Method: 将可读运动（legible motion）引入共享自主框架，使机器人动作在推进目标的同时明确揭示其推断的目标；同时通过置信度感知的自适应权责分配，在置信度高时提供果断的可读辅助动作，在不确定时增加用户控制权。

Result: 通过包含仿真和六自由度机械臂物理实验的用户研究发现，与传统共享自主相比，可读共享自主显著提升了用户对机器人信念的理解，并减少了用户控制负担。

Conclusion: 可读运动使共享自主从单向辅助转变为透明的双向协作，用户能够感知系统内部状态并及时调整控制，提高了人机协作效率。

Abstract: Shared autonomy systems combine user input with autonomous assistance to help users with motor impairments control robot arms to perform everyday manipulation tasks, by inferring user goals and providing appropriate guidance. However, the robot's internal beliefs about user goals cannot be observed by users. Traditional shared autonomy systems provide assistance along efficient shortest paths toward inferred goals, but when multiple objects lie in similar directions, such assistive motion remains ambiguous and fails to reveal the specific goal identified by the robot. This creates two critical problems. First, when the robot correctly infers the goal, users continue controlling because they cannot perceive understanding from ambiguous assistive motion, wasting effort when autonomous completion would suffice. Second, when the robot misunderstands intent, users cannot quickly detect errors until assistive motion diverges significantly, requiring substantial corrective input. We address this by introducing legible motion into shared autonomy, where robot actions must both advance toward the goal and clearly reveal which goal has been inferred, enabling users to understand the robot's beliefs and adjust control accordingly. The robot modulates communication strength through confidence-aware adaptive authority allocation by providing assertive legible assistive actions when confident while increasing user authority when uncertain, transforming shared autonomy into transparent bidirectional collaboration. User studies including simulation and physical experiments with a six-degree-of-freedom robot arm demonstrate that legible shared autonomy significantly improves users' understanding of robot beliefs and reduces user control effort compared to standard shared autonomy.

</details>


### [250] [Vision-Language Models for Deployable Social Robot Navigation: Bridging Semantic Reasoning and Low-Level Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28760)
*Runji Cai,Toshihiko Yamasaki,Ling Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）的社会性机器人导航方法，提出了由高层VLM推理、低层规划控制、以及连接推理与行动的中间机制构成的三组件统一框架，并给出了涵盖语义推理、评估器、空间定位、中间表示和控制模块的结构化路线图，最后讨论了关键挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统社会机器人导航方法缺乏语义推理能力，难以理解人类意图、社会规范和情境线索；VLM虽提供高层理解和常识推理能力，但如何将其可靠地集成到实时、安全关键的导航系统中仍是一大挑战。

Method: 采用统一视角，将现有VLM-based SRN方法组织为三个相互关联的组件：高层VLM推理、低层规划与控制、以及连接二者的中间机制。在此基础上提出了结构化路线图，涵盖语义推理、评估器、空间定位、中间表示和控制模块。

Result: 提出了VLM与导航系统耦合的结构化路线图，强调了VLM的优势和混合架构的必要性，并综述了代表性数据集和评估平台。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在为构建可靠、社会合规且可部署的VLM赋能导航系统提供基础，并指出了开放挑战。

Abstract: Social robot navigation (SRN) requires more than geometric path planning; it demands understanding human intentions, social norms, and contextual cues to generate socially compliant behaviors. Although classical navigation methods provide reliable metric planning and collision avoidance, they often lack the semantic reasoning capabilities necessary for operation in complex human-centered environments. Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have opened new opportunities for SRN by enabling high-level VLM understanding, commonsense reasoning, and natural language interaction. However, a fundamental challenge remains: how to integrate VLMs into real-time, safety-critical navigation systems and reliably translate their high-level reasoning into grounded navigation actions. In this survey, we present a unified perspective of VLM-based SRN and organize existing approaches into three interconnected components: high-level VLM reasoning, low-level planning and control, and intermediate mechanisms that bridge reasoning and action. Based on this perspective, we propose a structured roadmap for coupling VLMs with navigation systems, covering semantic reasoning, evaluators, spatial grounding, intermediate representations, and control modules. The roadmap highlights both the strengths of VLMs and the necessity of hybrid architectures for practical deployment. We further review representative datasets and evaluation platforms developed for SRN. Finally, we discuss key open challenges. This survey aims to provide a foundation for building reliable, socially compliant, and deployable VLM-enabled navigation systems.

</details>


### [251] [Physics Models for Sim-to-Real Transfer in Professional-Level Robot Table Tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28805)
*Christian Conti,Bilan Yang,Alexander Sigrist,Lorenzo Miele,Yamen Saraiji,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了高保真物理模型（空气动力学、球台碰撞、球拍碰撞），在涵盖职业级速度和旋转的范围内精确模拟乒乓球轨迹，并首次训练出能与职业选手对战的真实机器人乒乓球AI智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略在真实世界中训练成本高且危险，依赖高保真仿真；但现有仿真模型仅在有限速度和旋转范围内有效，无法捕捉职业比赛中的丰富球路，对手会利用模型不准确的区域作为可攻击弱点。

Method: 1) 空气动力学：将阻力和马格努斯力系数建模为雷诺数和旋转比的函数；2) 球台接触：考虑球体屈曲对恢复系数的影响，引入残差修正瞬时点接触模型；3) 球拍接触：引入残差神经网络组件，补充法向/切向恢复系数和扭转自旋阻尼系数。

Result: 该模型首次实现了能够在真实世界中与职业乒乓球选手对战的机器人AI智能体。

Conclusion: 高保真物理仿真对于将强化学习策略从仿真迁移到真实机器人至关重要，所提出的模型覆盖了职业比赛所需的大范围速度和旋转，填补了先前方法的空白。

Abstract: At competitive speeds and spins, a table tennis ball follows complex, counterintuitive trajectories that a robot must track and precisely counter within fractions of a second.
